Hmahrûkna

'Bhavishya Malika' hi kum 600 vel kaltaah Lalpa Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu-a Panchasakhas (ṭhian panga/ṭhuihruai) te ziah, lehkhabu za tam tak khawmkhawm a ni. Hêng lehkhabute hi kum zabi tam tak chhûng thuhruk an ni a, chumi hun chhûng chuan mi tam zâwk chuan an hre lo. Mahse, kum 1999-a Odisha-a thlipui na tak (super cyclone) tleh khan mi tam takin 'Bhavishya Malika' an ngaihven phah a, chûng lehkhabuahte chuan thlipui chungchâng hrilh lâwkna chu lo awm tawh angin an sawi vâng a ni.

Chuti chungin, Odia ṭawnga ziah a nih avângin Bhavishya Malika chu chumi hun chhûng chuan Odisha-ah chauh a la awm a. Odisha pâwn lam mi chuan hêng lehkhabute hi an hre meuh lo. Mahse, tûn hnuah chuan hun a danglam ta a. Kum 2020-ah chuan, COVID-19 hri lêng chuan mi tam takin Bhavishya Malika an ngaihven leh phah a. Hri lêng a reh hnuah chauh hian Malika lehkhabute chu Odisha pâwn lamah thehdarh leh hrilh darh ṭan a ni a, he hmalakna hi tûn thlengin a la chhunzawm zêl a ni.

Chuvângin, kum tlêmte chhûng hian mi sâng tam takin Bhavishya Malika chungchâng an zir ṭan a. Mahse, a thleng ṭhin angin, he hriatpuina ṭha tak nêna kal kawpin, Malika châng ṭhenkhat chungchângah hrilhfiah sualna leh thu dik lo thehdarh a ni bawk ṭan a. Entîr nân, mi ṭhenkhat chuan Kaliyuga kum chu kum 10,000 a ni tih thu dik lo an thehdarh ṭan a; Puri lal hnuhnung ber hming chu Mukunda Dev a ni ang tih te; Malika thilthleng pawimawh tak takte leh Lalpa Kalki-a thil tihchhiatna ropui tak takte (Sanhaar-Leela) chu Meena-Shani Yoga hun (kum 2054-57 inkâr) lo awm tûrah a thleng ang tih te; Khandagiri bial chu Bhubaneswar-ah ni lovin hmun dangah a awm tih te; leh Satyayuga a lo thlen hma chuan kum tam tak a la awm tih te an thehdarh a ni.

Chûng thu dik lo thehdarhte chuan Pathian ngaihsak mi te rilruah rinhlelhna tam tak a siam ṭan a. Hêng rinhlelhnate hi chinfelna dik tak a awm loh avângin, Malika an rinna chu a tlahniam ṭan a. He thuziak tum ber chu chhiartu tharte hnêna Bhavishya Malika chungchânga hriatna dik leh rintlak pêk mai bâkah, Pathian ngaihsak mi (devotees) te rilrua rinhlelhna lo awmte chinfel a ni. Hêng thil pawimawh tak takte chungchânga hriatna dik an neih chuan, rinna nghet tak nei mite chuan Malika an rinna chu an tichak zual thei dâwn a ni tih beisei a ni.

A chhûng thu tlangpui

1 Bhavishya Malika – Hriattirna Tlêmte

1.1 Hriattirna

Kum zabi 15-na leh 16-na inkarah khan, Odisha-a piang sakhaw mi ropui panga (Shishu Ananta Das, Yashobanta Das, Achyutananda Das, Balarama Das, leh Jagannatha Das) chuan hrilh lawkna tam tak awmna lehkhabu za tam tak an ziak a. Chung lehkhabute chu 'Bhavishya Malika' tia hriat an lo ni ta a. Kum 1486-a piang, Bhagawan Sri Krishna leh Jaganmata Radharani-te inzawmkhawmna anga ngaih Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu chuan heng sakhaw mi panga te nen hian inzawmna thuk tak a nei a. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu chuan heng panga te hi 'Panchasakha' (Thian Panga) tiin a ko a. Heng Panchasakha-te hian Mahaprabhu ngei thupek angin Malika lehkhabute hi an phuah a. Panchasakha-te bakah, sakhaw mi ropui dangte pawhin Malika ziak dan hi an chhawm nung zel a. Entirnan, kum zabi 17-naa piang bhakta Salabega, kum zabi 18-naa piang Saint Arkhit Das leh Saint Hadidas, kum zabi 19-naa piang Saint Bhima Bhoi, kum zabi 20-naa piang Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, leh adangte pawh an tel a.

Heng Malika lehkhabuteah hian Kaliyuga hun chhung, Lalpa Kalki pian dan, mi sualte tihboralna, bhakta-te venhimna, Dharma dinthar lehna, leh Satyayuga lo thlen tur chungchangah hrilh lawkna kimchang tak ziak a ni.

1.2 Panchasakha-te chu tunge an nih?

Panchasakha-te zinga mi, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu pakhatah heti hian a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ବଳବନ୍ତ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମେ ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Balabanta Yaśhobanta Ananta Achyuta,
Jagannātha Dāsa Pañchasakhā nāme khyāta.

Awmzia:
Balarama Das, Yashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, leh Jagannatha Das-te chu Panchasakha tia hriat lar an lo ni ta a.

Panchasakha-te chanchin hi hun rei tak a ni tawh tih hriat a tha. Yuga tin tawpah, Panchasakha-te chu Lalpa hmun thenkhat atanga piangin, Lalpa Dharma dinthar lehna hnaah an thawh hlu tak an pe thin.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Udaya Kahani’ ah heti hian a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ପଞ୍ଚକୁଳରେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଆଜ୍ଞାରେ।
ଯୁଗ ଯୁଗରେ ଯେ ଜନମ ହେଉ,
କୃଷ୍ଣଲୀଳାମାନ ମୁଖରେ ଗାଉ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ambhe Pañchasakhā pañchakulare,
janama hoilu Kṛṣhṇa ājñāre.
yuga yugare je janama heu,
Kṛṣhṇalīḷāmāna mukhare gau.

Awmzia:
He chângah hian, Saint Achyutananda Das chuan heti hian a sawi: "Keini, thian panga te hian, Lalpa Krishna thupek angin hnam hrang hrang pangaah kan piang thin. Kum tinah kan piang a, Krishna-a thil tih thianghlimte (Leela) chu kan ka atangin kan sa thin."

1.3 Yuga Pali Li-a Panchasakha-te Inlar Dan

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Udaya Kahani’ ah Yuga pali-a sakhaw mi ropui panga te an pian dan a sawifiah a:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଦିଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ଅନ୍ତରେ, ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରବେଶ ହୋଇଲା ମହୀରେ।
ଅସୁର ବିନାଶ କରିବା ପାଇଁ, ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିଲେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣେ ଯାଇଁ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଋଷିତନୁ ବହିଲେ, କୃପାଜଳ ଋଷି ଗାଗର୍ବ ହେଲେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଯେ ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ନାରଦ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ କହିଲି ଭେଦ।
ରାମ ଜନମ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଗୁଣ କହିଲେ ନ ସରେ।
ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନଳ ନୀଳ ସୁଷେଣ, ଜାମ୍ବବ ହନୁ ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନ।
ତ୍ରେତୟା ଅନ୍ତେ ଦ୍ୱାପର ପ୍ରବେଶ, ଗୋପାଳ ମୂରତି ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବେଶ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନେ ହେଲେ ଜନମ, ସୁବଳ ସୁବାହୁ ସୁଦାମ ଦାମ।
ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଘେନି ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ହେଲେ, ଦ୍ୱାପର ଲୀଳାମାନ ସାଙ୍ଗ କଲେ।
ଅବନୀ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ନାମ ସ୍ଥାପିଲେ, ହରିନାମରେ ଜଗତ ମୋହିଲେ।
ସ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଶୂଦ୍ର ଆଦି ପାଷାଣ୍ଡ ଯେତେ, ହରିନାମ ଶୁଣି ହେଲେ ମୁକତେ।
ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ ହେଲୁ, ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନାମ ବହିଲୁ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୋଇଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ମେଳ ବଳରାମରେ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ନାମରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ, କହିଦେଲୁ ବାବୁ ତୋର ଆଗେଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Adi Ananta Yugaṭi Antare, Satya Prabesha Hoilā Mahire.
Asura Bināsha Karibā Pāiñ, Prabhu Janamile Brāhmaṇe Jāiñ.
Pañchasakhā Ṛuṣi Tanu Bahile, Kṛupājala Ṛuṣi Gāgarba Hele.
Mārkaṇḍa Je Swayambhū Nārada, Pañchasakhā Janama Kahili Bheda.
Rāma Janama Tretayā Yugare, Tretayā Guṇa Kahile Na Sare.
Tretayāre Naḷa Nīḷa Suṣeṇa, Jāmbaba Hanu Pañchasakhāmāna.
Tretayā Ante Dwāpara Prabesha, Gopāḷa Mūrati Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Besha.
Pañchasakhāmāne Hele Janama, Subaḷa Subāhu Sudāma Dāma.
Shrībatsa Gheni Pañchasakhā Hele, Dwāpara Līḷāmāna Sāṅga Kale.
Abanī Madhyare Nāma Sthāpile, Harināmare Jagata Mohile.
Strī Shūdra Ādi Pāṣāṇḍa Jete, Harināma Shuṇi Hele Mukate.
Ambhe Pañchasakhā Janama Helu, Ananta Achyuta Nāma Bahilu.
Jagannātha Jashobanta Hoile, Pañchasakhā Meḷa Baḷarāmare.
Emanta Nāmare Ambhe Janama, Kahidelu bābu tora āgeṇa.

Awmzia:

Chu chu, hmasang hunah, Ananta Yuga tawpah Satyayuga a intan khan, Lalpa Chungnungber chu ramhuai tihboral turin Brahmin anga a piang a. Chumi hunah chuan, sakhaw mi panga te chu Lalpa taksa atangin Rishi angin an inlar a: Kripajala, Garga, Markandeya, Swayambhu, leh Narada.

Satyayuga hnuah, Tretayuga-ah, Lalpa chuan a Rama piannaah thil tih thianghlim tam tak a tih khan, heng thian panga te bawk hi Nala, Nila, Sushena, Jambavan, leh Hanuman angin khawvelah an lo chhuk a.

Tretayuga tawpah, Dwaparyuga a lo thleng a, Lalpa chu ran vulhtu naupang, Shri Krishna angin a piang a. Chumi hunah chuan, Subala, Subahu, Sudama, Dama, leh Shribatsa te chu Panchasakha an lo ni a, Shri Krishna nen thil tih thianghlimah an tel a.

Chu bakah, Kaliyuga-ah, Lalpa chu a Chaitanya piannaah a inlar khan, heng thian panga te hian hming thianghlim (Hari-naam) thehdarh turin an piang a, mi zawng zawng tan chhandamna kawng awlsam leh harsa lo an siam a. Kaliyuga-ah chuan, Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, Jagannatha Das, Yashobanta Das, leh Balarama Das tia hriat lar an lo ni ta a.

1.4 Yuga Hrang Hrangah Panchasakha-te Thlahtu Chanchin Kimchang

Zirtirtu Ramadas chuan Yuga pali-a Panchasakha-te hming bik a zawh khan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Udaya Kahani’ ah heti hian kimchang takin a sawi a.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ଋଷି କୃପାଜଳ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ସଖାଟି ଅଟଇ ନଳ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁଦାମ ସଖାଟି ହୋଇ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲାଇ।
ଗାର୍ଗବ ଋଷି ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ହେଲେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଜାମ୍ବବ ନାମ ବହିଲେ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁବାହୁ ବ୍ରଜ ଅଟନ୍ତି, ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ନାମ କଳିରେ ପାନ୍ତି।
ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସେ ଜାଣେ, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ଅଟେ ସେହି ସୁଷେଣ।
ଗୋପରେ ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ଜାତ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ନାମ ଶିଶୁ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସଖା ନାରଦ ଋଷି, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନୀଳ ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶି।
ଦାମ ଗୋପାଳ ଦ୍ୱାପର ଯୁଗରେ, ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ନାମ କଳିରେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଋଷି ଅଟେ ହନୁମାନ, ସୁବଳ ସଖାଟି ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ପୁଣ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ନାମ କଳିରେ ହେଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶ କଲେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Satya Yugare Ṛuṣi Kṛupājala, Tretayā Sakhāṭi Aṭai Naḷa.
Dwāpare Sudāma Sakhāṭi Hoi, Kaḷi Yugare Achyuta Bolāi.
Gārgaba Ṛuṣi Satya Yugare Hele, Tretayā Jāmbaba Nāma Bahile.
Dwāpare Subāhu Braja Aṭanti, Jashobanta Nāma Kaḷire Pānti.
Swayambhū Satya Yugare Se Jāṇe, Tretayāre Aṭe Sehi Suṣeṇa.
Gopare Shrībatsa Dwāpare Jāta, Kaḷi Yugare Nāma Shishu Ananta.
Satya Yugare Sakhā Nārada Ṛuṣi, Tretayāre Nīḷa Nāma Prakāshi.
Dāma Gopāḷa Dwāpara Yugare, Baḷarāma Dāsa Nāma Kaḷire.
Mārkaṇḍa Ṛuṣi Aṭe Hanumāna, Subaḷa Sakhāṭi Dwāpare Puṇa.
Jagannātha Nāma Kaḷire Hele, Pañchasakhā Nāma Prakāsha Kale.

Hming Inangte Letlingna:
  • Achyutananda Das: Satyayuga-ah Rishi Kripajala a ni a, Tretayuga-ah Nala a ni a, Dwaparayuga-ah Sudama a ni.
  • Yashobanta Das: Satyayuga-ah Rishi Garga a ni a, Tretayuga-ah Jambavan a ni a, Dwaparayuga-ah Subahu a ni.
  • Shishu Ananta Das: Satyayuga-ah Rishi Swayambhu a ni a, Sushena in Tretayuga, leh Shribatsa in Dwaparayuga.
  • Balarama Das: Satyayuga-ah Rishi Narada a ni a, Tretayuga-ah Nila a ni a, Dwaparayuga-ah Dama a ni.
  • Jagannatha Das: Satyayuga-ah Rishi Markandeya a ni a, Tretayuga-ah Hanuman a ni a, Dwaparayuga-ah Subala a ni.

1.5 Panchasakha-te Thiltihtheihna Bikte leh An Thiamna

Panchasakha-te chu engkim hria, mahni inhrechiang, leh Pathian hrechiang sakhaw mi an ni. Anni chu Yogi ropui tak takte, zawngtute, leh bhakta-te an ni a, pakhat theuh chu thlarau lam thiamna hrang hrangah an thiam hlawm a. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika-ah heti hian a sawifiah a:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଗମ୍ୟ ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ,
ଗାର କଟା ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଜାଣେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ଆଗତ ଅନାଗତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭଣେ,
ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ବଖାଣେ।
ଭକ୍ତିର ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମହନ୍ତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Āgamya Bhāba Jāṇe Jashobanta,
Gāra Kaṭā Jantra Jāṇe Ananta.
Āgata Anāgata Achyuta Bhaṇe,
Baḷarāma Dāsa Tattwa Bakhāṇe.
Bhaktira Bhāba Jāṇe Jagannātha,
Pañchasakhā E Oḍiśā Mahanta.

Awmzia:
Odisha-a piang Panchasakhas-te zingah:
  • Yashobanta Das chu hmasawn leh hma hun (Agam-Nigam) thuruk thûk tak hriatu a ni.
  • Shishu Ananta Das chu thil mak tak lem leh khawl hmanrua (Yantra-Vigyan) thiamna lama mi thiam tak a ni.
  • Achyutananda Das chu hmasawn, tun leh hma hun hmu thei (Trikal-Darshi) a ni.
  • Balarama Das chu thil awmzia tak tak leh finna (Tattwa-Vigyan) lama mi thiam thûk tak a ni.
  • Jagannatha Das chu inhlan pumhlûmna (Bhakti-Bhaav) rilruah chuan a tluk rual an awm lo.

1.6 Panchasakhas-te Hmunpui (Ashrams)

Panchasakhas-te chuan Odisha ram pumah hmun panga hrang hrangah an Peethas leh hmun thianghlimte an din a. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah hetiang hian a sawi:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅଢ଼ଙ୍ଗେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ, ବାଲିପାଟଣାରେ ରହିଲେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସାତ ଲହଡ଼ିରେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ରହି, କ୍ଷେତ୍ରବରେ ବଳବନ୍ତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Nemāḷe Achyuta Aḍhaṅge Jashobanta, Bālipāṭaṇāre rahiḷe Ananta.
Sāta Lahaḍire Jagannātha rahi, Khetrabare Baḷabanta.

Awmzia:
  • Achyutananda Das chuan Nemalo (Cuttack district)-ah a hmunpui a din.
  • Yashobanta Das chuan Adhanga (Jagatsinghpur district)-ah a Peetha a din.
  • Shishu Ananta Das (Ananta) chuan Balipatna (Khurda district)-ah a hmun thianghlim a din.
  • Jagannatha Das chuan Sata Lahadi-ah a Ashram a din.
  • Balarama Das (Balabanta) chuan Shrikhetra (Puri) chu a chenna hmunah a siam.

1.7 Panchasakhas – Lalpa Lo Channa Thianghlimte

Panchasakhas-te chuan an Malika lehkhabute-ah vawi tam tak an sawi tawh a, anmahni chu Lalpa Chungnungber ngei lo channa tlem (Anshavatar) an nih thu an sawi. Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das chuan a lehkhabu 'Chumbak Malika'-ah hetiang hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲିଣ କଳିଯୁଗେ ନାମ ସୁଦାମ ସଖା ଅଟନ୍ତି,
ଶ୍ରୀଅଙ୍ଗରୁ ଜାତ କଟିରୁ ଭକତ ଶୁଣ ହୋ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଚେତି।
ମୁଖୁଁ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୃଦୁଁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜାତ ବାହୁ ମୂଁଳୁ ବଳରାମ,
ରାଧାଙ୍କ ହାସ୍ୟରୁ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରେ ଘେନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Achyuta boliṇa Kaḷiyuge nāma Sudāma sakhā aṭanti,
Shrī-aṅgaru jāta kaṭiru bhakata shuṇa ho Bāraṅga cheti.
Mukhuṃ Jashobanta hṛuduṃ ambhe jāta bāhu mūṃḷu Baḷarāma,
Rādhāṅka hāsyaru Jagannātha Dāsa Bāraṅga heture ghena.

Awmzia:
Kaliyuga-ah chuan, Achyutananda Das chu Dwaparayuga aṭanga ṭhian Sudama kha a ni a, Lalpa kawng (Kati) aṭanga piang a ni. Yashobanta Das chu Mahaprabhu kâ thianghlim (Shri-mukh) aṭanga piang a ni. Shishu Ananta Das chu Lalpa thinlung (Hriday) aṭanga lo lang a ni a, Balarama Das chu Lalpa bân (Bahu) aṭanga piang a ni. Chu bâkah, Jagannatha Das chu Nu Radharani nuih (Haasya) aṭanga lo chhuk a ni.

Shishu Ananta Das ji chauh ni lovin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das pawhin a Malika lehkhabu, 'Chakada Madana'-ah hei hi a ziak bawk. Mahapurush chuan a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ନିରାକାର ଦେହୁ ପାଞ୍ଚଟି ଜ୍ୟୋତି,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅନନ୍ତ ପଞ୍ଚକ ମୂରତି।
ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ବଳରାମ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ ଏ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ଧାମ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Nirākāra dehu pāṃchaṭi jyoti,
Achyuta Anaṃta paṃchaka mūrati.
Jashobaṃta Jagannātha Balarāma,
janama hoilu e martya dhāma.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a ni, Lalpa Hmel Nei Lo taksa êng-hmêl aṭangin, keini Panchasakhas panga kan lo chhuk a, Achyutananda, Shishu Ananta, Jashobanta, Jagannatha, leh Balarama hming puin he khawvelah hian kan piang a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan Malika lehkhabu, 'Barnatika'-ah a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଶରୀର ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତରୁ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନ୍ମ କହିଛି ଗୁରୁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shrī Kṛiṣhṇa sharīra paṃchabhūtaru,
paṃchasakhā janma kahichi guru.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a ni, Panchasakhas-te pianna chu Lalpa Shri Krishna taksa thil panga aṭanga lo awm a ni.

1.8 Pathian Thupêk Aṭanga Hrilh Lâwkna

Malika chângte chu Mahaprabhu ngei thu angin ngaih a ni. Panchasakhas-te zinga pakhat, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’-ah hetiang hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ମୁଁ କହୁଛି ବୋଲି ମୋର କଥା ନୁହେଁ ଆଦ୍ୟୁଁ ଖଞ୍ଜିଛନ୍ତି ଧାତା,
କର୍ମବାମ ହେଲେ କଳ୍ପନା ଗ୍ରାସିବ ପ୍ରକୃତି କାଟିବେ ଚିତା।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Muṃ kahuchi boli mora kathā nuheṃ ādyuṃ khañjichanti dhātā,
Karmabāma hele kalpanā grāsiba prakṛuti kāṭibe chitā.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan a sawi: "Eng pawh ka ziah hi ka thu a ni lo. Heng zawng zawng hi Lalpa'n a tir aṭanga a ruat lawk vek a ni."

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das chuan ‘Patta Madana’-ah a zirtîr a hrilh bawk:

Odia Sloka:

ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରଖ ଯାକୁସାର,
ଭାବବିନୋଦିଆ ହରିଙ୍କ ଏ ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ସିନା ଅଗୋଚର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Āhuri kathāe shuṇare Bāraṅga heturakha jākusāra,
Bhābabinodiā Hariṅka e bāṇī aṭe sinā agochara.

Awmzia:
"Aw zirtîr Baranga, ngaithla rawh! Heng hi ka ta a ni lo, Lalpa Shri Hari thu zâwk a ni."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu, 'Anakara Samhita'-ah a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଛତିଶ ସଂହିତା ଅଠସ୍ତରୀ ଗୀତା ବଂଶାନୁ ସପ୍ତବିଂଶରେ,
ଉପବଂଶାନୁ ଦ୍ୱାଦଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ବେନି ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଶତ ଖଣ୍ଡ ରେ।
ପଦ ପଦ୍ୟାବଳି ଲକ୍ଷେକ ଯେ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ସବୁ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ମହିମା,
ତୋ ଆଗେ କହିଲୁଁ ବ୍ରଜ କୁମର ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ସାରସ୍ୱତ ସୀମା।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chatisha saṃhitā aṭhastarī gītā baṃshānu saptabiṃshare,
upabaṃshānu dwādasha khaṃḍa beni bhabiṣhya shata khaṃḍa re.
Pada padyābali lakṣheka je graṃtha sabu Shrīkṛiṣhṇa mahimā,
to āge kahilu braja kumara brahma sāraswata sīmā.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a sawi a, a lehkhabute a phuah zinga telte chu Samhitas 36, Gitas 78, Banshanucharitras 27, Upabanshanucharitras 24, Malika lehkhabu 100, leh châng (padas) nuai tam tak a ni. Mahapurush chuan a sawi belh bawk a, heng lehkhabu zawng zawng hi Lalpa Shri Krishna ropuina emaw khawngaihna avanga phuah a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'-ah a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀରଙ୍ଗା ଚରଣ ବିନୁ ନ ଜାଣଇ ନୀଳାଚଳେ ମନ ମୋର,
ତାଙ୍କ କଥାମାନ ଉଲ୍ଲେଖ କରଇ ନ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଛାର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shrī raṃgācharaṇa binu na jāṇai nīlāchale mana mora,
tāṃka kathāmāna ullekha karai na kahe Achyuta chāra.

Awmzia:
He sloka-ah hian, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a rilru chu Lalpa Chungnungber kete bâk thil dangah a bet lo tih a sawi. A rilru chu Nilachala Dhama, Puri-ah chuan a pil reng a ni, chu chu, Lalpa chunga hmangaihna nena inhlannaah a ni. A sawi belh bawk a, Malika lehkhabute-a a ziah apiang chu Lalpa thu chiah chiah a ni vek a; amah duhthlanna aṭangin eng mah a ziak lo a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu, 'Parardha Jataka'-ah a ziak leh:

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଲା ଯେପରି,
ଆମ୍ଭେ ଲେଖିଲୁ ପୋଥି ସେହିପରି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Prabhunkara ājñā helā jepari,
āmbhe lekhilu pothi sehipari.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a ni, Lalpa Chungnungberin a hrilh leh a pêk ang chiah chiahin, kei chuan Malika lehkhabu chu chutiang chiah chuan ka phuah a ni.

Achyutanandaji chuan a 'Agata Bhabishya Prabandha Malika'-ah a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ମୁଖରୁ ଯାହା ମୋତେ ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଉଅଛି,
ସେହି ପଦମାନ ସବୁ ପୁରାଣେ ଲେଖୁଛି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shrī mukharu jāhā mote ājñā heuachi,
sehi padamāna sabu purāṇe lekhuchi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a ni, Lalpa Chungnungber thupêk avangin, Amah kaihhruaina ngei avangin, Malika hi ka ziak a ni. Lalpa aṭanga lo chhuak apiang chu, chu sloka chu Malika Purana-ah ka sawifiah a ni.

1.9 Malika chu Lehkhabu Thuruk a Ni a, a Hrilh Lâwkna chu Thup a Ni

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’-ah a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋଗୀମାନେ ଯୋଗ ଅନ୍ତ ନ ପାଇବେ ଆଉ କେବା ସାମରଥା।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Yogīmāne yoga anta na pāibe āu kebā sāmarathā.

Awmzia:
Yogis leh mi fing ropui tak takte pawhin Yoga hmangin heng thil thupte hi an thuruk an hmu chhuak thei lo a, mi dangte phei chuan an hmu chhuak thei lo lehzual ang.

Achyutananda Das chuan lehkhabu thuruk chungchangah a ziak belh bawk:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଲଟ ପାଲଟ କକ୍ଷା ଏହି ଅଙ୍କ, କର ହୃଦେ ତୁହି ଠାବ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଲୀଳା ଶେଷ ଯେ ହୋଇବ, ମସିହାରେ ନାହିଁ ଠାବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Olaṭa pālaṭa kakshā ehi aṅka, kara hṛude tuhi ṭhāba.
Kaḷiyuga līḷā shesha je hoiba, masihāre nāhiṃ ṭhāba.

Awmzia:
Hrilh lâwkna chu chhiarkawp hmanga ziah a ni a, leh heng number-te hi an letlingin thup a ni. Thil thleng turte chu a thleng ngei ang, mahse calendar kum pangngai angin ni lovin; thuruk chhinchhiahna chhiarkawp an zui zâwk ang.

Kalki pianna chungchangah, a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହେବୁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସନ୍ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବୁ।
ଗୁପତ ଭକତ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଯାହା,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ଏ ଅଟେ ଏହା।
ଗୁପତରେ କେତେ ଖେଳ କରିବୁ,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କକୁ ଚଳାଇ ଥିବୁ।
ମୋର ଭକତଟି ଜାଣିବେ ସେହି,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ବୋଲିଟି ଯେ ଏହି।
ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ,
କହୁଅଛନ୍ତି ସେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Gupatare ambhe janama hebu,
gupatare san sāla chālibu.
Gupata bhakata jāṇanti jāhā,
gupata aṅka e aṭe ehā.
Gupatare kete kheḷa karibu,
gupata aṅkaku chaḷāi thibu.
Mora bhakataṭi jāṇibe sehi,
gupata aṅka boliṭi ye ehi.
Gupata gupata ati gupata,
kahuachanti se dīna Achyuta.

Awmzia:
Lalpa pianna chu thuruk a ni ang. Hunte leh kumte chu thuruk takin a kal ang. Mi inhlân thurukte chauhvin hei hi an hria ang, number-te chu thup a nih avangin.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu, 'Chakada Madana'-ah hian heti hian a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଆନେ ନ କହିବୁ ତୁହି,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଗୀତରେ କହଇ ମୋ ପାଦକୁ ଥାଅ ଛୁଇଁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata āne na kahibu tuhi,
dīna Achyuta je gītare kahai mo pādaku thāa chuiṃ.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Achyutananda Das ji-a chuan Lalpa (Lord) hriat reng chung leh A ke zungpui thianghlim ngaihtuahna thuk takah a ziah avangin, heng thu hi thuruk mai ni lovin, thuruk ropui tak an ni.

Lehkhabu dangah chuan, Mahapurush-a chuan a zirtir Ramadasa hnenah heti hian a sawi:

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେଲେ ତୋହ ମୋହ ଦେଖା,
ତେରଜଣ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଥିବୁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା।
ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ ଅଟଇ ହେ ରାମ,
କହୁଅଛି ବ୍ରଜ ବାଳ ହେତୁରେ ତୁ ଘେନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kalki prakāsha hele toha moha dekhā,
terajaṇa madhye thibu āmbhe pañchasakhā.
Gupata ati gupata aṭai he Rāma,
kahuachi braja bāla heture tu ghena.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lalpa Kalki (Lord Kalki) chu a lo piang ang, A lo inlar ropui ang, keini Panchasakhas-te hian A ke bulah rawngbawl leh inpe pumhlum tura hun tha ropui tak kan nei ang. Mahse, heng Pathian thiltih ropui (divine plays) hi thuruk mai ni lovin, thuruk takin a thleng ang.

Mahapurush-a chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଶୁଣ ତୁ ରେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର,
ବ୍ରହ୍ମା ଶଙ୍କରକୁ ଭେଦ ତ ନଥିବ ନ ଜାଣିବେ ଇନ୍ଦ୍ର ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata shuṇa tu re Rāmachandra,
Brahmā Shaṅkaraku bheda ta nathiba na jāṇibe Indra Chandra.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, aw zirtir Ramachandra, Malika thu hi thuruk mai ni lovin, thuruk ropui tak an ni. Prajapati Brahma leh Lalpa Shankar pawhin an thuruk hi an hre thiam thei lovang, Lalpa Indra (Lord Indra) leh Lalpa Chandra phei chu sawi loh.

Sant Bhima Bhoi pawhin a Malika lehkhabu, 'Stuti Chintamani'-ah hian he thil chungchang hi a ziak bawk a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ସଂସାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ କେମନ୍ତେ ଜାଣିବେ ନର ଅଙ୍ଗେ ଦେହ ବହି,
ଗତ ଆଗତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଜଣାନାହିଁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Saṃsāra madhyare kemaṃte jāṇibe nara aṅge deha bahi,
gata āgata yugara byabasthā samastaṃku jaṇānāhiṃ.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, khawvela mipa leh hmeichhe tam takte hian hun kal zel dan, Kali Yuga tawpna, Malika leh a hrilh lawknate chungchang hriatna an nei thei lovang. Lalpa khawngaihna dawngte chauh, hun hmasa lama Lalpa ringtute (devotees) lo ni tawhte chauhvin, heng hrilh lawkna thuk takte hi an hre thei ang.

Tunah zawhna a lo chhuak, hun hmasa lama Lalpa ringtute zinga tunge Lalpa khawngaihna dawng ang a, Malika thu ring ang a, an hre thei ang a, Kali Yuga tawpna leh Kalki avatar chungchangah hriatna dik tak nei ang?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'-ah hian chhanna a pe a ni. Mahapurush-a chuan heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

କୋଟିକେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଏ ରସ ତିନି ସସ୍ରେ ଗଣାସେହି,
ମହିମା ପ୍ରକାଶ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ସେହି ଦାସ ଆନେ ମୁଁ କହନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Koṭike goṭie jāṇaṃti e rasa tini sasre gaṇāसेhi,
mahimā prakāsha nishche sehi dāsa āne muṃ kahaṃti nāhiṃ.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, mi nuai khat zelah mi pakhat chauhvin heng hrilh lawknate hi a hre thei ang a, khawvel pumpuiah chutiang mi chu 3,000 chauh an awm ang.

1.10 Malika Hrilh Lawknate chu a Dik Lo Lovang

Bhavishya Malika hi Lalpa thu ngei a ni a, chuvangin a dik loh theihna a awm lo. Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah chuan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ପୃଥିବୀ ଦୁଇଫାଳ ହେଲେ ହୋଇବ, ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମେ ଯିବ।
ପର୍ବତ ଶିଖରେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ମିଥ୍ୟା ନୁହଇଁ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତର ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ କ୍ଷମତା କାହାର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Pṛuthibī duiphāḷa hele hoiba, pūrba sūrya abā pashchime jiba.
Parbata shikhare phuṭiba kaiñ, Achyuta bachana mithyā nuhaiñ.
Achyutara bāṇī aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ khyamatā kāhāra.

Awmzia:
"Lei hi a thenawm thei a, chhak lama ni chhuak chu thlang lamah a chhuak thei a, tlang chhipah lotus par a vul thei a—mahse Achyutananda thu hi a dik lo thei ngai lovang. Lung chunga ziah ang an ni a, tuma'n an tihdik loh theihna an nei lo."

‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’-ah chuan, heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ସୀତା ଠାକୁରାଣୀ ଅସତୀ ହୋଇବେ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ତିଳେ ନ ଟଳିବ ପାଷାଣର ଗାର ଏହି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Sītā ṭhākurāṇī asatī hoibe parbate phuṭiba kaiñ,
Achyuta bachana tiḷe na taḷiba pāṣāṇara gāra ehi.

Awmzia:
Kaliyuga-ah chuan, miin Nu Sita (Mother Sita) pawh nula thianghlim lo an ti thei a, tlang chhipah lotus par a vul thei a, mahse Achyutananda Das-a thu hi a dik lo thei ngai lovang, lung chunga ziah ang a nih avangin.

1.11 ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’-ah chuan, Lalpa Inlar Lo (Nirakar) thu sawiin, heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଥା ଯେବେ ନ ହୋଇବ, ଆମ୍ଭ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ମିଥ୍ୟା ହେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

E kathā jebe na hoiba, ambha Shrīmukha mithyā heba.

Awmzia:
He chângah hian, Lalpa Inlar Lo (formless Lord) ngei chuan heti hian a sawi: "Malika thu hi a dik loh chuan, ka ka thianghlim ngei hi a dik lo ang." Heng hi Lalpa ka atanga chhuak thu an nih avangin, a dik lo thei ngai lovang.

Mahapurush-a chuan 'Chakada Madana'-ah heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ବାଣୀ ପଥରର ଗାର ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମକୁ ଯିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ ଏହି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shrī Achyuta bāṇī patharara gāra parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
pūrba sūrya abā pashchimaku jiba mo bachana satya ehi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, chhak lam aiin thlang lamah ni a chhuak thei a, tlang chhipah lotus par a vul thei a, mahse Achyutananda thu hi thlak danglam theih a ni lo. Achyutananda thu hi lung chunga ziah a ni; thudik tak a ni.

Achyutananda Das ji-a pianthar, Sant Hadi Das-a, kum zana 18-naa Odisha rama Jajpur district-a Chatia bul hnaia piang khan, Malika lehkhabu tam tak a phuah bawk.

A Malika lehkhabu 'Kali Chautisha'-ah chuan, heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳିବ ନାହିଁଟି ଏ କଥା ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହୋଇବ ଦିନେ,
ଟାକି ରହିଛନ୍ତି ଭରସା କରି ଭକତମାନେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ṭaliba nāhiṃṭi e kathā nishche hoiba dine,
ṭāki rahichaṃti bharasā kari bhakatamāne.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Malika lehkhabua ziah thilte hi thlak danglam theih a ni lo – ni khat chu a thleng ngei ang. Ringtu zawng zawngte chuan rinna nen heng thil thleng turte hi an nghak a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu, 'Agata Bhabishyat Malika'-ah heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ ବାରମ୍ବାର,
ବୁଝାଏ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଧୀର।
ନମଣ ଏହାକୁ ଯେ ଆନ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ କରେ ଧ୍ୟାନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶଯିବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା,
ସାରିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକାଡୋଳା।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Achyuta dāsa bārambāra,
bujhāe bhakataṃku dhīra.
Namaṇa ehāku je āna,
Achyuta dāsa kare dhyāna.
Kali kalmaṣha nāshajiba,
Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Anaṃta Achyutanka līlā,
sāribe prabhu chakāḍolā.

Awmzia:
Heti hian a ziak a ni, ringtute hnenah ka hrilhfiah nawn fo thin. Ringtute chuan heng thu hi a dik loah ngai suh se, Achyuta chuan ngaihtuahna thuk takah (meditation) heng lehkhabute hi a phuah a ni. Kali Yuga sualte leh harsatnate chu tihboral a ni ang a, Lalpa Kalki Pathian thiltih ropuite chu a chiang a ni. Mahapurush-a chuan a thu hi thlak danglam theih a ni lo tih a ziak belh bawk.

Mahapurush-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu pakhatah heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଅମୃତ ଭକ୍ଷି ଯାଇପାରେ ପ୍ରାଣ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ନୋହିବ ଆନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Amṛita bhakṣhi jāipāre prāṇa,
Achyuta bachana nohiba āna.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Amrit in avanga thi theih loh ni lovin, nunna chan ang hial pawha thil theih loh chu a thleng thei a, mahse Achyutananda Das-a thu hi a dik lo thei ngai lovang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a 'Udaya Kahani'-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଆନ ନୋହିବ,
କହିଲେ ଅପ୍ରାଧ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପାଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Achyuta bachana āna nohiba,
kahile aparādha nishche pāiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Achyutananda Das-a thu hi a dik lo thei ngai lovang. Tunge heng thu hi a dik lo ti chu, chu sualnaa tel ve ngei ang.

Mahapurush-a chuan 'Chakada Madana'-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ମୋ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗାଇଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହଇ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mo shrīmukha bāṇī anyathā nohiba parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
Nemāla baṭaku laya lagāiṇa dīna Achyutī kahai.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, tlangah lotus par a vul pawhin, ka thu hi a danglam ngai lovang! Heng thu hi Nemal banyan thing (Nemal Vata) hnuaiah ngaihtuahna thuk takah ziah a ni.

Malika lehkhabu 'Chakada Madana' ngeiah chuan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀଫାଟି ଜଳୁପଛେ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଅନଳ,
ଯାହା କହିଛନ୍ତି ତାହା ସର୍ବଦା ଅଚଳ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mahīphāṭi jalupache pralaya anala,
jāhā kahichaṃti tāhā sarbadā achala.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, lei a keh hnuah kangmei chhuak chuan khawvel pumpui hi tihboralna (Pralaya) a thlen thei a, mahse ka thu hi a dik lo thei ngai lovang. Ka thu hi hnial rual loh leh thlak danglam theih loh a ni.

2 Malika Tum chu – Pathian Ngaihsak Mi (Devotees) Te Chhandamna

Mi tam tak chuan Malika hrilh lawknate an chhiar hian an hlau thin a, "tihhlauhna" maiin an ngai thin. Mahse, he ngaihtuahna hi a dik lo hlawm vek a ni. A thiltum ber chu ringtute thlarau lam venhimna leh kaihhruaina, hun inthlak danglamna kal tlanga tih a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ହେତୁ ରସାଇବା ପାଇଁକି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶାହାସ୍ତ୍ର ପୁରାଣ କଲା,
କଳିକାଳଠାରୁ ବଳି କାଳ ଯହିଁ ହକ କଥାଟା ଲେଖିଲା।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Hetu rasāibā pāiṃki Achyuta shāhāstra purāṇa kalā,
kalikālaṭhāru bali kāla jahiṃ haka kathāṭā lekhilā.

Awmzia:
Kaliyuga tawpnaah, ringtute hriatna muhilte kaihthawh nan, heng Malika lehkhabute hi a phuah a ni. A thiltum chu Kaliyuga thim takah, hriat lohna thimna a len hunah, Lalpa ringtute (Lord's devotees) chuan Malika hmangin Mahaprabhu Kalki an thleng thei ang a, chu chuan ringtu leh Pathian inzawmna a thlen ang. Chuvang chuan, Kaliyuga atanga Sangam Yuga (Hun Inthlak Danglamna) leh Ananta Yuga (Adi Satyayuga) lo thleng tur thlengin Malika lehkhabua hrilh lawkna pawimawh tak takte hi a ziak a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଅବନୀକୁ ଚେତେଇବା ପାଇଁ ଚେତାବନୀ,
ଅବନୀ ହିତେ ଲେଖା ଏ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Abanīku cheteibā pāiñ chetābanī,
Abanī hite lekhā e bhabishya bāṇī.

Awmzia:
Malika lehkhabua ka ziah hrilh lawknate thiltum chu khawvel hrilh lawkna pek a ni. Heng hrilh lawknate hi khawvel tan a ni tih a hrilhfiah a ni.

Mahapurush-a chuan ‘Chakada Madana’-ah heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ପୂର୍ବର ବାସନା ମୂଳେ ଜ୍ଞାନ ଉଦୟ ହେଲା,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହିତରେ ମାଳିକା ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଲେଖି ରଖିଲା।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Pūrbara bāsanā mūḷe jñāna udaya helā,
Bhaktanka hitare Māḷikā grantha lekhi rakhilā.

Awmzia:
Hmasang 'vasana' (ngaihtuahna) avangin, chu chu pian hmasa lama Lalpa (Lord) rinna avangin, he piantharah hian hriatna ka nei a, chu hnuah ringtute hlawkna leh thatna turin Malika lehkhabute hi ka phuah a ni.

Mahapurush-a chuan ‘Chakada Madana’-ah heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ସତର୍କ ଘଣ୍ଟି ଯେ ଚେତାବନୀ ଏହି ଜାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa satarka ghaṇṭi je chetābanī ehi jāṇa.

Awmzia:
Malika thute hi hriattirna atana hman a ni; vaukhanna anga ngaih tur a ni.

Mahapurush chuan a ziak belh leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଏହି ଚେତାବନୀ ପୁଣ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ଭକ୍ତ ହିତେ ପୁଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Sāraswata grantha ehi chetābanī puṇa,
bāraṇa beḷe prakāsha bhakta hite puṇa.

Awmzia:
Malika changte hi mihringte tana vaukhanna a ni. Kaliyuga tawp hunah Pathian mi rinawmte tan hlawkna a thlen nan ziah a ni. Chuvangin, Malika hian hlauhna a thehdarh lo va, chhandamna kawng zawk a hawng tih kan hriat chian a pawimawh. Bhavishya Malika thiltum ber chu khawvel hlawkna a ni.

2.1 Malika hi mi zawng zawng tan a ni lo

Malika hi mi zawng zawng tan a ni lo tih kan hriat chian a pawimawh. Panchasakha Mahapurushate chuan Malika thuteah an zirtirte chu mi â hmaah heng thute hi thehdarh lo turin an khap a ni. Mahapurush chuan heng thute hi mi zawng zawng tan a ni lo; mi zawng zawngin an hrethiam vek lovang. Lalpa mi rinawmte chauhvin an ring thei ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika thuziak ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan heti hian a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ କଳିପୁଣି ଆଗତ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ,
ଭକତ ହିତରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲି ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଭଣି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Lakshe pañchāashī grantha kaḷi puṇi āgata bhabishyabāṇī,
Bhakata hitare prakāsha karili dīna Achyuta je bhaṇi.

Awmzia:
Thuziak 1,85,000 ka phuah a, chu bâkah, hrilh lâwkna tam tak ka ziak bawk. Heng zawng zawng hi Pathian mi rinawmte hlawkna turin ka tlangzarh a ni.

Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika thuziak ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a zirtir Ramadas hnenah heti hian a sawi a:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଏ ପରମତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ମୂର୍ଖକୁ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ତତେ ସପ୍ତବାର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି କହଇ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ତିନି ଅଠର ମାଳିକା କଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ରଖିଛି ଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଜନଙ୍କ ପୀୟୂଷ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra e paramatattwa mūrkhaku na kara prakāsha,
tate saptabāra niyama deuchi kahai dīna Achyuta.
Lakshe pañchāashī tini aṭhara mālikā kali prakāsha,
bāraṇa beḷaku e grantha rakhichi jñānī janaṅka pīyūṣa.

Awmzia:
Ngaithla rawh, zirtir Ramadas, heng hi thuruk thute an ni. Mi â hmaah hrilh suh, chuti lo chuan sual i khawhral ang. Heng thuziakte hi mihringte tana vaukhanna a ni a, Kaliyuga tawp hunah Pathian mi rinawmte tan Amrita ang a ni ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan heti hian a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ହୋଇବ ଡାକିନୀ ଗୋଳ ପଡ଼ିବ ଚହଳ,
ଅବନୀ କମ୍ପିବ ସଦା ହୋଇ ଟଳଟଳ।
ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ମାନ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Hoiba ḍākinī goḷa paḍiba chahaḷa,
Abanī kampiba sadā hoi taḷataḷa.
Nānā utpāta māna antarikṣe heba,
Bhakta binā anya kehi jāṇi na pāriba.

Awmzia:
Kaliyuga tawp hunah, Dharma dinthar lehna hunah, indona rapthlak tak a thleng ang a, khawvelah buaina nasa tak a awm ang. Lei hi a nghing ang. Boruakah buaina tam tak a thleng ang. Mahse, Pathian mi rinawmte tih loh chu, tuma'n heng hi Lalpa tihchhiatna Leela a ni tih an hrethiam lovang.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika thuziak ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a zirtir Ramadas hnenah heti hian a sawi a:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାବୁରାମ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ କହିବୁ ନାହିଁ,
ଘୋର କଳିକାଳ ଟାହି ଯେ ଟାପରା ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା ହେବ ତହିଁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shuṇa Bāburāma Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhanku kahibu nāhiñ,
Ghora Kaḷikāḷa ṭāhi je ṭāparā bānā nindā heba tahiñ.

Awmzia:
Aw zirtir Ramadas, he ‘Chakada Madana’ thuziak thu hi mi â hmaah hrilh suh. Kaliyuga thuk takah chuan, mi â te chuan an nuihsawh ang a, chu chuan Lalpa sawiselna a thlen ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika thuziak ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a ziak nawn leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇଟି ବିଷ ଜ୍ଞାନୀଙ୍କୁ ଅଟେ ପିୟୂଷ,
ଅଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲେ ମୋ ଅମୃତେ ହେବ ବିଷ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mūrkhanku e grantha aṭaiṭi biṣa jñānīnku aṭe piyūṣa,
Ajñānī āgare prakāsha karile mo amṛute heba biṣa.

Awmzia:
Mi â tan chuan, heng Malika thute hi tur ang an ni a, mi fing tan erawh chuan 'Piyush' emaw Amrita ang an ni. Heng thute hi mi hriat loh hnenah hrilh chu Amrita-ah tur pawlh ang a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a ziak nawn leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କହୁଛି ଏହି ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଅଟଇ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଏହା ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ଖେଳ ହୋଇବ ତୋତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଗଣ୍ଠିଆ ଭକତ ଅଟଇ ଗଣ୍ଠି କଥାରେ ମନ,
ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ ପୁରାଣ।
ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ହୋଇବ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକତ,
ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଦାସରେ ମୋର ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra kahuchi ehi niṣṭhā bachana,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa aṭai bhaktaṅka gaṇṭhidhana.
Bāraṇa beḷaku rakhichi ehā bhaktaṅka pāiṃ,
Gupatare kheḷa hoiba tote deli chetei.
Gaṇṭhiā bhakata aṭai gaṇṭhi kathāre mana,
Sātasha bāstarī parārddha bhabishyata purāṇa.
Dharma saṃsthāpana hoiba bhakta hebe mukata,
Shuṇa Rāmachandra dāsare mora bachana satya.

Awmzia:
Ngaithla rawh zirtir Ramadas, ka Malika thute hi Pathian mi rinawmte tan 'Ganthidhana' emaw nunna hlu tak ang an ni. Lalpa chuan Dharma a dinthar ang a, Pathian mi rinawmte chu chhandam an ni ang. Heng Leela te hi thuruk takin a thleng ang. Pathian mi rinawmte chhandamna atan chauhvin heng Malika thute hi ka phuah a ni. ‘Barana Bela’ emaw Kaliyuga tawp hunah an tan tangkai tak an ni ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika thuziak ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a ziak belh leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗେଇ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଖେଦା ଲେଖଇ।
ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇ ଏହି,
ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ।
ଉତ୍ତମ ଶିଷ୍ୟରେ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Nemāḷa baṭaku laya lagei,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa khedā lekhai.
Sāraswata grantha aṭai ehi,
Beḷaku rakhichi bhaktaṅka pāiñ.
Uttama shiṣyare kara prakāsha,
Bānā nindā kahe Achyuta Dāsa.

Awmzia:
Nemalo Banyan thing hnuai ah hian, he ‘Chakada Madana’ Malika thuziak hi ka ziak a ni. Kaliyuga tawp hunah Pathian mi rinawmte tan tangkai tak a ni ang. Pathian mi rinawm tha berte zingah chauh tlangzarh rawh; chuti lo chuan, heng thute leh Lalpa sawiselna a awm ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a ziak nawn leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗି ଅଲଗାରେ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଭକ୍ତ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ ଜାଣ,
ଭକତ ଜାଣିବେ ଏହାର ଯେ ଭେଦ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଅଛି ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ପ୍ରକାଶ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟ ଯେ ସାଧୁ ପଦାଶ୍ରିତ ଭଣେ ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Lāgi alagāre Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa bhakta gaṇṭhidhana jāṇa,
bhakata jāṇibe ehāra je bheda shūnyare achi pramāṇa.
Bāraṇa beḷare e grantha prakāsha mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
Nemāḷa baṭa je sādhu padāshrita bhaṇe pāmara Achyuta.

Awmzia:
Chakada Madana hi Pathian mi rinawmte tan hausakna a ni. Kaliyuga tawp hunah chuan, Pathian mi rinawmte chauhvin a thuruk leh a hriat thiam theih loh thilte an hrethiam thei ang. Mi â hmaah tlangzarh suh.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ ah chuan heti hian a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଚେତୁଆ ଭକତମାନଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ତିନି ଅଠର ଖଣ୍ଡ,
ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଲେଖିଲା ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଦୁରୁଦଣ୍ଡ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chetuā bhaktamānaṅka nimante tini aṭhara khaṇḍa,
Bhabishya lekhilā pāmara Achyuta na jāṇanti durudaṇḍa.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan Malika hrilh lâwkna bu 318 chu Pathian mi rinawm harhvakte hlawkna turin chauh a phuah a ni. Mi â leh mi sualte chuan heng thuziak thuruk hi an hrethiam thei lovang.

A ziak nawn leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ବିନାଶି ପ୍ରଭୁ ମେଦିନୀ ରଖିବେ,
ସନାତନ ଧର୍ମ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପ୍ରଚାର କରିବେ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନରହରି,
ନାମ ଭଜଏ ଘୋର କଳିରେ ଯିବ ତରି।
ବିପଦ କାଳରେ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ରକ୍ଷା ପାଇଁ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ରଚଇ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mlechha bināshi Prabhu medinī rakhibe,
Sanātana Dharma nishche prachāra karibe.
Kaḷiyuga sheshare prakāsha Narahari,
Nāma bhajae ghora kaḷire jiba tari.
Bipada kāḷare bhaktaṅka rakṣā pāiñ,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa grantha Achyuta rachai.

Awmzia:
Kaliyuga tawp hunah, Lalpa chu a lo chang ang. Lei humhim nan mi sualte leh mi sualte chu a tichhe ang a, Sanatana Dharma a dinthar leh ang. Mahapurush chuan chu hunah chuan, Pathian mi rinawmte kaihhruaina leh an humhimna atan, Malika thute hi a phuah a ni tih a ziak a. He changah hian, Mahapurush chuan Malika phuah chhan chu harsatna hunah Pathian mi rinawmte humhim leh an chhandamna kawng hawn a ni tih chiang takin a ziak a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika thuziak, 'Chakada Madana' ah chuan a ziak belh leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ପାପ ନାଶନ,
ଖେଦା ଚେତାବନୀ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଜ୍ଞାନ।
ସବୁ ଲେଖିଗଲି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଭକ୍ତ ମୋହର ଯେ ସୋଦର ଭାଇ।
ଭକତ ମୋ ପଞ୍ଚ ପ୍ରାଣଟି ଜାଣ,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ଖେଦା ଲିଖନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dharma saṃsthāpana pāpa nāshana,
khedā chetābanī bhabishya jñāna.
Sabu lekhigali bhaktanka pāiṃ,
bhakta mohara je sodara bhāī.
Bhakata mo pañcha prāṇaṭi jāṇa,
bhaktanka nimaṃte khedā likhana.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kali Yuga tawp hunah sualna tawpna leh Dharma dinthar lehna chungchang ka Malika thutea ka ziah hrilh lâwkna zawng zawngte hi Pathian mi rinawmte tan vek a ni, Pathian mi rinawmte hi ka unau ang an nih avangin. Pathian mi rinawmte hi ka nunna thâwk panga (Pancha-prana) an ni a, heng Malika thute hi an tan chauhvin ka ziak a ni.

Mahapurush chuan a ziak nawn leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଅଟଇ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଜାଣ,
ଘୋର କଳିଯୁଗ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନ ଛନ୍ତି ଡର ଲାଗୁଅଛି ପୁଣ।
ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନ କର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି ମୁହିଁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Sātasha bāstarī parārddha aṭai Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa jāṇa,
ghora kaliyuga duṣhṭajana chaṃti ḍara lāguachi puṇa.
Mūrkhanka āgare prakāsha na kara niyama deuchi muṃhi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, zirtir Rama, he Malika thuziak 'Chakada Madana' ah hian Parardhas emaw bung 772 a awm a ni. Mi â hmaah tlangzarh suh. Kali Yuga rapthlak takah hian mi sual tam tak an awm a; an hnen atangin thuruk takin vawng rawh.

'Chakada Madana' ah ngei chuan, Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନୋହିଲା ନୋହିଲା ପଡୁଥିବ ହୁରି ଭକ୍ତ ଜାଣିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shuṇa Rāmadāsa Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
nohilā nohilā paḍuthiba huri bhakta jāṇiba abashya.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Aw zirtir Ramadasa, heng Malika hrilh lâwkna thute hi mi â hmaah tlangzarh suh. Heng thute hi dawt an ni, heng thil hi a thleng lovang an ti ang. Chuvangin, Pathian mi rinawmte hnenah chauh hrilh rawh; anni chauhvin an ring thei ang.

3 Malika Hrilh Lâwkna Lo Thleng Dik Tawh Te

Bhavishya Malika hi Mahaprabhu thu ngei a ni a, chuvangin Malika-a hrilh lâwkna tam tak lo thleng dik tawh hi mak a ni lo. Hrilh lâwkna dik 100% lo thleng dik tawh thenkhatte chu kan sawi ho ang.

3.1 Atal Bihari Vajpayee Ji Chungchang Hrilh Lâwkna

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika thuziak 'Kalikalpa Gita' leh 'Achyuta Thara' ah chuan heti hian a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ ଯେବେ, କଳ୍କୀଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା ଲାଗିବ ତେବେ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ରାଜା, ଫେରିବ କିଛିତାହାର ତରିଜା।
କିଛି ଦିନ ଭୋଗ କରିବ ମହୀ, ତେର ଦିନ ସେହି ଛତ୍ର ଉଡ଼ାଇ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇଲେ ଯେବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଯେ ରାଜା, ତାଙ୍କ ସମୟରେ କିଛି ହୋଇଲା ତରିଜା।
ତେରଦିନ ଛତ୍ର ସେହି ଉଡ଼େଇଣ ଦେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ମହୀ ଭୋଗ କଲେ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
ପ୍ରଥମେ ତେର ଦିନ ପ୍ରଧାନ ମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ହେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ଶାସନ କରିଲେ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ ଶକତି କାହାର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Tera dina pakṣa hoiba jebe, Kaḷkīṅka līḷā lāgiba tebe.
Khetratre hoiba Brāhmaṇa rājā, pheriba kichhitāhāra tarijā.
Kichhi dina bhoga kariba mahī, tera dina sehi chhatra uḍāi.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Khetratre hoile jebe Brāhmaṇa je rājā, tāṅka samayare kichhi hoilā tarijā.
Teradina chhatra sehi uḍeiṇa dele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa mahī bhoga kale.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Prathame tera dina pradhāna mantrī hele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa shāsana karile.
Achyuta bachana aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ shakati kāhāra.

Awmzia:
Kum za tam tak liam taah khan, Mahapurush chuan India zalenna hnu lama politician pawimawh tak chungchang a ziak a. Brahmin-in India a rorel hunah chuan, khawvelah danglamna tam tak a lang chhuak ang a ti. A hmasa berin, ni 13 chhung a rorel ang a, hnuah kum nga chhung a thunei ang. Mahapurush chuan Delhi atanga roreltu hming chu "Atal Bihari Vajpayee" a ni ang tih a ziak a. Chanchin hian a tarlang angin, Shri Vajpayee chuan 1996-ah ni 13 chhung PM hna a chelh a, chumi hnuah 1999 atanga 2004 thleng kum 5 chhung a chelh leh a ni.

3.2 Mahatma Gandhi Chungchang Hrilh Lâwkna

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan, kum 600 liam taah khan a thuziak lar tak 'Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' ah chuan Mahatma Gandhi chungchang hrilh lâwkna zau tak a lo siam tawh a.

Odia Sloka:

ଜନମ ହୋଇ ଗୁଜରାଟରେ, ମୋହନ ମୋହିବ ରାମ ନାମରେ।
ଅହିଂସାକୁ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର କରିବ ଜାଣ, ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ ହଟିବେ ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣ।
ତା ଅନ୍ତେ ଧର୍ମ ହୋଇବ ହାଣି, ମୋହନ ରାଜୁତି ହୋଇବ ହାଣି।
ଗୁଳି ଚୋଟେ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଯିବ, ଗଡସେ କଳିମଞ୍ଜି ପୋତିବ।
ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବେ ସଂସାର ପ୍ରାଣୀ, ଟେକି କହୁଥିବେ କଥା ନ ଜାଣି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Janama hoi Gujarāṭare, Mohana mohiba Rāma nāmare.
Ahinsāku astra kariba jāṇa, phiriṅgī haṭibe se kāḷe jāṇa.
Tā ante dharma hoiba hāṇi, Mohana rājuti hoiba hāṇi.
Guḷi choṭe tāra prāṇa jiba, Gaḍse kaḷimañji potiba.
Jāṇi na pāribe saṃsāra prāṇī, ṭeki kahuthibe kathā na jāṇi.

Awmzia:
"Gujarat state-ah a piang ang. A hming chu 'Mohan' a ni ang a, Rama hming chu a lam reng ang. 'Ahimsa' (Tharum Hman Lo) chu a ralthuam a ni ang a, a thawhrimna avangin British (Firingis) te chu India chhuahsan tura tih luih an ni ang."

Mahapurush chuan Mohan chu amahin thuneihna a chelh lo vang a, a thih hnuah Dharma a hniam ang tih a ziak bawk. "Godse" hming pu mi pakhatin "Guli" (bullet) hmangin a that ang a, chutianga a tih chuan ramah Kali chi a theh ang tih a sawi chiang hle. Mi tam takin Gandhi chu an hrethiam lo vang a, hriat loh avangin tualthatu thiltih chu thiam an chantir ang tih pawh a hrilh lawk. Kan hriat angin, Hnam Pa chungchang hrilh lawkna thu tin hi a dik vek a ni.

3.3 Kum 1999 Super Cyclone leh Kalpavata Tliah Hrilh Lawkna

A lehkhabu 'Jai Birachit Chautisha' ah Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan kum 1999 thlipui chu kum 600 hmain a lo hrilh lawk tawh a ni.

Odia Sloka:

ସମୁଦ୍ର ଉଠି ଆସିବ ବତାସ ବହିବ, ଶ୍ରୀ କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଭାଙ୍ଗି ପକାଇବ।
ସତ୍ୟଭାବ ହେ, ସାଲ ଅନେଶତ କର ଠାବ ହେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Samudra uṭhi āsiba batāsa bahiba, Shrī Kaḷpabaṭa mora bhāṅgi pakāiba.
Satyabhāba he, sāla aneshata kara ṭhāba he.

Awmzia:
"Tuipui a lian ang a, thlipui na tak a tleh ang. Kalpavata thianghlim (Jagannath Temple compound chhunga Banyan thingkung hlui) phel chu a tliak ang." Mahapurush chuan he thil hi kum "Anesata" (99) ah a thleng ang tih a sawi.

October 29, 1999 khan Super Cyclone chuan Odisha chu 250 km/h thli chakna nen a nuai a. Chhiatna nasa tak a thlen a, mi 10,000 chuang nunna a la. Hrilh lawkna ang chiahin, Puri temple chhunga Kalpavata thingkung hlui phel lian tak chu he thlipui hian a tichhe a ni.

3.4 India Zalenna Hrilh Lawkna (1947)

A lehkhabu 'Garuda Kalpa' ah Saint Achyutananda chuan India chu kum 1947-ah British rorelna atangin a zalen ang tih number code hmangin a hrilh lawk.

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ଭକ୍ତି ବେଦ ବାରକୁ ଥୋଇ, ଏ ଠାରେ ବିଦେଶୀ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Brahma bhakti beda bāraku thoi, e ṭhāre bideshī jibe paḷāi.

Number Hrilhfiahna:
Mahapurush chuan number te tan hian thil entirna a hmang:
  • Brahma: 1-in a entir ("Ekam Brahma" tih ang hian).
  • Bhakti: 9-in a entir (Navadha Bhakti hmun kua ang hian).
  • Veda: 4-in a entir (Veda pali ang hian).
  • Baar: 7-in a entir (kar ni sarih ang hian).

Hêngte hi an belhkhawm chuan (1-9-4-7) 1947 a ni. Hrilh lawkna chuan he kumah hian ram dang mi (British) te chu he ram atang hian an tlanbo ang tih a sawi.

3.5 Hmeichhe Naupai Tihhlum Hrilh Lawkna Chungchang

'Jaiphula Malika' ah Mahapurush chuan Kaliyuga tawp hunah khawtlang sualna a awm ang tih a hrilh lawk:

Odia Sloka:

କନ୍ୟା ରତ୍ନକୁ ଗର୍ଭର, ନଷ୍ଟ କରିବ ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ହୃଦୟେ ନ କରି ପାପ ବିଚାର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kanyā ratnaku garbhara, naṣṭa kariba tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
hṛudaye na kari pāpa bichāra.

Awmzia:
"Mihringte chuan hmeichhe naupang (Kanya Ratna) chu chhul (Garbha) chhungah an tihlum nawn fo ang a, an thinlungah sualna hriatna tel lovin an ti ang." Hei hian tun hnaia khawtlang tibuaitu hmeichhe naupai tihhlum hun thim chu dik takin a tarlang.

3.6 Kum 2020 Hripui (COVID-19) Hrilh Lawkna

Mahapurush Hadidas, kum zabi 18-naa piang Panchasakhas-a piang chuan 'Agata Bhavishyat Chautisha' ah a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ବିଂଶ ଯେବେ ବର୍ଷ ବୋଲାଇବ, ବହୁ ବିତିପାତ ହେବ।
ଅଜଣା ବ୍ୟାଧିରେ ବହୁ ନାଶ ଯିବେ, ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dui biṃsha jebe barsha bolāiba, bahu bitipāta heba.
Ajaṇā byādhire bahu nāsha jibe, hāhākāra paḍijiba.

Awmzia:
"Kum 'Dui-Binsha' (Kum Sanghnih Sawm / 2020) a lo thlen hunah, buaina nasa tak a awm ang. Mihringte chu natna hriat loh (Ajana Vyadhi) avangin an thi ang a, hmun tinah beidawnna au thawm (Haahakaar) a awm ang." Hei hi kum 2020-a khawvel pumpui tibuaitu COVID-19 hripui sawifiahna dik tak a ni.

3.7 Hirakud Dam Hrilh Lawkna

A lehkhabu 'Chakada Madana' leh 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' ah Saint Achyutananda leh Arkhit Das chuan Odisha-ah tuihna lian tak sak tur an hrilh lawk.

Odia Sloka:

ଛବିଶ ଠାବରେ ହୀରାକୁଦ ଉଛୁଳିବ, ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଅର୍ଦ୍ଧଭାଗ ଜଳମଗ୍ନ ହେବ।
ଧନଜନ ନଷ୍ଟ ହେବ କହିଲି ବୁଝାଇ, ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ବେଶୀ ବେଳ ନାହିଁ।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲିଣଟି ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକାକାର ହେବ।
ସେହି ବେଳେ ଗଙ୍ଗା ପୁଣି ଆସିବେଟି ମାଡ଼ି, ବୈତରଣୀ, ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣୀ ଯେ ଦେବେ ଘୋର ରଡ଼ି।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ବଡ଼ ବନ୍ଧ ତିଆରି ଯେ ଥିବ, ସେଇବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁଦ୍ୱାରା ଭାଙ୍ଗିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ଛଅ ଜିଲ୍ଲା ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇ, ଯିବାପାଇଁ ବାଟ ନ ପାଇବେ ପୁଣି କେହି।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲି ଗ୍ରାମର ଯେ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chabiṣha ṭhābare Hīrākuda uchuḷiba, e Oḍiśhā arddhabhāga jaḷamagna heba.
Dhanajana naṣhṭa heba kahili bujhāi, dīna Achyuta kahai beśhī beḷa nāhiñ.
Hīrākuda boliṇaṭi grāma nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu ekākāra heba.
Sehi beḷe Gaṅgā puṇi āsibeṭi māḍi, Baitaraṇī, Brāhmaṇī je debe ghora raḍi.
Oḍiśhāre baḍa bandha tiāri je thiba, seibeḷe śhatrudwārā bhāṅgiṇa je jiba.
Oḍiśhāre chha-a jillā jaḷārṇnaba hoi, jibāpāiñ bāṭa na pāibe puṇi kehi.
Hīrākuda boli grāmara je nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu eka je hoiba.

Awmzia:
Achyutananda Das ji chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Chakada Madana' ah leh Brahma Avadhut saint Arakhita Das, kum zabi sawmpariatna tawp lamah piang a, Olashuni pukah sadhana famkim a ti, chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' ah Odisha state-ah tuihna lian tak sak a ni ang a, a hming chu "Hirakud" a ni ang tih a ziak. A sawi belh zelnaah chuan Kaliyuga tawp hunah, hmelma beihna avangin chu tuihna chu a tichhe ang a, chu chuan Odisha state district paruk chu tuiin a chimpil ang a, lirthei kal vel a tawp ang a, sum leh mihring chan nasa tak a awm ang. Mahapurushate chuan chu hunah chuan Ganga chuan a hmel rapthlak tak a lantir ang a, Baitarani, Brahmani, etc. luite pawhin chhiatna rapthlak tak an thlen ang an ti.

Hriat angin, kum 1948-ah India rama Odisha state-ah Hirakud hming pu tuihna sak hna chu tan a ni a, kum 1953-ah zawh a ni. Kum 1957 January thlaah, Prime Minister Pandit Jawaharlal Nehru hmasa chuan he tuihna hi a hawng. Km 26 a sei, Odisha-a Sambalpur district-a Mahanadi lui chungah sak a ni a, he tuihna hi tunah chuan khawvelah mihring siam tuihna sei ber a ni. Chutiang chuan, he hrilh lawkna hmasa lam chu a dik tih a lang.

Reiloteah khawvelin hrilh lawkna bung hnihna pawh a dik tak a hmu ang a, India leh China indona hunah, China missile beihna avangin he tuihna hi a tichhe ang a, Odisha state chu tui lian rapthlak takin a nuai ang.

3.8 Pakistan Pian Hrilh Lawkna Kum Zabi Tam Tak Hmain

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das leh midangte chuan Pakistan din leh a hming chu kum 600 liamtaah 'Gupta Kheda Malika' leh 'Bhavishyat Malika' tih lehkhabu teah an sawi tawh. Chutiang bawkin, kum zabi 18-naa saint Arkhit Das chuan 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' tihah a sawi bawk.

Odia Sloka:

ପାକିସ୍ତାନର ଦଶା ଯାହା ହେବ ସହସା, ମନ କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦଇ ଥରେ ଶୁଣ ସେ ଭାଷା।
ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ତାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ, ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପ୍ରାଚ୍ୟ ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ଗଣ ଏହିପରି ମିଳିଣ, ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ଲାଗି ସର୍ବେ କରିବେ ରଣ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟଗୋଟି ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ହାହାକାର ଶୁଭୁଣ ଯେ ଥିବ।
ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ, ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟ ଗୋଟା ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ପଛ ବେଳକୁ ତ ପୁଣି ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଯେ ସ୍ଫୁରିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Pākistānara dashā jāhā heba sahasā, mana karṇṇa dei thare shuṇa se bhāṣā.
Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākistānaṭi raṅge, mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Prāchya pāshchātya gaṇa ehipari miḷiṇa, Pākistāna lāgi sarbe karibe raṇa.
Pākistāna rājyagoṭi chhārakhāra heba, chāriāḍe hāhākāra shubhuṇa je thiba.
Chīna Pākistāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa, Ruṣiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmana Jāpāna.
Pākistāna rājya goṭā chhārakhāra heba, pachha beḷaku ta puṇi buddhi je sphuriba.

Awmzia:
"Pakistan hming pu ram pakhat chu thut thutah a lo awm ang. Tawp hunah, India chuan Pakistan nen indona na tak an hmachhawn ang. Middle East leh Western ram chak takte chu an inzawm ang; Muslim ramte, Western ramte, China leh America chuan Pakistan an thlawp ang a, Russia, Germany leh Japan erawh chuan India an pui ang. Pakistan chu he indonaah hian tihchhiat vek (Chara-khara) a ni ang a, khawvel pumpui beidawnna zingah, a tawpah chuan a tihsualte a hre chhuak ang."

Hriat reng tur chu, 'Pakistan' tih thu hi kum 1933-ah chauh siam chhuah a ni a, nimahsela, hêng saint-te hian kum zabi tam tak hmain an lo hming lo vuah tawh a ni.

3.9 Bhubaneswar Khawpui Hrilh Lawknate Chungchang

Tunlai Bhubaneswar lungphum chu kum 1948-ah PM Nehru-a'n a phum. Nimahsela, a khawpui anga sawina chu 'Chakada Madana' leh 'Kali Agata Bhabishyant' tih Malika lehkhabu teah a lang.

Odia Sloka:

ଭୈରବୀ ଡାକିବ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର, ଚୈତ୍ର ମାସ ଯେ ମଙ୍ଗଳବାର।
ପୃଥିବୀରେ ଗୋଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଆରମ୍ଭିବ ଯେବେ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସୈନ୍ୟ ମାଡ଼ି ଯେ ଆସିବେ।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ, ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ତ ପୁଣି ରାଜଧାନୀ ଜାଣ, ବିଦେଶୀ ଶତ୍ରୁଙ୍କ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି ପଡ଼ିବଟି ପୁଣ।
ଭୈରବ ଡାକ ହେବ ଜାଣ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରଟି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhairabī ḍākiba Bhubaneshwar, Chaitra māsa je Maṅgaḷabāra.
Pṛuthibīre goḷa yuddha ārambhiba jebe, Bhubaneshwaraku sainya māḍi je āsibe.
Bhubaneshwarare lāgiba goḷa, Ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Bhubaneshwar ta puṇi rājadhānī jāṇa, bideshī shatruṅka dṛuṣṭi paḍibaṭi puṇa.
Bhairaba ḍāka heba jāṇa, Bhubaneshwarati pramāṇa.

Awmzia:
Saint-te chuan Chaitra thla Thawhlehni-ah, Bhubaneswar-ah ri nasa tak (Bhairavi's roar) hriat tur a awm ang tih an hrilh lawk. Khawvel Indopui Pathumnaah, hmelma sipai rualte chu Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Bana) an thleng ang. Lehkhabute chuan Bhubaneswar chu khawpui a ni ang a, ram dang hmelmate tan beihna hmun a ni ang tih chiang takin an sawi.

3.10 Spiritual Guru Hmingthang Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra Hrilh Lawkna Chungchang

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan, kum 600 liamta velah a Malika lehkhabute-ah, "Yugapurusha" (Hun Mihring) pian tur a hrilh lawk a, chu chuan Pathian thiltih ropui tak tak tam tak a ti ang a, a hmingthanna chu khawvel hmun tinah a darh ang.

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳାମାନ ହେବ କୀର୍ତ୍ତି ରହି ଯିବ, ଅନୁକୂଳ ନାମ ଧରି।
ଦେଶ ବିଦେଶରୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିବେ, ସତସଙ୍ଗ ଆଶ୍ରା କରି।
ସତନାମ ଗୋଟି ଉଚ୍ଚାରି ବୋଲିବେ, ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହେବ ମେଳ।
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ମାର୍ଗେ ରହି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦେଖିବ, ଯୁଗପୁରୁଷଙ୍କ ଖେଳ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Līḷāmāna heba kīrtti rahi jiba, Anukūḷa nāma dhari.
Desha bidesharu bhakata āsibe, sat-saṅga āshrā kari.
Satanāma goṭi uchchāri bolibe, bhaktaṅka heba meḷa.
Shūnya mārge rahi Achyuta dekhiba, Yugapuruṣaṅka kheḷa.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan "Anukul" hming pu saint pakhat a lo lang ang a, India leh ram dang atanga Pathian mi te chu a "Satsanga"-ah himna zawngin an lo kal ang tih a ziak. Hming thianghlim (Satnaam) chu lam a ni ang a, Pathian mi tam tak an inkhawm ang. Achyutananda Das chuan amah ngei pawh, thil hmuh theih loh/awm lohna hmun (Shunya Maarga)-ah awmin, he Yugapurusha Pathian leela chu a hmu ang tih a sawi belh bawk.

He zawlneihna hi tihlawhtlingin, Anukulchandra Chakravarty chu September 14, 1888-ah, Bengal ram then hran loh (tunah Bangladesh) Pabna district-ah a piang a. Khawvel pumah Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra tia hriat a ni a, ani chuan 1946-ah Deoghar, Jharkhand-ah 'Satsang Ashram' a din a ni. Khawvel hmun hrang hrang atanga mi maktaduai tam takin a rawngbawlnaah an tel a, châng takin 'Yuga Purushottam' tiin an ko thin.

3.11 Malika Lehkhabuahte Atomic Bomb hriattirna

Atomic bomb hi kum zana sawmhnihna laihawl velah siam chhuah a ni chungin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das (a lehkhabu 'Gupta Kheda Malika' leh 'Bhavishyat Malika'-ah) leh Balarama Das (a lehkhabu 'Kali Tandaba Gita'-ah) chuan kum zabi tam tak liamtaah khan an lo sawi tawh a ni.

Odia Sloka:

ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା କରିଦେବେଟି ଜମା, ପୋଡ଼ିଜାଳି ଦେବା ପାଇଁ ଭାରତ ସୀମା।
ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା ଯାହା ଲାଗି ଗାରିମା, ଦେଖାଇ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ଆଜି ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ସେନା।
ତାହା ଫୁଟିବ ନାହିଁ କେଣେ ଯିବ ମିଳାଇ, ଏହା ଦେଖି ବିଦେଶୀଏ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।
ଅଗ୍ନିର ଦାହିକା ଶକ୍ତି ଲୋ ବଉଳ ପ୍ରଭୁ କରିବେ ହରଣ, ପରମାଣୁ ବୋମା ଯାହାଲାଗି ଗାରିମା ଶୀତଳ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Paramāṇu je bomā karidebeṭi jamā, poḍijāḷi debā pāiñ Bhārata sīmā.
Paramāṇu je bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā, dekhāi huanti āji pāshchātya senā.
Tāhā phuṭiba nāhiñ keṇe jiba miḷāi, ehā dekhi bideshīe jibe paḷāi.
Agnira dāhikā shakti lo bauḷa Prabhu karibe haraṇa, paramāṇu bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā shītaḷa hoiba jāṇa.

Awmzia:
Mi thianghlimte chuan an ziak a, Khawvel Indopui Pathumnaah, India ram hmelmate chuan India ramri tihchhiat nan Atomic Bombs (Paramanu Bomba) hmangin ruahmanna an siam ang. Mahse, an ruahmanna chu a hlawhchham ang, Lalpa Kalki-an heng bomb-te kangralna/tihchhiatna thiltihtheihna (Dahika Shakti) a hip luh vek dawn avangin. Hmar lam leh hmelma sipaiin an chhuang em em thilthuamte chu "vawt" (thawh lo) ah a chang ang a, India ramahte chuan engmah a ti thei lovang. He thil theih loh hi an hmuhin, ram dang hmelmate chu hlauvin an tlanbo ang. Lehkhabuahte chuan nuclear indopui rapthlak tak (Anu-Paramanu Yuddha) a thleng ang a, khawvel hi chenna tlak loh deuhthaw a siam ang, chu chu Pathian rawngbawltute chuan an hmu ang.

3.12 Madan Mohan Malaviya, Mahatma Gandhi, leh Motilal Nehru hriattirna

1850-ah a piang, mi thianghlim ropui Bhima Bhoi (Panchasakhas thurin zuitu) chuan a lehkhabu 'Padmakalpa'-ah India zalenna sualtu hruaitute chungchang a hrilhfiah.

Odia Sloka:

ମଦନମାଲବ୍ୟ ନାମ ବୋଲିକରି ଏକ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବ ଜନ୍ମି,
ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ସହିତରେ ଅହିଂସା ଧର୍ମରେ ନିରନ୍ତରେ ଥିବେ ପୁଣି।
ଚୁଡ଼ଙ୍ଗ ବଂଶରେ ଉତପତ୍ତି ହୋଇ ମୋତିଲାଲ ଯେ ନେହେରୁ,
ମହାପ୍ରତାପୀ ଅଟଇ ସେହୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିଛନ୍ତି ଶୂନ୍ୟପୁରୁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Madanamālabya nāma bolikari eka bhakta thiba janmi,
Gāndhī sahitare ahinsā dharmare nirantare thibe puṇi.
Chuḍaṅga baṃshare utpatti hoi Motilāl je Neheru,
mahāpratāpī aṭai sehu bhakta āsichanti Shūnyapuru.

Awmzia:
He lehkhabu hian "Madan Mohan Malaviya" tia hming pu Pathian rawngbawltu a piang dawn tih a lo hrilh lawk a, ani chuan "Mahatma Gandhi" nen, tharum thawh lohna sakhaw (Ahimsa) behchhanin hmalakna an kalpui ang. Chung bakah, thuneihna leh thil tithei tak Pathian rawngbawltu "Motilal Nehru" pian tur thu a sawi bawk. An pathum hian Indian National Congress leh zalenna sualnaah hruaitu pawimawh tak an ni.

3.13 Mahatma Gandhi, Nathuram Godse-a thah a nih tur chungchang zawlneihna

'Jaiphula Malika'-ah, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan Mahatma thihna tur a lo hrilh lawk a ni.

Odia Sloka:

ମୋହନ ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ମରଣ, ନାଥୁରାମ ହସ୍ତେ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏ କଥା ସିଦ୍ଧ ଫଳ ରେ ନିପୁଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mohana Gāndhī maraṇa, Nāthurāma haste hoiba jāṇa lo jāiphula,
e kathā siddha phaḷa re nipuṇa.

Awmzia:
"Mohan Gandhi thihna chu Nathuram kutah a thleng ang." Chanchin hian a ziah angin, January 30, 1948-ah, Nathuram Vinayak Godse chuan Mahatma Gandhi a that a ni.

3.14 Mahatma Gandhi Hruainaa India Zalenna

'Kali Bhagabata'-ah, Mahapurush Abhiram Paramahansa (kum zana sawmhnihnaa Malika thurin zuitu mi thianghlim) chuan politika inthlak danglam dan a hrilhfiah.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟାଗ୍ରହୀ ହୋଇଲେ ମେଳି ।
ଅନ୍ତର ହୋଇଯିବ କଳି ।।
ଇଉରୋପିଆନ ଯିବେ ଛାଡ଼ି ।
ମୋହନ ସେନା ଦେବେ କାଢ଼ି ।।
ପ୍ରଥମ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ସେନାପତି ।
ହେବେ ମୋହନ ସୈନ୍ୟ ଘେତି ।।
ମୋହନ ବୋଲି ଯାଙ୍କୁ କହି ।
ମହତ ଆତ୍ମା ଅଟେ ସେହି ।।
ସେହି ନାରଦ ମହାମୁନ୍ୟ ।
ତାଙ୍କୁ କରନ୍ତି ସର୍ବେ ମାନ୍ୟ ।।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Satyāgrahī hoile meḷi,
antara hoijiba kaḷi.
Iuropiāna jibe chhāḍi,
Mohana senā debe kāḍhi.
Prathama yuddhe senāpati,
hebe Mohana sainya gheti.
Mohana boli jāṅku kahi,
mahata ātmā aṭe sehi.
Sehi Nārada mahāmunya,
tāṅku karanti sarbe mānya.

Awmzia:
Mi thianghlim chuan a lo hrilh lawk a, Satyagrahi-te inkhawmna hmangin, Europeans-te chu chhuak tura tih an ni ang. Ani chuan "Mohan" (Gandhi) chu he zalenna sualna ropui hmasa ber hruaitu/hotu atan a hria. Chung bakah, Mahatma Gandhi chu thlarau ropui (Mahatma) leh Narada-a lo chang, a thu sawite chu mi zawng zawngin an zah tur a ni tih a hrilhfiah bawk. Hei hi a thleng dik a, Gandhi hruainain British-te chu India ram atanga chhuak tura hruaitu pawimawh tak a ni.

4 Kaliyuga Kum

Sanatan lehkhabuahte tarlan danin, Kaliyuga hun pum chu kum 432,000 a ni. Bhavishya Malika pawhin he hun chhung zawng hi a nemnghet a, mahse, zawlneihna pawimawh tak a belhchhah: mihringte sualna nasa lutuk avangin, Kaliyuga hian a hun chhung zawng a hmang zo lovang.

4.1 Kum 432,000 atan Lehkhabu Thianghlim Thupêk

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan 'Adi Samhita'-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିରେ ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯାଏ, ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନିର୍ଣ୍ଣୟ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷire chāri lakṣa jāe, batiśa sahasra nirṇaya.

Awmzia:
"Kaliyuga-ah chuan, nuai li leh sang sawmthum pahnih (432,000) kum chu a hun chhung atan ruat a ni."

Chutah Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a lehkhabu, 'Dashapatala Malika'-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଭୋଗ ପ୍ରମାଣ ସାର,
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ହଜାର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaliyuga bhoga pramāṇa sāra,
chārilakṣha je batisha hajāra.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a ni, Kaliyuga hun chhung ruat chu nuai li, sang sawmthum pahnih kum a ni.

Ani chuan a Malika lehkhabu, 'Parardha Jataka'-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶି ସହସ୍ର କଳି,
କରିଲି ତିନି ଯୁଗଠାରୁ ବଳି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chārilakṣha batishi sahasra kali,
karili tini yugaṭhāru bali.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a ni, Kaliyuga hun chhung chu nuai li, sang sawmthum pahnih kum a ni.

Nimahsela, Mahapurushas-te chuan hei ringawt hi an sawi tawp mai lo; an Malika lehkhabuahte chuan an sawi bawk a, mihringte sualna hrang hrang avangin, Kaliyuga hian a hun chhung zawng a hmang zo lovang.

4.2 Engvangin nge Kaliyuga hi a hmaah a tawp ang

Panchasakhas-te chuan an hrilhfiah a, hun chhung theory-a kum 432,000 a ni chungin, "Bhoga" (a tak taka hman hun chhung) chu sualna rit lutuk avangin a tawi zawk ang. 'Dashapatala Malika'-ah chuan, hetiang hian a inziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର, ଭୋଗ ନୋହିବ କହ ପୀତବାସ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra, bhoga nohiba kaha Pītabāsa.

Awmzia:
"Aw Lalpa Pitabasa (Jagannath)! Engtin nge Kaliyuga hian a hun chhung zawng, kum 432,000 hi a hman zawh loh tur min hrilh rawh."

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu, 'Kalikalpa Gita'-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ବର୍ଷ ଆଉ,
ଭୋଗ ନହେବ ପାପଭାରା ଯହୁଁ।
ଆପଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଉଦିତ,
ପାପୀମାନଙ୍କୁ କରିବେ ମୁକତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chāri lakṣha batisha sasra barṣha āu,
bhoga naheba pāpabhārā jahuṃ.
Āpaṇe prabhu hoibe udita,
pāpīmānaṃku karibe mukata.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a ni, Kaliyuga-a sualna rit lutuk avangin, Kaliyuga hian a hun chhung kum 4,32,000 hi a hmang zo lovang. He hun hma pawhin, Lalpa chu a lo chang ang, sualtute chu tihboral an ni ang, leh Dharma chu din a ni ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu pakhatah a ziak nawn leh a:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ,
ପାପ ଯୋଗୁଁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kali chāri lakṣha batisha sasra nohiba bhoga,
pāpa joguṃ kṣhaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a ni, Kaliyuga hian a hun chhung ruat, kum 4,32,000 hi a hmang zo lovang. Sualna avangin, a hun chhung chu a tlahniam ang, awmzia chu, a pum puiin hman theih a ni lovang.

4.3 Kum 4,32,000 a nih loh chuan, Kaliyuga hun chhung a tak taka hman chu eng nge ni ang?

Tunlai hian, mi thenkhat chuan an thehdarh a, Malika tarlangin, Kaliyuga hun chhung a tak taka hman chu kum 10,000 a ni tih. Hei hi a dik lo hlak a ni tih hriat tur a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶ ସହସ୍ରରେ କଳିନାଶ ହେବ ଏ ଅଟେ ବିଷମ ବାଣୀ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dasha sahasrare kalināsha heba e aṭe biṣhama bāṇī.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a ni, Kaliyuga hi kum 10,000-ah a tawp ang – hei hi thu inmil lo emaw, dawt thu emaw a ni.

Panchasakha Mahapurushas-te chuan an Malika lehkhabuahte hmun tam takah an ziak a, Kaliyuga hun chhung a tak taka hman chu kum 5,000 a ni ang, chumi hnuah Lalpa Kalki chu a lo chang ang, leh Satya-Kali emaw Ananta Yuga emaw Adya Satya Yuga emaw a lo thleng ang.

Lo kal rawh u, he thil chungchangah hian Malika sloka hmangin pakhat te tein hriatna kan nei ang.

A hmasa berin, Lalpa Shri Krishna ngeiin Kaliyuga hun chhung chungchangah eng nge a sawi i hria ang u.

4.4 A Tak Taka Hun Chhung: Kum 5,000

Kaliyuga kum 10,000 a awh nia thenkhatin thenawm loh thu an thehdarh ang lo takin, Malika lehkhabute chuan Kali rorelna tak tak chu kum 5,000 hnuah a tawp a, chumi hnuah chuan inthlakthleng hun a awm ang, tiin an sawi nawn fo.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan 'Agata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika' ah Lalpa Krishna-an Garuda hnena a thu sawi a tarlang:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ, ପାପ ଯୋଗୁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେ ପଚାଶ ଭୋଗ ହେବ ନିକର, ଏଥୁ କିଛି ଉଣା ହୋଇବ ଶୁଣ ଗରୁଡ଼ ବୀର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷi chāri lakṣa batiśa sahasra nohiba bhoga, pāpa jogu kṣaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.
Pāñcha sahasra je pachāsha bhoga heba nikara, ethu kichi uṇā hoiba shuṇa Garuḍa bīra.

Awmzia:
"Aw, Garuda huaisen! Ngaithla rawh, Kaliyuga hian kum 432,000 a awh lovang. Sual avangin a hun chhung a tawi ang. Kum 5,050 velah a tawp ang.

4.5 Ravana Entirna

Odia Sloka:

ରାମଦାସ ବୋଲଇ ହୋ ଶୁଣ ଗୁରୁଦେବ, କଳିଯୁଗ କେତେ ପୁଣ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ଯେ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ, କେମନ୍ତେ ଯେ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ହୋଇବଟି ଶେଷ।
ଏଡ଼େ ବଡ଼ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ସଂଶୟ ଫେଡ଼ିଣ ମୋତେ କହ ଗୁରୁଦେବ।
ରାମଦାସ ବାଣୀ ଶୁଣି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହନ୍ତି, ତୋତେ କହୁଅଛି ରାମ ଶୁଣ ଦେଇ ମତି।
ରାବଣ ଯେ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ ତପ କଲା, ଛପନ ଗଣ୍ଡା ଯୁଗକୁ ବର ସେ ପାଇଲା।
ପାପେ ଗଲା ତାର ତିନି ଭାଗ ଯେ ବିନାଶ, ଏକଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସ୍ୱର୍ଗେ ପଶି ଅପସରୀ ମାନଙ୍କୁ ହରିଲା, ପର ଦ୍ରବ୍ୟ ହରି ଯେବେ ପାଦେ ଖଟାଇଲା।
ଆପେ ଜଗତମାତା ଯେ ଜନକ ନନ୍ଦିନୀ, ତାଙ୍କୁ ହରି ନେଲା ଗର୍ବେ ମୂଢ଼ ବିଂଶପାଣି।
ସୁରାପାନ କଲା ସେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିଙ୍କୁ ବଧିଲା, ବ୍ରହ୍ମହତ୍ୟା ଆଦିକରି କେତେ ପାପ କଲା।
ପାପରୁ ଯେ ତିନି ଭାଗ ହୋଇଲା ବିନାଶ, ଏକ ଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସେହିପରି କଳିଯୁଗ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ପାଞ୍ଚସସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅନ୍ୟ ପାପେ ଯିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Rāmadāsa bolai ho shuṇa gurudeba, kaliyuga kete puṇa bhoga je hoiba.
Chārilakṣha batisha je sahasra baraṣha, kemaṃte je yuga puṇa hoibaṭi sheṣha.
Eḍe baḍa yuga puṇa bināsha hoiba, saṃshaya pheriṇa mote kaha gurudeba.
Rāmadāsa bāṇī shuṇi Achyuta kahaṃti, tote kahuachi Rāma shuṇa dei mati.
Rābaṇa je tretayā yugare tapa kalā, chapana gaṇḍā yugaku bara se pāilā.
Pāpe galā tāra tini bhāga je vināsha, ekabhāga bhoga kalā Bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Swarge pashi apasarī mānaṃku harilā, para drabya hari jebe pāde khaṭāilā.
Āpe jagatamātā je Janaka naṃdinī, tāṃku hari nelā garve mūḍha biṃshapāṇi.
Surāpāna kalā se kṣhatriṃṃku badhilā, brahmahatyā ādi kari kete pāpa kalā.
Pāparu je tini bhāga hoilā bināsha, eka bhāga bhoga kalā bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Sehipari kaliyuga bināsha hoiba, pāṃchasasra bhoga heba anya pāpe jiba.

'Kalpatika' ah chuan, Achyutananda Das chuan Ravana entirna hmangin he thil thleng hi a hrilhfiah. Ravana chuan tanna nasa tak a lo nei a, "56 Ganda" (khawvel hun rei tak) damchhung hun a hlawhchhuak. Mahse, Nu Sita a rukbo avang te, van nungchate a tihmualpho avang te, Brahman-hatya ang chi sual rapthlak tak a tih avang tein, a malsawmna pakhat chauh a hmang hman a, a dang pathum chu a sualna avangin a boral ta a ni.

Chutiang bawkin, Kaliyuga kum 432,000 awh tur chu khawvel sualna nasa taka pung chak avangin "ei ral" mek a ni, chumi avang chuan kum 5,000 hnuah a hun hmain a tawp ang, Ananta Yuga emaw Satya Yuga lo thleng tur atan kawng a sial a ni.

4.6 Kaliyuga Kum: Chian taka Ngaihtuahna

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu pakhatah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗର ଅଟଇ ଆୟୁଷ।
ପାପଭାରରେ ଆୟୁ କଟିଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra,
Kaḷiyugara aṭai āyuṣa.
Pāpabhārare āyu kaṭijiba,
Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi bhoga hoiba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga kum chu nuai li sing thum sang hnih (432,000) a ni, mahse sual thuhnuaia awm avangin, he kum hi tih tawiin kum sang nga (5,000) chauhvin a tawp ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a piantharna pakhat, kum zana sawmpariatna (18th century) ah Odisha state-a Jajpur district-a piang, Mahapurush Hadi Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kali Chautisha’ tihah Kaliyuga kum chhung a sawi a. Heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ସଂବତ୍ସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗ ଆଦ୍ୟ ହୋଇବ ଶୁଭଯୋଗେ ପ୍ରବେଶ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Saṃbatsara pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hoiba sheṣa,
Satya yuga ādya hoiba shubhajoge prabesha.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, "Sambatsara Paancha Sahasra" (kum 5,000) ah chuan, Kaliyuga a tawp ang, chumi hnuah chuan, Adya Satya Yuga chu hun tha takah a lo intan ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan Malika lehkhabu dangah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ଯେ ହଜାର ଥାରୁ ମହାଭୟ ହେବ,
ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସମ୍ରାଟକୁ ଆସି ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଗାନ୍ଧାର ସେନାଟି ଗୋଳ କରିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର ପଚାଶ କଳିର ଆୟୁଷ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Pāñcha je hajāra thāru mahābhaya heba,
Dillī samrāṭaku āsi bipada paḍiba.
Gāndhāra senāṭi goḷa kariba abashya,
Pāñcha hajāra pachāsha Kaḷira āyuṣa.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan heta tang pawh hian Kaliyuga kum 5,000 hnuah chuan hlauhawmna nasa tak a awm ang; Delhi Lalber emaw ram Prime Minister chungah chhiatna a thleng ang. Gandhara sipaite (Taliban) chuan India an do ngei ang. Mahapurush chuan, heng zawng zawng hi Kaliyuga a tawp hnuah, a nih loh leh Kaliyuga kum 5,050 a liam hnuah a thleng ang, tiin a ziak bawk.

Achyutananda Das ji chuan a Malika lehkhabuah chang a ziak bawk a, chutah chuan Kaliyuga kum chu 5,000 emaw 5,050 emaw tiin a sawi lang tlang lo a, entirna hmangin a sawi zawk:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶେ ମସିହା ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କରିବେ କଳି ବିନାଶ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Biṃshe masiha hoiba sheṣa,
Ananta karibe Kaḷi bināsha.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, '20s' ah—kum 2000 atanga 2099 inkarah—he hun chhungah hian engtik hunah emaw Kaliyuga a tawp ang. Lalpa Ananta chuan leiah Kaliyuga a tichhia ang.

Panchasakha zinga pakhat, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Aagata Bhavishya Malika’ ah Kaliyuga kum chhung chu chian takin a hrilhfiah:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବୋଲଇ ଶୁଣିମା ଗୋସାଇଁ କୁହ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବିଚାର,
କେତେବେଳେ କଳ୍କୀ ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ଶୁଣଇଁ ମୁଖୁ ତୁମ୍ଭର।
ଶିଶୁ ବୋଲନ୍ତି ହେ ଶୁଣିମା ବାରଙ୍ଗ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ତୁହି,
ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଯେବେ ଯିବ ଭୋଗ ହୋଇ।
ଜେସେନେକେ ନିଶି ପାହିଲେ ପ୍ରଭାତ ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ଏହା ଜାଣ,
ଯେମନ୍ତ ସମୟେ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ସେ ନାରାୟଣ।
ସମକ୍ଷର ବତା ଶୁଣି ଆଣିକରି ପ୍ରମାଣ ଏହାକୁ କର,
ସବୁ ଏକଠାବେ ମିଶାଇ କରିଣ କରିବୁ ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର।
ଏହି ସମୟକୁ ତଳେ କରିଥିବୁ କହଇ ହୋ ବାବୁ ତୋତେ,
ଠିକ ରେ ଏ କଥା ଦେଖାଇ କହିଲୁ ରଖିଥିବୁ ହୃଦଗତେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bāraṅga bolai shuṇimā gosāiṃ kuha bhabiṣya bichāra,
ketebeḷe Kalkī swarūpa hoibe shuṇaiṃ mukhu tumbhara.
Shishu bolanti he shuṇimā Bāraṅga Kaḷaṅki swarūpa tuhi,
yugashandhi pāñcha sahasra baraṣa jebe jiba bhoga hoi.
Jeseneke nishi pāhile prabhāta yugashandhi ehā jāṇa,
jemanta samaye Kaḷaṅki swarūpa hoibe se Nārāyaṇa.
Samakṣara batā shuṇi āṇikari pramāṇa ehāku kara,
sabu ekaṭhābe mishāi kariṇa karibu pāñcha hajāra.
Ehi samayaku taḷe karithibu kahai ho bābu tote,
Ṭhika re e kathā dekhāi kahilu rakhithibu hṛudagate.

Awmzia:
Zirtir Baranga Das chuan Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das hnenah—"Aw Guru Dev, hun lo thleng tur min hrilh rawh. Lalpa chu engtikah nge a Kalki pianziaa a inlar dawn tih i ka atangin hriat ka duh."

Hei hi a hriatin, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das chuan a zirtir ropui Baranga hnenah 'Yuga-Sandhi' (hun inthlakthlengna) a thlen hunah chauh Lalpa chu a Kalki pianziaah a lo piang ang, tiin a hrilh. A hrilhfiahnaah chuan, he Yuga-Sandhi hi Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a liam hnuah a thleng ang, a ti. Zan hnuah zing a lo thlen ang chiahin, hun pakhat hnuah hun dang a lo thleng thin. Hun hnih inkar lai chu Yuga-Sandhi an ti. Tin, he Yuga-Sandhi ah ngei hian, Narayana chuan a Kalki pianzia a la ang. He Yuga-Sandhi hi Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a liam hnuah a lo thleng ang.

Achyutananda ji chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ ah Kaliyuga kum chhung a sawi langin, heti hian a ti:

Odia Sloka:

ଚହଟିବ ଲୀଳା ତୁ ଚାରିରେ ମିଶା ଏକ,
ଚଢ଼ା ତିନି ଶୂନ ତହିଁ ଯେତେ ହେଲା ଠିକ।
ଚଳିଯିବ ଘୋର କଳି ଦଳି ଦେବେ ମିଳି,
ଚେତାଇ ଗୀତ ଛନ୍ଦଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ସେ ଭାଳି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

chahaṭiba līḷā tu chārire mishā eka,
chaḍhā tini shūna tahiñ jete helā ṭhika.
Chalijiba ghora Kaḷi daḷi debe miḷi,
chetāi gīta chhandai Achyuta se bhāḷi.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, pali (4) ah pakhat (1) belh la (4+1=5) a, a belhkhawm bulah zero pathum dah rawh; a number lo chhuak chuan Kaliyuga hun rapthlak tak a tawp hun tur a kawk a ni. Chunga changah chuan, Mahapurush chuan Kaliyuga kum chhung chu entirna hmangin a sawi a ni. Ani chuan 4+1=5 a ti a, chumi hnuah 5 bulah zero pathum a dah chuan Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a liam hnuah chu hun chu a tawp ang tihna a ni.

Malika lehkhabu dang ‘Bhavishya Chautisha’ ah chuan, a zirtir Ramachandra hnenah heti hian a ti:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅମରପୁର,
ଠାକୁର ତହିଁରୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ, ଠାରି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଧର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ṭhikaṇā Amarapura,
Ṭhākura tahiñru hebe bāhāra, Rāmachandra he,
ṭhāri pāñcha sahasra dhara.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Aw zirtir Ramachandra, Lalpa Kalki piantharna chu Amarapur (khawvel hremhmun lo thleng ngai lo—Satya Yuga hma pawha awm tawh, a nih loh leh Jajpur) ah a thleng ang, tin, hei hi Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a liam hnuah a thleng ang.

Chumi hnuah, a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kali Bayalisha’ ah chuan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan Kaliyuga kum chhung chu entirna hmangin a sawi. Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ti:
A awmzia chu, Lal Dasharatha fapa neih zat chungah number 3 ziak rawh (a nih loh leh, 4^3 = 64). Mahapurush chuan chumi hnuah chu number chu letling turin a ti a, chumi number atang chuan 11 paih turin a ti (64 letling chuan 46 a ni a, 46 - 11 = 35). Mahapurush chuan he number lo chhuak dinglam number chu 'Gandiva' (a nih loh leh, 1000) in pun turin a ti a, Kaliyuga chu a number lo chhuak emaw kum 5,000 ah a tawp ang, tiin a ziak.
Hriattirna: Heta 'Gandiva' tih hian, Mahapurush chuan a kah theih chin, 1,000 Yojanas a ni, tih a kawk a ni.

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶରଥ ସୁତ ମୁଣ୍ଡରେ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କ ଚଢ଼ିବ,
ବଜ୍ର ଅଙ୍କ ଦେଇ ଏଗାର ଅଙ୍କ ତହୁଁ ଫେରିବ।
ଶେଷ ଡାହାଣ ଭାଗ ଯାହା ହେଲା ଗାଣ୍ଡିବ ଗୁଣ,
କଳି ଶେଷ ଭୋଗ ଏହିଟି କହିଲି ଅରଜୁନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dasharatha suta muṇḍare tini aṅka chaḍhiba,
bajra aṅka dei egāra aṅka tahuṃ pheriba.
Sheṣa ḍāhāṇa bhāga jāhā helā gāṇḍiba guṇa,
kaḷi sheṣa bhoga ehiṭi kahili Arajuna.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Lal Dasharatha-a fapa zat chungah 3 chu ziak rawh (i.e., 4^3 = 64). Chumi hnuah Mahapurush chuan chu number chu letlinga, a atanga 11 paih turin a ti (64 letling chuan 46 a ni a, 46 - 11 = 35). Mahapurush chuan he number hmuh chhuah tak dinglam number chu 'Gandiva' (i.e., 1000) nen pun turin a ti a, Kaliyuga chu he number hmuh chhuah takah hian a tawp ang, a nih loh leh kum 5,000-ah a tawp ang tiin a ziak a ni.
Hriat tur: Hetah hian, 'Gandiva' tih hian Mahapurush chuan a kah theih chin, Yojanas 1,000 a ni tih a sawina a ni.

Achyutananda Das ji chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Adi Sanhita’ ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ ଯେ ସୁବାହୁ ଯୁଗ କଳି,
କ୍ଷୀଣ ଆୟୁଷ ମହାବଳି।
ପାପେ ସକଳ କ୍ଷୟ ଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

E je subāhu yuga kaḷi,
kṣīṇa āyuṣa mahābaḷi.
pāpe sakaḷa kṣaya jiba,
pāñcha sahasra bhoga heba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga kum 432,000 awh tur chu sual avangin kum 5,000 chauhvin a tawi ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’ ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଭୋଗ ହେବ,
ସେହି ଦିନୁ ସତ୍ୟକଳି ପ୍ରକାଶ ପାଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷi pāñchasahasra baraṣa bhoga heba,
sehi dinu Satyakali prakāsha pāiba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga hun chu kum 5,000 a ni ang. Chumi hnuah, 'Satyakali'—Kaliyuga chhunga Adya Satya Yuga eng—chu a lo lang ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’ ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାରରେ ଗଙ୍ଗାଦେବୀ ମେଳ କଟକ ଠାରେ,
ଭକ୍ତ ଦେଖିବେ ନେତ୍ରରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷi pāñchahajārare Gaṅgādebī meḷa Kaṭaka ṭhāre,
bhakta dekhibe netrare.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a liam hnuah, Pathiannu Ganga chuan Cuttack a paltlang ang; a awmzia chu, Kolkata atanga Cuttack thlengin engkim a pil ang; tuilian rapthlak tak a thleng ang.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’ ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହଟେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ,
ମୋର ତଥ୍ୟମାନ ସେତେବେଳେଟି ହୋଇବ।
ପୂର୍ବ ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଆବର ଦକ୍ଷିଣ,
ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେବେ କଳଙ୍କି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hāte satya upujiba,
mora tathyamāna setebeḷeṭi hoiba.
pūrba pashchima uttara ābara dakṣiṇa,
prakāsha hebe Kaḷaṅki Achyuta bachana.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a liam hnuah, Kaliyuga a tawp ang a, Thutak (Satya) a lo thleng ang. Chumi hnuah, Lalpa Kalki eng chu kawlkil tinah—Chhak, Thlang, Hmar, leh Chhimah—a lo lang ang.

Saint Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’ ah heti hian a ziak nawn leh:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ସପ୍ତ ବିଂଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଆୟୁଷ ହେବ ବିନାଶ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରଟି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ,
ସତ୍ୟଯୁଗ ପୁଣି ଉଦୟା ହେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chāri lakṣa sapta biṃsha sahasra,
Kaḷiyuga āyuṣa heba bināsha.
pāñcha sahasraṭi bhoga hoiba,
Satyayuga puṇi udayā heba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga kum 432,000 awh tur atangin kum 427,000 chu tihboral a ni ang; kum 5,000 chauh hman a ni ang a, chumi hnuah Satya Yuga a lo thleng ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chauh ni lovin, Panchasakha zinga pakhat dang, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das pawhin a Malika lehkhabu hrang hrangah Kaliyuga kum chhung a sawi bawk. A Malika lehkhabu ‘Chumbaka Malika’ ah a zirtir Baranga hnenah a hrilhfiah laiin, heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଏବେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ଠିକ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବିଚାର ଚିତ୍ତରେ ଘେନ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ebe pāñcha ṭhika kahibā shuṇa,
Bāraṅga bichāra chittare ghena.
pāñcha sahasra jetebeḷe heba,
sampūrṇa līḷā prakāsha hoiba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, zirtir Baranga, ngun takin ngaithla rawh, number 5 hi vuan tlat rawh. Kum 5,000 a liam hnuah chauh Lalpa Leela (Pathian thil tih) famkim chu a lo lang ang.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chumbaka Malika’ ah heti hian a ziak nawn leh:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଗଲେ,
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଜନମ ଯେ ହୋଇବେ ଭଲେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷiyuga pāñcha sahasra gale,
Viṣṇu janama je hoibe bhale.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a liam hnuah, Lalpa Vishnu piantharna a thleng ang.

Kaliyuga chu kum sang nga a liam hnuah a tawp ang a, Lalpa thil tih (Leela) a thleng ang, tiin a hrilhfiahin, Mahapurush Balarama Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’ ah heti hian a ziak:
A awmzia chu, Hastinapur Cuttack lo ni thin ram chu tunlai Delhi khawpui a ni. Hlui thawnthu ngaithla rawh—Devaapi chu tannaah a inhmang mek. India ram Uttarakhand biala Kalapa khuaah tanna a nei mek a, chu chu Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a liam hnuah a zo ang. Tanna a zawh hnuah, Lalpa a hmu ang. Lalpa chuan a kut nem takin lallukhum a khumtir ang a, Hastinapur emaw Delhi Lalberah a siam ang.

Odia Sloka:

ହସ୍ତିନା କଟକ ଯେବଣ ଭୂଇଁ,
ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସହର ସେହି।
ପୂର୍ବର ଚରିତ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ଦେବାପି ଅଛନ୍ତି ତପସ୍ୟାରେଣ।
ଉତ୍ତର ଖଣ୍ଡେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରନ୍ତି,
ତପସ୍ୟା ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ହେ ହୋଇ ନାହାନ୍ତି।
ପଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରେ ବରଷ ପୁଣ,
ତପସ୍ୟା ତାଙ୍କର ହୋଇବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ।
ବାରୁଣା କୁଳେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଭେଟିବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଶ୍ରୀହସ୍ତେ ସିରୀପା ବାନ୍ଧିବେ।
ଶ୍ରୀପଦ୍ମ ହସ୍ତ ତା ମୁଣ୍ଡେ ଲଦିବେ,
ହସ୍ତିନା ରାଜ୍ୟ ନଗ୍ରରେ ସ୍ଥାପିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Hastinā kaṭaka jebaṇa bhūiṃ,
bartamāna Dillī sahara sehi.
Pūrbara charita kahibā shuṇa,
Debāpi achhanti tapasyāreṇa.
Uttara khaṇḍe tapasyā karanti,
tapasyā pūrṇa he hoi nāhānti.
Pañcha sahasre baraṣa puṇa,
tapasyā tāṅkara hoiba pūrṇa.
Bāruṇā kuḷe prabhuṅku bheṭibe,
Prabhu shrīhaste sirīpā bāndhibe.
Shrīpadma hasta tā muṇḍe ladibe,
Hastinā rājya nagrare sthāpibe.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Hastinapur Cuttack lo ni thin ram chu tunlai Delhi khawpui a ni. Hmanlai thawnthu ngaithla rawh—Devaapi chu tluklehdingawn a ti mek a ni. India rama Uttarakhand biala Kalapa khuaah tluklehdingawn a ti mek a, chu chu Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a ral hnuah a zo ang. Tluklehdingawn a zawh hnuah Lalpa a hmu ang. Lalpa chuan A kut zungtang parmawi hmangin a luah lallukhum a khumtir ang a, Hastinapur Lal, a nih loh leh Delhi Lal a siam ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika text ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’ tihah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଦାରୁ ଚଢ଼ି ଦାରୁରୂପ ତେଜିକରି କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହୋଇବ,
ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ ମୋହର ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳିରେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଘଟିବ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dāru chaḍhi dārurūpa tejikari Kalkī rūpa hoiba,
niṣṭhā bachana mohara Rāmachandra Kaḷire nishche ghaṭiba.
āhuri kathāe shuṇa Rāmadāsa Kaḷi pāñcha sahasrare.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a ral hnuah, Lalpa Jagannatha chuan A 'Daaru-rupa' emaw Jagannatha pianzia chu kalsanin Kalki pianzia chu a la ang.

Chung châng zawng zawng atang chuan, Kaliyuga hun hman tawh chu kum 5,000 chauh a ni tih a chiang a ni. Kum 432,000 emaw kum 10,000 emaw a ni lo.

Mahse, zawhna pawimawh tak pakhat a lo chhuak a ni— Kan hriat angin, Kaliyuga kum 5,000 chu a ral tawh a, tunah hian kum 5,127-na a ni mek a ni. He chhutna atang chuan, Kaliyuga chu tunah hian a tawp tawh tur a ni a, 'Adya Satya Yuga' chu a kal mek tur a ni! Mahse, Kaliyuga thuneihna chu hmun tinah a la lang reng a ni. Sualna leh fel lohna chu hmun tinah a awm a ni. Engvangin nge he inpersan hi a awm?

A chhanna chu hetiang hi a ni— Pakhatnaah, kum 5,000 hi thu tlangpui sawina a ni a, a dik chiah chiah lo. Kum 5,000 tih chu kum 5,000 vel tihna a ni. Hei vang hian hmun tam takah chuan kum 5,050 tih pawh a inziak a ni.

Pahnihnaah, Malika-in a sawi dan chuan, Kaliyuga hun chu a tawp tawh a, tunah hian Kaliyuga leh Satya Yuga inkar 'Sandhikala' (hun inthlakna) chu a kal mek a ni. Malika-in a sawi dan chuan, he Sandhikala hunah hian Lalpa Kalki pianna leh Amah avanga Dharma dinthar lehna—a nih chu, mi sualte tihboralna leh mi rinawmte chhandamna—chu a thleng ang. Hei vang hian Kaliyuga hun a tawp tawh chungin sualna leh fel lohna chu a sang ber a ni.
