Dharma dinthar lehna a zawh hunah chuan, Kaliyuga thuneihna chu a tawp vek ang a, Thutak, Dharma, leh Rorelinna chu dinthar leh a ni ang tih hriat tur a ni.

Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika text-ah te pawh Lalpa Kalki hruainaa Dharma dinthar lehna hi engtikah nge a thlen ang tih chungchang a ziak a ni.

4.7 Meena-Shani Inzawmkhawmna

Mahapurush chuan a text ‘Aagata Bhavishyat Malika’ tihah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲେ,
ଠ’ ତିନି ବାମେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ରଖିଲେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ,
ଠକିଯିବ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭଲେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ṭhikaṇā Achyuta bole,
ṭha’ tini bāme pāñcha rakhile Rāmachandra he,
ṭhakijiba mīna shani bhale.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, zero pathum leh a hmaah number 5 (5,000) i dah chuan, Kaliyuga kum 5,000 a ral hnuah, Meena-Shani (Saturn in Pisces) inzawmkhawmna a thleng ang, chu chu a pawimawh hle ang.

5 Meena-Shani Yoga; Malika-in a sawi danin

5.1 Meena-Shani chu eng nge ni?

Arsi chhutnaah chuan, Saturn (Shani) chu planet kal muang berah ngaih a ni, zodiac sign pakhatah kum 2.5 vel a cham thin. Zodiac cycle-ah hian zodiac sign 12 a awm a, Saturn chu pakhat theuha kum 2.5 vel a cham thin. Hei hian Saturn chu zodiac cycle pumpui kal chhuak turin kum 12 × 2.5 = 30 vel a mamawh tihna a ni.

Tunah Saturn chu zodiac sign-ah a lut ta se; kum 30 vel hnuah chuan a sign ngai bawkah chuan a kir leh thin. Meena-Shani tih chu Saturn-in Pisces (Meena) zodiac sign-a a kalna hun a ni.

Saturn-in Pisces a kal hnuhnun ber chu kum 1995 kha a ni. Saturn chu a sign ngai bawkah a kir leh turin kum 30 vel a mamawh avangin, 1995 + 30 = 2025. Chuvangin, kum 2025-ah chuan, Saturn chu Pisces-ah a kal leh ang. Kum 2025 hnuah chuan, a kal lehna hun chu 2025 + 30, a nih chu 2054–2055 a ni ang.

Panchasakha Mahapurushas-te chuan an Malika text-ah te chiang takin an sawi a ni, mi sualte tihboralna, mi rinawmte venhimna, leh Lalpa Kalki avanga Dharma dinthar lehna chu Meena-Shani hunah hian a thleng ber ang.

Hei hi hriatthiam zawk nan, Malika-a tarlan châng thenkhat leh an hrilhfiahna te i en ang u.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika text ‘Prachi Kalpa’ tihah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀମଣ୍ଡଳରେ ଚମକ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ନ ଜାଣିବେ ହୀନ ଜନ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀରେ ସଂହାର ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ର ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mahīmaṇḍaḷare chamaka paḍiba,
na jāṇibe hīna jana.
Mlechcha māruṇīre saṃhāra hoiba,
mīna shani ra pramāṇa.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, lei chungah hian au thawm a awm ang; hmun tinah buaina a awm ang. Mi rinawmte tih loh chu tumahin an hrethiam thei lovang. Heng thil thlengah hian, mi sual leh mi dik lote tam tak tihboral an ni ang. Mahapurush chuan heng thil thlengte hi Meena-Shani Yoga kum 2.5 chhungin a thleng ang a ti.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika text ‘Chakada Madana’ tihah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦା ପ୍ରମାଣ, ଗୁପ୍ତରେ ଲାଗିବ ସେ ମାୟା ରଣ।
ଥୋକେ ଯିବେ କାମଚଣ୍ଡୀ ମୁଖରେ, ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଗୁପ୍ତରେ।
ତରକ ତେରୁଆ ଯେତେ ଭକତ, ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦାରେ ନାଶ।
ଛିନ୍ନମସ୍ତା ଦେବୀ ଖର୍ପର ପାତି, କାଠମାଳିଆଙ୍କୁ ଖାଇବ ବାଛି।
ଘୋର ଅନ୍ଧକାର ସେବେଳେ ହେବ, ସବୁ ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଜ୍ଞାନ ହଜିଣ ଯିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ନ ମିଳିବ ଶସ୍ୟ ନିଅଣ୍ଟି, ମୀନ ଶନିଆସି ଦେବଟି ଘାଣ୍ଟି।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ଚୁଲି ସେହିଦିନ ଫିଟିବ, ହାଡ଼ିଆ କମାର ପରଣୁ ଥିବ।
ସେଦିନ ଯେ ଗୁରୁବାରଟି ଜାଣ, କାଳିଆ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଅଟେ ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Jobarā Kāḷiābodā pramāṇa,
guptare lāgiba se māyā raṇa.
Thoke jibe Kāmachaṇḍī mukhare, Mlechcha māruṇī je heba guptare.
Taraka teruā jete bhakata, Jobarā Kāḷiābodāre nāsha.
Chhinnamastā debī kharpara pāti, kāṭhamāḷiāṅku khāiba bāchi.
Ghora andhakāra sebeḷe heba, sabu buddhi jñāna hajiṇa jiba.
Anna na miḷiba shasya niaṇṭi, mīna shaniāsi debaṭi ghāṇṭi.
Lakṣe chuli sehidina phiṭiba, hāḍiā kamāra paraṇu thiba.
Sedina je gurubāraṭi jāṇa, Kāḷiā parīkṣā aṭe pramāṇa.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ziak a ni, Saturn chu Pisces-ah a awm hunah, chu hunah chuan, Cuttack khaw Jobra leh Kaliaboda-ah te mi vervek leh mi rinawm lote zawng zawng tan fiahna a thleng ang. Nu Pathian (Goddesses)-te chuan mi rinawm lote zawng zawng chu an tiboral ang. Chu hunah chuan, ni sarih leh zan sarih chhung thimna a awm bawk ang. Mi zawng zawng rilru chu a thawk thei tawh lovang. Chu hun chhung chuan, mi sualte chuan chaw pawh an hmu thei lovang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika text ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ tihah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଅଙ୍କ,
ଭାରତଶ୍ୱରୀ ହେବ ଉତପାତ।
ହେତୁ ରଖିଥାଅ ବ୍ରଜକୁମର,
ସେକାଳେ ତେଜିବି ମୁଁ କଳେବର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna shani heba Mukunda aṅka,
Bhāratashwarī heba utapāta.
Hetu rakhithāa Brajakumara,
sekāḷe tejibi muñ kaḷebara.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, Saturn chu Pisces-ah a awm hunah, India ramah buaina a awm ang. Chu hunah chuan, Lalpa Chungnungber chuan a mihring pianzia pangngai chu kalsanin Kalki pianzia chak tak chu a la ang.

Malika text dangah chuan, Mahapurush chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନିର ସଂଯୋଗ ହେବାର ସମୟ,
ପୃଥବୀରେ ହେବ ପୁଣି ଘୋର ଯେ ପ୍ରଳୟ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna shanira saṃyoga hebāra samaya,
pṛthabīre heba puṇi ghora je pralaya.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, Meena-Shani inzawmkhawmna hunah chuan, lei chungah hian tihboralna rapthlak tak (Pralaya) a thleng leh ang.

Achyutananda Das ji chuan a Malika text ‘Chakada Madana’ tihah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ୋଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧୃବ।
ଅଢ଼ୋଇ ବରଷ ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ ଛବିଶ ଅଙ୍କ କୁଅନା,
ଶ୍ମଶାନ ପରାୟ ଏ ସାରା ସଂସାର ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ସିନା।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna shani āsi deuthiba ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhōi heba,
dasha aṇā jiba chha' aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.
aḍhāi baraṣa ghora utpāta chhabbisha aṅka kuanā,
shmashāna parāya e sārā saṃsāra uchhanna hoiba sinā.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, Saturn-in Pisces a kal hunah, lei chungah, India telin, Indopui Pathumna a intan ang. Kum hnih leh a chanve chhung chu, lei chungah tihboralna rapthlak tak a lang ang. Khawvel pumpui chu tihboral a ni ang a, thlanmual ang a ni ang. Chu hun chhung chuan, mi tam tak an thi ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan Malika text dang ‘Bhabishyata Malika’ tihah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ସାବଧାନ ହୁଅ ତୁହି,
ଠା ଠିକଣା କିଛି ରହିବ ନାହିଁ।
ହେ ଭକ୍ତଗଣ! ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସିଗଲେ ଧାଇଁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Sābadhāna hua tuhi,
ṭhā ṭhikaṇā kichhi rahiba nāhiñ.
He bhaktagaṇa! Mīna shani āsigale dhāiñ.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, Aw mihringte u, fimkhur rawh u. Meena-Shani Yoga chu a lo thleng dawn a ni. Chu hunah chuan, chhiatna tam tak a lo thleng ang, leh tihboralna chuan mi zawng zawng deuhthaw a nghawng ang; tumah an him lovang. Mihringte rilru chu a thawk thei tawh lovang. Hmun tinah buaina a awm ang.

Kum zana 19-naa piang, hlabu ziaktu thianghlim Bhima Bhoi chuan he thil chungchang hi a Malika text ‘Padmakalpa’ tihah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଖିବ ଜଳ ଅବିଳମ୍ବେ,
ମୀନ ଶନିର ମଧ୍ୟଭାଗେ।
ମୋଗଲ ପଠାଣର ଧାଡ଼ି,
ମଧ୍ୟ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଥିବ ମାଡ଼ି।
ସିଂହ ମଙ୍ଗଳ ଗ୍ରହ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି,
କିଛି ମେଦିନୀ ଯିବ ଫାଟି।
ଗିରି କନ୍ଦର ଗୁମ୍ଫାମାନ,
ମୋ ଭକ୍ତେ ରହିବେଟି ପୁଣ।
ଆସିବେ ଚଣ୍ଡୀକାଇଗଣ,
ଭକ୍ଷିବେ ନଗ୍ରରେ ପଶିଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shukhiba jaḷa abiḷambe,
mīna shanira madhyabhāge.
Mogala paṭhāṇara dhāḍi,
madhya Oḍishā thiba māḍi.
Siṃha maṅgaḷa graha dṛṣṭi,
kichhi medinī jiba phāṭi.
Giri kandara gumphāmāna,
mo bhakte rahibeṭi puṇa.
Āsibe chaṇḍīkāigaṇa,
bhakṣibe nagrare pashiṇa.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, Meena-Shani Yoga laihawl hnuah, lui leh tui luangte chu reiloteah an kang ang. Hmelma ram sipaiho chuan Odisha an lut ang a, an indo ang. Chu Meena-Shani Yoga laihawl hunah bawk chuan, Mars chu Leo-ah a awm hunah, lirnghing a thleng bawk ang. Lalpa chuan a mi rinawmte chu tlang pukah fimkhur takin a humhim ang. Chandi leh Yoginis-te chuan mi sual leh mi dik lote chu an tiboral ang.

Hlabu ziaktu mi rinawm Narada Das chuan a Malika text-ah chuan Meena-Shani Yoga hun chhunga buaina thleng turte chu a sawi bawk a ni. A ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀଏ ନାଶ ଯିବ ରଙ୍କରେ।
ବତିଶ ତେତିଶ ପୁଣି ଚଉତିରିଶ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ।
ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ ଉଲ୍ଲୋଳ,
ଯୋଗିନୀ ହେବେ ପ୍ରବଳ।
ପଡ଼ି ହାଡ଼ କଙ୍କାଳ,
ଗଡ଼ିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ ମାଳ।
କମ୍ପିବ ଭୈରବ ଡାକରେ,
ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare,
prāṇīe nāsha jiba raṅkare.
Batisha tetisha puṇi chautirisha,
mīna shani bhoga heba aḍhāi baraṣa.
Pṛthabī heba ulloḷa,
yoginī hebe prabaḷa.
Paḍi hāḍa kaṅkāḷa,
gaḍiba muṇḍa māḷa.
Kampiba bhairaba ḍākare,
mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, Meena-Shani Yoga chu kum hnih leh a chanve chhung a awm ang leh he hun chhung hian, mi tam tak an thi ang. Lei chungah au thawm a awm ang. Yoginis-te chuan tihboralna divine acts chu an thlengtir ang. He hunah hian, ruangte chu lei chungah an darh ang. Lei chu Bhairavi au thawm avangin a nghing ang.

Dvapara Yuga hun chhunga Lalpa Krishna leh Nu Rukmini inbiakna chu tarlangin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika text ‘Rukmini Gita’ tihah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ମେଳ ଗୋ ହୋଇବ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ସେବେଳେ ସମର ହେବ ଭାରତ ମଣ୍ଡଳେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna shani meḷa go hoiba jetebeḷe,
sebeḷe samara heba Bhārata maṇḍaḷe.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, Saturn-in Pisces a kal hunah, India ramah indona rapthlak tak a thleng ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ପଡ଼ିଲେଟି ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ମନେ ରଖିଥିବୁ ରାମ ତୁହି ବ୍ରଜସୁତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna shani paḍileṭi ghora utpāta,
mane rakhithibu Rāma tuhi Brajasuta.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, Meena-Shani Yoga a thlen hunah, he lei chungah hian buaina rapthlak tak a lang ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan ‘Chakada Madana’ tia hming vuah Malika text-ah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ତେରଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ ରାଜନ,
ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଗଗନ।
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ କଷଣ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ଚଉଦ୍ୱାରେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ।
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନିକ ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଚଳ,
ନିକଟରେ ଗୋଳ ଚହଳ।
ତପ ସଙ୍ଗେ ତୋପ ପରୀକ୍ଷା,
ସତ୍ୟ ଅନନ୍ତଟି ଦେଖା।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ହୋଇବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Teradina pakṣa hoiba,
mīna shani yoga paḍiba.
Aḍhāi baraṣa rājana,
dibasare tārā gagana.
Bhaktaṅku kaṣaṇa paḍiba,
Chaudwāre yuddha hoiba.
Baijñānika yantra achaḷa,
nikaṭare goḷa chahaḷa.
Tapa saṅge topa parīkṣā,
satya Anantaṭi dekhā.
Ananta yugaṭi hoiba,
Achyutī kāmanā pūriba.

Awmzia:
A nih chu, Kaliyuga tawpah, Dharma dinthar lehna hunah, ni sawm pathum chhung Paksha (fortnight) a thleng ang. Meena-Shani Yoga chu kum hnih leh a chanve chhung a awm ang. Chu hun chhung chuan, chhunah arsi a lang ang. Chu hun chhung chuan, mi rinawmte chuan harsatna tam tak an tawk ang. Cuttack khaw Choudwar-ah indona a thleng ang; science leh electric hmanruate chu an thawk thei tawh lovang. Indona rapthlak tak a thleng ang. Hmelma ramte missile leh silai liante chu India ram mi thianghlim leh hruaitute tlawmngaihna (Tapa) thiltihtheihna chuan a do let ang. Lalpa Kalki chu a lo lang ang; mi rinawmte chuan Lalpa hmuhna (Darshan) chu an dawng ang. Kum Tawp Nei Lo (Ananta Yuga) chu a lo thleng ang. Achyutananda duhna chu a thleng famkim ang.

A Malika text ‘Chakada Madana’ tihah chuan a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଆକାଶୁ ଖସିବ ମୀନ ଶନିର ରାଜତ୍ୱ,
ବାଡ଼ି ବେମାରିରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dibasare tārā ākāśu khasiba mīna shanira rājatva,
bāḍi bemārire aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, chhunah van atangin arsi a tla ang; natna leh hripui avangin mi tam tak an thi ang. Chumi hunah chuan, Meena-Shani Yoga a kal mek ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି କରିବ ରାଜତ୍ୱ ମାନବ ମାରୁଣୀ ହେବ,
ପରାଧୀନ ହେବ ଏ ଭାରତବର୍ଷ ଶାସନ କଳ ଲାଗିବ।
ଅରାଜକ ହେବ ଏହି ଜମ୍ବୁଦ୍ବୀପ ଭକ୍ତ ହୋଇବେ ଛାନିଆଁ,
ଅନହୁତି ଅଗ୍ନି ପ୍ରଜ୍ୱଳିତ ହେବ ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ସାରା ଦୁନିଆ।
ଛିନିଛତ୍ର ହେବ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳ ଥୟ ନ ପଡ଼ିବେ କେହି,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କୁହଇ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna shani āsi kariba rājatwa mānaba māruṇī heba,
parādhīna heba e Bhāratabarṣa shāsana kaḷa lāgiba.
Arājaka heba ehi Jambudwīpa bhakta hoibe chhāniāñ,
anahuti agni prajwaḷita heba uchhanna sārā duniā.
Chhinichhatra heba Oḍishā maṇḍaḷa thaya na paḍibe kehi,
Chakaḍā maḍāṇa prakāsha kariṇa dīna Achyuta kuhai.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Saturn Pisces ah a luh hunah, mihringte tana boralna hun a intan ang. Democracy (ram rorelna) a chhe ang, India chuan a zalenna a hloh ang—mi dang rorelna hnuaiah a awm thei hial ang. Rorelna kawngah buaina nasa tak a thleng ang, ram chu buaina leh remruatna awm lohnaah a pil ang. Pathian mi (devotees) te chuan heng thil thlengte an hmuhin an hlau hle ang. Boralna mei chuan Odisha leh India chauh ni lovin, khawvel pumpui a fan chhuak ang.

Achyutananda ji chuan a Kalikalpa Gita’ ah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକୁ ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଗମନ ହେଲେ ଏମନ୍ତ ଭିଆଣ,
ଭାରା ନିବାରଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଦେବରାୟ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେ ଉହ୍ଲେଇବେ ଜାଣ।
କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna rāshiku Shanishchara gamana hele emanta bhiāṇa,
bhārā nibāraṇe prabhu debarāya martye uhleibe jāṇa.
Kalkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣatrīmānaṅku nikṣatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, lei hi sual lak ata chhan chhuak turin, Lal Mahavishnu chuan lei ah hian tisaah a lo chang ang. Meena-Shani Yoga hun chhungin, Kalki hmelpuin, Lalber chuan ram chhungah a zin kual ang a, mi sualte leh thil sual titute tihboralin, Lei Nu chu a chhan chhuak ang.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଭାବ ବିନୋଦିଆ ମୂର୍ତ୍ତିଙ୍କୁ ଭାବ,
ଭାବରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ ଚୁମ୍ବିବ।
ବାଇଆ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଗୀର,
ବାରହାତ ଖଣ୍ଡା ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବିରାଟ ଗାଦୀ ସଂହାର।
ଭକତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ,
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହୋଇ ପୃଥବୀ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ ବିଶେଷ।
ମାୟାରେ ବୁଲେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଣାକାର ହୃଦରେ ଘୋଷ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ଦୃଶ୍ୟ।
ଯୋବରା ଘାଟର ଲୀଳା,
ଜଗିଛି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ କରି ହେଳା,
କଟିବ ପାପୀଙ୍କ ଗଳା।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
bhāba binodiā mūrttiṅku bhāba,
bhābare Achyutī dāsa chumbiba.
Bāiā Achyutī gīra,
bārahāta khaṇḍā heba bāhara,
birāṭa gādī saṃhāra.
Bhakata Achyutī dāsa,
bhūmikampa hoi pṛthabī ushwāsa,
bhakta rahibe bisheṣa.
Māyāre bule Achyuta,
mantra aṇākāra hṛdare ghoṣa,
mīna shani heba dṛshya.
Jobarā ghāṭara līḷā,
jagichhi Achyuta na kari heḷā,
kaṭiba pāpīṅka gaḷā.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Mahabharata indona la awm chu a thleng ang. Cuttack khawpui Virat Gadi emaw Choudwar ah emaw indona rapthlak tak a thleng ang a, kut sawm leh pahnih sei khandaih chuan mi sualte a that ang. Lirnghing nasa tak a thleng ang a, chu chuan mi sualte a tiboral ang. Cuttack khawpui Jobra Ghat ah chuan mi vervêk zawng zawngte chu fiahna an tawk ang a, Pathian Nu chuan an thisen a in ang. Chumi hunah chuan, Pathian mi te chu venhim an ni ang. Mahapurush chuan heng thil thlengte hi Meena-Shani Yoga ah a thleng ang a ti.

Achyutananda Das ji chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Tattvabodhini’ ah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକି ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଯିବ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ନଷ୍ଟ ଭ୍ରଷ୍ଟ କରିଦେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna rāshiki Shanishchara jiba,
Oḍishā naṣṭa bhraṣṭa karideba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Saturn Pisces ah a luh hnuah, Odisha state chu tihchhiat a ni ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ଦେଶକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହେବ,
ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ସେଠାବରେ ହେବ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ମନେ ଭାବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna shani yoga deshaku paḍiba tera dina pakṣa heba,
gupata māruṇī seṭhābare heba Rāmachandra mane bhāba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Meena-Shani Yoga a thleng ang a, ni sawm pathum chhung thla chanve a awm ang. Chumi hunah chuan, mi tam tak rûk rûkin an thi ang a, Dharma chu din a ni ang.

‘Kalikalpa Gita’ ah chuan a sawi belh a ni, a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଚଳିଯିବ କଳି ଗୋଳ ଉପୁଜିବ ବାବୁ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହୋଇଥିବ ଗୁପତ ରଖିବୁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chaḷi jiba kaḷi goḷa upujiba bābu,
mīna shani hoithiba gupata rakhibu.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Meena-Shani Yoga hun chhungin, Kaliyuga a tawp ang a, Dharma chu din a ni ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଛାରଖାର ହେବ ଏ ସୁନା ସଂସାର ଭଇରବୀ ଦେବ ଡାକ,
ଅକାଳ ମରଣ ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ମୀନ ଶନିଯୋଗ ହେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chārakhāra heba e sunā saṃsāra bhairabī deba ḍāka,
akāḷa maraṇa gupata māruṇī mīna shaniyoga heba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, he khawvel hi "tichhe" vek emaw, tihboral vek emaw a ni ang. Nu Bhairavi chuan rûm nasa tak a chhuah ang; mi sual tam tak chu an hun lo takin an thi ang a, mi tam tak rûk rûkin an thi ang. Heng tihboralna thil thleng zawng zawngte hi Meena-Shani Yoga hun chhungin a thleng ang, a awmzia chu, Saturn chu Pisces zodiac sign ah a awm hunah a ni.

A chunga Malika chângte atang hian, Meena-Shani Yoga hun chhungin, Lal Kalki-a tihboralna Leela (Pathian thiltih) te chu a thawk nasa ber ang tih a chiang a ni. He hun chhung hian, khawvel pumpui chuan thil rapthlak leh la thleng ngai lo, nasa tak tak a hmu ang. Hmun tinah au thawm, indona, buaina, inthlak danglamna, leh chhiatna thlengte chauh hmuh tur a awm ang. He hunah hian, mi sualte leh fel lote chu khawvel pumpui atangin tihboral an ni ang.

5.2 Meena-Shani eng hunah nge Lal Kalki-a Leela te chu a thlen ang?

A chunga chângte atanga Kalki tihboralna Leela te chu Meena-Shani hun chhungin a thleng ang tih hriat tawh angin, zawhna lian ber a lo chhuak a ni: heng thil thlengte hi Meena-Shani hun eng berah nge a thlen ang?

Meena-Shani Yoga hi kum 30 danah vawi khat a thleng thin. Chuvangin, Malika sawi danin, Dharma dinna thil thlengte chu Meena-Shani eng hunah nge a thlen ang tih hriat a pawimawh a ni.

Ngawih ngawih a kan en chuan, kum 1995 ah Saturn chu Pisces ah a awm; tunah, kum 2025 ah, Saturn chu Pisces ah a lut leh tawh; chumi hnuah kum 2054 ah, leh chumi hnu kum 30 velah, kum 2084 ah, Saturn chu Pisces ah a lut leh ang.

Meena-Shani eng ber nge?

Bhavishya Malika ah chuan chiang takin a inziak a ni, heng thil thlengte hi Meena-Shani kum hnih leh a chanve chhungin a thlen hunah, chumi hunah chuan Puri, Odisha Lal hming chu Divyasingha Deva a ni ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chandra Kalpa’ ah chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଥିବ ସେହି ବେଳର,
ପୃଥବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ଘନ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତର ଏ ପ୍ରଳୟ ରାଶି,
ଭୂଭାରାକୁ ରାମ ଯିବ ରେ ଗ୍ରାସୀ।
ସେକାଳେ ମୀନେ ଶନି ଥିବ ଚଲି,
ନାଶିବ କାଳ ହେବ ଜଳ ଖେଳି।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ଗୁରୁ ସ୍ମରିଣ,
ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳ୍ପରେ ମୁଁ କଲି ବଖାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dibyasiṃha thiba sehi beḷara,
pṛthabī hoijiba ghana andhāra.
pañchabhūtara e pralaya rāśi,
bhūbhārāku rāma jiba re grāsī.
sekāḷe mīne śani thiba chali,
nāśiba kāḷa heba jaḷa kheḷi.
Achyuta kahai guru smariṇa,
chandra kalpare muñ kali bakhāṇa.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Saturn chu Pisces ah a awm hunah, chumi hunah chuan, ni 7 leh zan 7 chhungin lei hi thimna thuk takin a khuh ang. Thil panga (Lei, Tui, Mei, Boruak, leh Van) avanga boralna chuan khawvel pumpui a ei zo ang. Mahapurush chuan chumi hunah chuan Puri Lal hming chu ‘Divyasingha’ a ni ang a ti.

Tunah hian, thil pawimawh thenkhat hriat tur a awm. Puri Lal tunlai, Shri Divyasingha Deva (IV) chu kum 72 vel a upa a ni. Meena-Shani lo thleng leh turah—a awmzia chu, tun atanga kum 30 hnuah—a kum chu 100+ a ni tawh ang a, chumi hunah chuan Puri lalthutthleng a la luah reng ang tih chu a theih loh hial ang!

Tunah hian mi thenkhat, thil thleng hun chu Meena-Shani lo thleng leh tur (a awmzia chu, kum 2054–2057) ah a ni ang titute chuan, heng thil thlengte hi Divyasingha hming pu lal dang rorel laiin a thleng thei ang an ti! Mahse, hei hi a theih loh tih ka hrilh duh che u a ni. Puri Lal tunlai chu Lal 19-na a ni a, Malika sawi danin, Lal 19-na chu Kaliyuga Lal hnuhnung ber a ni ang. A awmzia chu, Malika sawi danin, Lal Shri Divyasingha Deva tunlai hnuah chuan, Puri Lal dang an awm tawh lo ang.

A chunga sawina atang hian a finfiah a ni, thil rapthlak thleng zawng zawngte hi kum 2025–2028 inkar Meena-Shani Yoga hun chhungin, Shri Divyasingha Deva IV rorel laiin a thleng ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’ ah chuan a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ବହୁତ ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ପାପର ଭାରା,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ହୋଇବେ ଧରା।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରେ ପାଣି ପଶିବ,
ସିଂହ ଦ୍ୱାରେ ରକ୍ତଧାର ବହିବ।
ଟିକରା ଗୋହିରୀ ହୋଇବ ରଡ଼ି,
ଧାନ ଥିବ ଯିବ କଣ୍ଠା ଅଗାଡ଼ି।
ସେବେଳକୁ ଏକା ଅକ୍ଷର ସାର,
ସପତ ଦିନ ଯେ ହେବ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ଗାଲୁଆ ମାଲୁଆ ଭକତ ଛନ୍ତି,
ବଗୁଲିଆ କର୍ମେ ପେଟ ପୋଷନ୍ତି।
ଏମାନେ ଯେ ଦେବୀ ଖପରରେ ଯିବେ,
ଭକତମାନେ ଆନନ୍ଦରେ ରହିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bahuta anyāya pāpara bhārā,
Dibyasiṃha deba hoibe dharā.
Baḍa deuḷare pāṇi pashiba,
siṃhadvāre raktadhāra bahiba.
ṭikarā gohirī hoiba raḍi,
dhāna thiba jiba kaṇṭhā agāḍi.
sebeḷaku ekā akṣara sāra,
sapata dina je heba andhāra.
gāluā māluā bhakata chanti,
baguliā karme peṭa poṣanti.
emāne je debī khaparare jibe,
bhaktamāne ānandare rahibe.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga tawp lamah, fel lohna nasa tak leh sual rit tak a awm ang. Chumi hunah chuan, tuipui tui chu Jagannath Temple ah a lut ang. Temple chhungah indona a chhuak ang. Singhadwara (Sakeibawm Kawngka) ah chuan thisen luang a awm ang. Chumi hunah chuan, ni sarih leh zan sarih chhungin khawvel pumpui chu thimna in a khuh ang. Pathian mi vervêk zawng zawng, thil sual ti a eizawngte chu Pathian Nu (Divine Mothers) te chuan an tiboral ang. Pathian mi te chuan hlim takin an hun an hmang ang. Chumi hunah chuan, Puri Lal chu Shri Divyasingha Deva a ni ang a, chu chu hnehchhiahna hmangin man emaw, rûkbo emaw a ni ang.

A chunga châng atang hian a chiang hle a ni, Dharma dinna thil thlengte a thlen hunah, leh ni sarih leh zan sarih chhungin lei ah thimna a awm hunah, chumi hunah chuan Puri Lal chu Shri Divyasingha Deva a ni ang.

Odisha atanga Mahapurush dang, kum zabi 20-naa piang chuan a lehkhabu pakhatah a ziak a ni, kum 2033 hnuah chuan, hun thar (Satyayuga) eng a lo chhuak ang; a awmzia chu, Mahapurush sawi danin, hun inthlak danglamna Leela zawng zawngte chu kum 2033 hmain a zo vek ang. Hei hi Chapter 12-ah chângte nen kan hrilhfiah ang che u. A pawimawh ber chu, thil rapthlak thleng zawng zawngte hi kum 2033 hmain a thleng vek ang.

Mahapurush Bhima Bhoi (Sambalpur, Odisha ah kum zabi 19-naa piang, Pathian mi (devotee) hla phuah thiam mitdel) chuan he thil chungchang hi a Malika lehkhabu ‘Padma Kalpa’ ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ନିଶ୍ଚୟ କଳଙ୍କି ହୋଇବ, ଯେ ଦିନ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ।
ପଞ୍ଚଗ୍ରହ ଯେ ଯୁକ୍ତ ହେବେ, ଆଗାମୀ ଅଢ଼ାଇର ଯୋଗେ।
ତଦନ୍ତେ ଷଡ଼ ଗ୍ରହ ଆସି, ଶନି କୁମ୍ଭରୁ ଯିବେ ଖସି।
ପ୍ରବେଶ ମୀନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ, ଟାକିଥାଅ ହେ ସୁଜ୍ଞ ନରେ।
ହୋଇବ ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ ସମର, ଦମନ ହୋଇବ ବାହାର।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ନାଶିବ ଅବହେଳେ, ଜଗତେ ରଖି ବାହୁବଳେ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛଙ୍କୁ ଦମନ ନାଶିବ, ଦୁର୍ଗା ଖପର ପକାଇବ।
ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ କଳି ସେ ଦମନ, ଶୁଣ ହେ ସାଧୁ ସୁଜ୍ଞଜନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Nishchaya Kaḷaṅki hoiba, je dina mīna shani bhoga.
Pañchagraha je yukta hebe, āgāmī aḍhāira joge.
Tadante ṣaḍa graha āsi, shani kumbharu jibe khasi.
Prabesha mīna saṅgatare, ṭākithāa he sujña nare.
Hoiba asaṃkhya samara, damana hoiba bāhāra.
Biṣṇu nāshiba abaheḷe, jagate rakhi bāhubaḷe.
Mlechchaṅku damana nāshiba, Durgā khapara pakāiba.
Asaṃkhya kaḷi se damana, shuṇa he sādhu sujñajana.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Saturn chu Pisces ah a luh hunah, Lal Kalki-a tihboralna Leela te chu a thleng ang. He hun hi kum hnih leh a chanve a awh ang. He hun chhung hian, ram hrang hrangte inkarah indona tam tak a thleng ang. Lal Kalki-a chuan Pathian mi te chu a veng him ang a, Nu Durga chuan mi sualte leh thil sual titute a tiboral ang.

A chunga chângah chuan, Mahapurush chuan chiang takin a sawi a ni, Kalki tihboralna Leela te chu Meena-Shani eng hunah nge a thlen ang tih. Chumi Meena-Shani hriatna tur chu hrilhfiahin, a sawi a ni, Saturn chu Pisces ah a luh ni chuan, planet 5 chu Pisces ah an awm tawh ang. A awmzia chu, chumi ni chuan, Pisces ah chuan planet 6 an awm vek ang—planet 5 awm sa leh Saturn pakhat, a vaiin planet 6.

Kum 2025 Meena-Shani kan en chuan, Mahapurush sawi chu a dik chiah tih kan hmu a ni. March 29, 2025 ah, Saturn chu Pisces ah a luh ni chuan, planet panga (Rahu, Venus, Mercury, Sun, leh Moon) chu Pisces ah an awm tawh a ni.

Tunah chuan, kum 2054 Meena-Shani kan en chuan, May 14, 2054 ah, Saturn chu Pisces ah a luh ni chuan, planet 2 chauh (Venus leh Mars) chu Pisces ah an awm hmasa ang tih kan hmu a ni a, leh Saturn nen chuan, planet 3 a ni ang. Mahse, Saint Bhima Bhoi sawi danin, Pisces ah chuan planet 6 an awm tur a ni a, 3 ni lovin.

Kum 2084 Meena-Shani Yoga kan en chuan, March 20, 2084 ah, Saturn chu Pisces ah a luh ni chuan, planet 2 chauh (Mercury leh Sun) chu Pisces ah an awm hmasa ang tih kan hmu a ni. Saturn telh pawhin, planet 3 a ni. Hei hian a awmzia chu, planet 6 inthlak danglamna hi hetah pawh a thleng lo a ni.

Mahapurush chuan kum 2054 emaw 2084 emaw Meena-Shani Yoga chungchang a sawi lo tih a chiang a ni. A Malika lehkhabu ‘Padma Kalpa’ ah a sawi Meena-Shani chu kum 2025 Meena-Shani ngei kha a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ah a sawi bawk a ni, heng tihboralna Leela te a thlen hunah, Cuttack, Nemalo a awm a Padmaban Peeth Mahant (Hotu) chu tunge ni ang tih. A ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳରେ,
ନବ୍ୟକ୍ତ କଥା କହିଲି ରାମ ହେତୁ ଚିତ୍ତରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta kaḷi śeṣa beḷare,
Nabyakta kathā kahili rāma hetu chittare.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Aw zirtir Ramadas, Kaliyuga a tawp hunah, chumi hunah chuan Gaganānanda hming pu mi pakhat chu ka Peeth Hotu a ni ang.

Mahapurush chuan Malika lehkhabu dangah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ ଗଗନେ ଉଡ଼ିବ ଧୂଳି,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶ ଯେ ଚମକି ପଡ଼ିବ ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ହାଲହୁଳି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta hoiba gagane uḍiba dhūḷi,
Oḍishā desha je chamaki paḍiba paḍijiba hālahuḷi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahant Gaganānanda hunah Pathian thiltih ropuite chu a thleng ang. Chu mi hunah chuan, Odisha ramah au thawm nasa tak a awm ang.

5.3 Mahant Gaganānanda Goswami

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das thlah 14-na, a thlah leh tunah kum 85-90 mi Shri Gaganānanda Goswamiji chu Mahapurush-a thlarau lam hmun thianghlim, Padmaban, Nemalo-a hotu a ni tih hi hriat reng tlak a ni. Hei hian Mahapurush chuan tun hun chanchin a sawi a ni tih a ti chiang bawk.

Chung point pali atang chuan, Malika-in a sawi danin, Kalki-a tihchhiatna Pathian thiltih ropuite a thlenna Meena-Shani chu kum 2033 hmain a lo thleng ang a, chu Meena-Shani hun chhung chuan, Shri Divyasingha Deva chu Puri Lal a ni ang a, Shri Gaganānanda chu Padmaban Peeth, Nemalo-a Mahant a ni ang. Hei chauh hi a ni lo, Saturn chuan Pisces a luh ni chuan, Shad-graha (planet 6) Yoga chu Pisces-ah a insiam ang.

Mahapurush chuan kum 2025-2028 inkarah lo thleng tur Meena-Shani chu a kawk a ni tih a chiang, chung point pali zawng zawng chu he Meena-Shani Yoga-ah chauh hian a inmil a, dangah a inmil loh avangin.

Sawi ngai lohvin, Malika-in a sawi danin, Lalpa Kalki-a tih tur thil chhiatna ropui tak takte chu kum 2025-2028 inkarah a thleng ang.

Hriattirna: Tunlai hian mi thenkhatin Malika-a sawi Meena-Shani chu tun Meena-Shani a ni lo, kum 2054-a lo thleng tur zawk a ni! an ti. Malika ringtu Pathian mi zawng zawng tan chuan hei hian an rilru a tihbuai loh nan a tha ang, chutiang sawite chu a dik lo hrim hrim avangin.

6 Kaliyuga tawpa Puri Lal

Bung hmasaah khan, Dharma dinna thil pawimawh ber berte chu Puri Lal, Shri Divyasingha Deva-a rorel hun chhungin a thleng ang tih kan hmu a. He bungah hian, Malika bung dang thenkhat atangin kan hrethiam ang.

Panchasakhas zinga mi, Mahapurush Jagannath Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kali Malika’-ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ପୁରୁଷୋତ୍ତମ ଦେବ ରାଜା ଠାରୁ,
ଉନ୍ନବିଂଶ ରାଜା ହେବ ସେଠାରୁ।
ସେଠାରୁ ପୁଣ ରାଜା ନାହିଁ ଆଉ,
ଅକୁଳି ହୋଇବେ କୁଳକୁ ବହୁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Puruṣottama Deba rājā ṭhāru,
unnabiṃsha rājā heba seṭhāru.
Seṭhāru puṇa rājā nāhiṃ āu,
akuḷi hoibe kuḷaku bahu.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Lal Indradyumna hnuah Lal Purushottama Deva atanga chhoin, lal 19 an awm ang a, hun hrang hrangah Jagannath Puri Lal an ni ang. Mahapurush chuan Lal 19-na chu fapa nei lovin a awm ang a, ani chu lal hnuhnung ber a ni ang; chu chu, a hnuah tumah lal an ni tawh lo ang tih a ziak a ni.

Tun dinhmun kan en chuan, tunlai Puri Lal chu Lal 19-na a ni a, fapa nei lo bawk a ni tih kan hmu. Mahapurush-a entirna chiang tak chu Dharma dinna Kalki-a Pathian thiltih ropuite zawng zawng chu tunlai lal rorel hun chhungin a thleng ang tih hi a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Adi Samhita’-ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନାମେ ରାୟେ,
ଏ ଯୁଗ ପ୍ରାନ୍ତେ ସେ ଉଦୟେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shri Dibyasiṃha nāme rāye,
e juga prānte se udaye.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga tawpah, Shri Divyasingha chu Puri Lal a ni ang.

Malika lehkhabu dangah chuan, a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଅଙ୍କେ ବାବୁ ସରବ ଦେଖିବୁ,
ଛାଡ଼ି ଚକା ଗଲୁ ବୋଲି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣିବୁ,
ନର ବାଳୁତ ରୂପରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମିବୁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dibyasiṃha aṅke bābu saraba dekhibu,
chhāḍi chakā galu boli nishchaya jānibu,
nara bāḷuta rūpare āmbhe janamibu.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Puri-ah Lal Shri Divyasingha Deva-a rorel hun a kal mek laiin, chu mi hunah chuan Lalpa Kalki-a Pathian thiltih ropuite zawng zawng chu a thleng ang. Chu mi hunah chuan, Lalpa chuan a Jagannath Temple chu a chhuahsan ang a, mihring taksaah Kalki Avatar angin a piang ang.

Chutichuan Malika lehkhabu ‘Kalikalpa Gita’-ah, Achyutanandaji chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ବୃଦ୍ଧ ବାଳକ ରାଜା ହୋଇଥିବ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସଣ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବ।
ବାଳବୃଦ୍ଧ ଅଙ୍କେ ବତାସ ହେବ,
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ।
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୀନମାନ ବହୁ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bruddha bāḷaka rājā hoithiba,
gupatare saṇa sāla chāliba.
bāḷabruddha aṅke batāsha heba,
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba.
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba,
pṛthibī hīnamāna bahu hoiba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, “Baalak-Briddha” chu Puri Lal a ni ang; chu chu, naupan laiin lal thutthleng luah a, tar thlengin chu dinhmunah chuan a awm tur mi chu lal a ni ang. A rorel hun chhungin, tui chhiatna leh thli chhiatna ang chi thil chhiatna a thleng ang.

Hriat a ni, tun thlenga lal lo ni tawhte zingah, Shri Divyasingha Deva tih loh chu tumah naupan lai atanga tar thlenga lal an ni lo. Tunlai Lal Shri Divyasingha Deva chuan July 7, 1970-ah, kum 17 mi chauh a nih laiin lal thutthleng a luah a. He dinhmunah hian a tar tawh a, tunah kum 72 vel a ni tawh. He bung hmang hian, entirna tawngkam hmangin, Divyasingha Deva chu Kaliyuga tawpa lal tur a ni tih Mahapurush chuan a puang chhuak a ni.

Achyutananda Dasji chuan a ‘Kalikalpa Gita’-ah a ziak belhchhah a:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ଅଙ୍କରେ ଜାଣ,
ଧୂମକେତୁ ହେବ ପୂର୍ବଭାଗେଣ।
ଉଲ୍‌କାପାତ ହେବ ହାଲହୁଳି,
ଶ୍ୱାନ ଶୃଗାଳ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ବୋବାଳି।
ମାତିବେ କାଳୀ ଯେ କରାଳୀଗଣ,
ଲୋକେ ମରିବେ ହୋଇ ରଣଭଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dibyasiṃha Deba aṅkare jāṇa,
dhūmaketu heba pūrbabhāgeṇa.
ulkāpāta heba hālahuḷi,
shwāna shṛgāḷa chhāḍibe bobāḷi.
mātibe kāḷī je karāḷīgaṇa,
loke maribe hoi raṇabhaṇa.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Shri Divyasingha Deva chu Puri Lal a nih hunah, chu mi hun chhung chuan chhak lamah arsi mei nei a lang ang. Lei chungah arsi tla a tam ang. Uite leh sakei ang chi sa ei hmang rannungte chuan ngaw an chhuahsan ang a, chenna hmun lam an pan ang. Nu Kali leh a thiante Yoginis-te chuan lei chunga mi sualte an tiboral ang a, mi tam tak an thi ang.

Mahapurush chuan chung bung hmang chuan, Kaliyuga tawpah, Puri Lal, Shri Divyasingha Deva-a rorel hun chhungin Dharma dinna thil thlengte chu a thleng ang tih tlemtein a hrilh a ni.

Mahapurush chuan a zirtir Ramadas hnenah Malika lehkhabu dangah a sawi a ni:

Odia Sloka:

କେଉଁ କାଳ ବୋଲି ବୋଇଲୁ ଶୁଣ ରାମ ରତନ,
ଥିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ରାଜନ କୋଳେ କନ୍ୟା ରତନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Keuñ kāḷa boli boilu śuṇa Rāma ratana,
thiba Dibyasiṃha rājana koḷe kanyā ratana.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Aw zirtir Ramadas, eng hun chanchin nge ka sawi ngaithla rawh—heng thil thlengte hi a thlen hunah, Divyasingha hming pu lal rorel hun a kal mek ang a, fapa nei lovin, fanu chauh a nei ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu pakhatah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ କରିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପତି,
ତାଙ୍କ କୁଳେ ପୁତ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭଣେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bāra bāra beḷaku kariba Dibyasiṃha nṛpati,
tāṅka kuḷe putra nohiba bhaṇe dīna Achyutī.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga tawpah, Divyasingha hming pu lal rorel hun a awm ang a, fapa nei lovin a awm ang.

Mahapurush-a zawlnei thu sawi hi tunlai Puri Lal, Shri Divyasingha Deva nen a inmil chiah chiah bawk. Tunlai Lal chu fapa a nei lo; fanu pali chauh a nei.

A Malika lehkhabu dang, ‘Kaliyuga Gita’-ah, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan Lalpa Krishna-a'n Dwaparayuga-ah Arjuna hnena a thusawi chu a ziak a, chutah chuan Lalpa Krishna chuan a sawi a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shrikshetra rājana mora na sebi payara.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Kaliyuga tawpah, Puri Lal chuan ka (Jagannath anga) ni tin rawngbawlna chu a ti lo ang.

Achyutananda Dasji chuan a ziak leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ନ ଥିବ ରାଜନ ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ମଉଡ଼ ମଣି,
ରାଜ୍ୟ ରାଜକୁଳ କନ୍ୟାକୁ ନ କରିବ ବର୍ଣ୍ଣି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Na thiba rājana Shrīkṣetre kṣetra mauḍa maṇi,
rājya rājakuḷa kanyāku na kariba barṇṇi.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Puri Lal chuan Shrikshetra emaw Puri-ah emaw a cheng lo ang a, hmun dangah a cheng ang. Odisha lal chhungkua atanga lal fanu a nei lo ang a, ram dang atanga lal fanu a nei ang.

Kum zabi 18-naa piang Odisha mi thianghlim ropui tak, Mahapurush Arakshita Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Dwadasa Chandrika’-ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଆସିବେ ଫେରି,
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରକୁ ନ ଯାଇ ରହିବେ ଜାଣ ସେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି।
ଭେଦ ମୁଁ କାହୁଁ ଜାଣିଥାନ୍ତି ଆଦିମାତା କହିଲେ,
ଭଣେ ଅରକ୍ଷିତ ଅଧମ ତହୁଁ ଶୁଣିଲି ଭଲେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati nishchaya āsibe pheri,
kṣhetraku na jāi rahibe jāṇa se Khaṇḍagiri.
bheda muñ kāhuñ jāṇithānti Ādimātā kahile,
bhaṇe Arakṣhita adhama tahuñ śuṇili bhale.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan Kaliyuga tawpah, Divyasingha Deva chu Puri Lal a ni ang a, Shrikshetra emaw Puri-ah emaw a cheng lo ang a, Khandagiri bialah emaw Bhubaneswar-ah emaw a cheng ang tih a ziak a. Mahapurush chuan engtin nge ani ngeiin heng thil thurukte hi a hriat theih tih a ziak belhchhah a; Nu Hmasa Ber (Adi Mata) chuan heng thil hi a hrilh a ni.

Chhiartute hnenah hian ka hrilh duh a ni, tunlai Lal hmaa lal zawng zawngte chuan Puri, Odisha-ah an cheng thin a, Lalpa Jagannath-a ni tin rawngbawlna an ti thin, mahse tunlai Lal Shri Divyasingha Deva chuan Lalpa Jagannath-a rawngbawlna chu hun pawimawh thenkhat chauhvin a ti thin. Puri-ah a cheng lo a, Shahid Nagar, Bhubaneswar-ah a cheng zawk. Bakah, Shri Divyasingha Deva chuan Odisha atanga lal fanu a nei lo a, Jammu mi Lieutenant Colonel Anant Singh Charak fanu a nei zawk.

Chung zawlnei thu sawi pathumte chu tunlai Puri Lal, Shri Divyasingha Deva nen a inmil chiah chiah. Hei hi Kaliyuga chu Lal Shri Divyasingha Deva-a rorel hun chhungin a tawp ang tih finfiahna a ni bawk.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu dang ‘Tera Janma Sharana’-ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Māgha Saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājattwa karibe ehi Ekāmra Nagara.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Divyasingha Deva chu Puri Lal a nih hunah, chutichuan Ananta Keshari, chu chu, Lalpa Kalki-a rorel hun a thleng ang. Magha Saptami ni a ni ang; Bhubaneswar (Ekambra Nagar) chu hmun a ni ang.

He bung hian a finfiah bawk, Kalki-a Pathian thiltih ropuite hun laia Puri Lal chu Shri Divyasingha Deva tih loh chu tumah a ni lo ang.

Achyutanandaji chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Mohakalpa’-ah chuan Shri Divyasingha Deva chu Puri lal hnuhnung ber a ni dawn tih chiang taka tarlanna dang a pe a. Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଧନୁଶର ଧରି ଆଗେ ସେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ଯେ ଜାଣ ବୈଷ୍ଣବ ଧର୍ମ ଛାଡିବେ।
କ୍ଷତ୍ରି ବୃତ୍ତି ଧରି ପଦାତିଙ୍କ ସହ କରିବେ ବହୁ ସମର,
ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କ ଦେଖି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଡିବେ ପଦ୍ମପାଦର।
ନବଲକ୍ଷ ଧର୍ମ-ହୀନ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ତହିଁ ତୁରିତେ ହେବେ ଛେଦନ,
ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଘାତେ ତହିଁ ମେଦିନୀ ଯେ କମ୍ପମାନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଠାରୁ ଆଦି ଓଡିଶା ରାଜ୍ୟରେ ଯେତେକ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଥିବେ,
ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେଇ ସମସ୍ତେ ତହିଁ ମରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ର ପୁଛିବେ,
ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ହତ୍ୟା ବୋଲି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ କହି ବଧ୍ୟ ନ କରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପରୀକ୍ଷା କରିବେ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ଜାଣ,
ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣଟି ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେବ ନାଶିବ ଯେ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ ଚକ୍ର ଶୂନ୍ୟ ଗଗନେ ରଖିବେ,
ଚଉଷଠି ଅକ୍ଷର ପ୍ରମାଣ ନ ପାଇ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଥାଇ ଛେଦିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Lalāṭa Kesharī dhanushara dhari āge se juddha karibe,
Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati je jāṇa baiṣṇaba dharma chhāḍibe.
Kṣatri bṛutti dhari padātiṅka saha karibe bahu samara,
Prabhuṅka dekhi Dibyasiṃha Deba paḍibe padmapādara.
Nabalakṣa dharma-hīna byakti tahiṃ turite hebe chhedana,
Lalāṭa Kesharī juddha ghāte tahiṃ medinī je kampamāna.
Kṣetra ṭhāru ādi Oḍishā rājyare jeteka brāhmaṇa thibe,
Gāyatrī mantraku parīkṣā na dei samaste tahiṃ maribe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba brāhmaṇamānaṅku Gāyatrī mantra puchhibe,
Brāhmaṇa hatyā boli brāhmaṇamānaṅku kahi badhya na karibe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba parīkṣā karibe Gāyatrī mantraku jāṇa,
Jeuṃ brāhmaṇaṭi parīkṣā na deba nāshiba je Sudarshana.
Ananta Kesharī Sudarshana chakra shūnya gagane rakhibe,
Chauṣaṭhi akṣara pramāṇa na pāi shūnyare thāi chhedibe.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Puri Lal, Shri Divyasingha Deva chuan a Vaishnava Dharma chu kalsanin Kshatriya hna a thlang ang a, indona tam tak a do ang. Lalpa Kalki a hmuh veleh, Lalpa ke bulah a tlu ang a, Lalpa malsawmna a la ang a, sakhaw dodaltu nuai kua a tiboral ang. Jagannath hmun, Puri-ah chuan, Brahmin awm zawng zawngte chu Gayatri Mantra hmangin an fiah vek ang. Brahmin thahna sual hlauin, Divyasingha Deva chuan fiahna paltlang lo Brahmins-te chu a that lo ang. Mahapurush chuan an zaa chu Lalpa Sudarshana Chakra ngeiin a tiboral vek ang a ti. Lalpa chuan a Sudarshana Chakra chu van ruakah a dah ang a, Gayatri Mantra chham lo Brahmins-te chu chu chakra ngei chuan a lu a tan ang.

Hetah hian, Mahapurush chuan Puri Lal, Shri Divyasingha Deva chuan Lalpa Kalki a hmu ang a, Dharma dinnaah a tel ve ang tih finfiahna a pe a ni. Lalpa Kalki tihchhiatna thiltih ropuite chu Shri Divyasingha Deva hun chhungin a thleng ang tih a chiang a ni.

Chutah Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kalpa Tika’-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିୟ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ କରିଥିବେ ସେ ବନ୍ଧନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ନୃପତି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଯେ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଯେ ରଖିଥିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଗୋପନ।
ବନ୍ଧନର ଦୁଇ ଦିନରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି,
ରାତ୍ରିକାଳେ ରାଜା ପାଶେ ବିଜେ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ରାଜାର ବନ୍ଧନ ଫେଇ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବାସୁ ଦେବେ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ତଳ ପୁରେ ଗୋପନେ ରଖିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dibyasiṃha Deba se je Kṣhatriya rājana,
tāhānku Phiringī karithibe se bandhana.
Kṣhetrara nṛupati Dibyasiṃha je rājana,
tāhānku je rakhithibe Shrī Hari gopana.
Bandhanara dui dinare Prabhu je Shrī Hari,
rātrikāḷe rājā pāśe bije je Shrī Hari.
rājāra bandhana phei Prabhu bāśu debe,
Khaṇḍagiri taḷa pure gopane rakhibe.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Puri Lal, Shri Divyasingha Deva chu ram dang sipaiin an man hunah, ni hnih hnuah, Lalpa Kalki chuan zanah lal chu a rawn thleng ang a, sal tangna ata a chhuah ang a, Khandagiri tlangpui hnuai lamah a thup ang.

Chubakah, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Padmakalpa Tika’-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ମୋ ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ,
ସେହିବେଳେ ସରକାର ଖାଦ୍ୟ ପକାଇବେ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଆଦିକରି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ananta gumphā madhyare mo bhakta rahibe,
sehibeḷe sarakāra khādya pakāibe.
Ananta Keshari swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha Lalāṭa Keshari ādikari.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, tui chhiatna nasa tak a thlen hunah, Lalpa Kalki-a ringtute chu Khandagiri tlanga awm Ananta pukah an him ang. Chu mi hunah chuan, sorkar chuan tuar tute hnenah chaw leh chhawmdawlna thil a pe ang. Mahapurush chuan chu mi hunah chuan Ananta Madhava, a nih loh leh Lalpa Kalki chu Khandagiri, Odisha-ah a awm ang a ti. Lal Divyasingha Deva pawh a hnenah a awm ve ang.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kalpa Tika’-ah chuan a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାବରେ ସେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଦେଖିବେ,
ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହକୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Anaṃta kesharī swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha deba se je lalāṭakesharī.
Khaṇḍagiri ṭhābare se prabhuṃku dekhibe,
Lalāṭakesharī Dibyasiṃhaku chihnibe.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Lalpa Ananta Keshari, a nih loh leh Lalpa Kalki chu Khandagiri-ah a awm hunah, chu mi hunah chuan Divyasingha Deva pawh Khandagiri-ah a awm ve ang. Khandagiri-ah chuan, Divyasingha Deva chuan Lalpa Darshan (hmuhna) a nei ang.

A chunga châng pathum atang pawhin, Lalpa Kalki Pathian thiltih ropui hunah chuan, Puri Lal chu Divyasingha Deva a ni ang tih hriat a ni.

6.1 Lalpa Kalki leh Baladeva-te leh Divyasingha Deva leh Lal Tam Takte Inhmuhna

Bhavishya Malika hi thil thleng thup leh hrilh lawkna tam takin a khat a ni. Chung hrilh lawknate zingah chuan lal tam takin Lalpa an hmuh tur thu hrilh lawkna a tel a ni.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabuah chuan nakin hunah chuan ram dang sipaiin Chhatia, Jajpur atanga Nilagiri emaw Nilachal, Puri thlengin an luah hun a thleng ang a ti. Hmelma sipai rual lian tak pawh Jagannath Temple chhungah an lut ang. Chung zawng zawng chu harsatna nasa takin a hmuhin, Lalpa chuan, inngaihtuahnaah a pil ang a, Sahadeva, Nakula, Ashwatthama, leh Shalya-te chu a hrechhuak ang. Lalpa'n anmahni a hriat chhuah veleh, he sipai huaisen pali, thal kah thiam tak takte inngaihtuahna leh inngaihtuahna chu a chhe ang a, an pali chuan Chhatia an thleng ang a, Lalpa ke bulah an tlu ang a, a thupek an dil ang. Lalpa chuan an pali chu Mughal sipai rual lian tak tihchhiat tur leh Pathian ngainatute venhim turin thu a pe ang. Lalpa thupek angin, an pali chuan Mughal sipai an tiboral ang a, chutah Lalpa hnenah an kir leh ang. Chu mi hunah chuan, Pathian ngainatu tam tak Lalpa hnenah Chhatia-ah an awm ang a, ni 27 chhung chu Chhatia chu Pathian ngainatute inhmuhna hmun a ni ang. Chu mi hunah chuan, Russia, Nepal, Manipur, Ali, Kujang, Kanika, etc., lal te chuan Lalpa an hmu ang a, a Darshan an dawng ang a, a ke bulah an inpe ang. Chu mi hunah chuan, Lalpa chuan an zaa chu a hruai ang a, Sealdah a thleng ang a, Lalpa Baladeva hnenah ram dang sipai la awmte tihchhiat turin a dil ang. Chutiang chuan, Lalpa hnena lal zawng zawngte chu Sealdah-ah an kal ang a, Baladeva an hmu ang a, chutah chuan Arjuna leh Bhima chu an lo awm tawh ang.

Hetah hian Mahapurush chuan he hun chhung hian, Lalpa Ananta Keshari leh Lalpa Baladeva hmu tur lal te zingah chuan Puri Lal, Shrikshetra pawh a tel ve ang a ti. Tunge a nih tur thu tarlangin, Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabuah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଯେ ରାଜା ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ତାହାଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗତେ ସ୍ଥିତି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kṣhetre je rājā Dibyasiṃha Deba tāhāṅka saṅgate sthiti.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Lalpa Darshan dawngtu lal te zingah chuan Shrikshetra Puri Lal, Divyasingha Deva a tel ang.

A chunga sawina atangte pawhin, Kalki Pathian thiltih ropui hunah chuan Puri Lal chu Shri Divyasingha Deva ngei a ni ang tih a chiang a ni.

A chunga châng 17 atangte hian, Kaliyuga tawpah, Lalpa Kalki a lo piang tawh a, a tihchhiatna thiltih ropuite khawvel pumah a thlen hunah, mi sualte tihchhiatna, Pathian ngainatute chhandamna, leh Dharma din hna a thlen hunah, chu mi hunah chuan Divyasingha Deva hming pu lal pakhat chu Jagannath Puri lalthutphahah a thu ang a, he dinhmunah hian lal hnuhnung ber a ni bawk ang tih chiang takin a finfiah a ni.

Shri Divyasingha Deva chu lal hnuhnung ber a ni ang—hei hi Mahapurush Jagannath Das, Panchasakhas zinga mi pakhat chuan a Malika lehkhabuah a ziak a, chu chu he bung châng hmasa berah khan kan sawi tawh a ni.

Mahse, chhinchhiah tlak chu Jagannath Das chauh ni lovin, Panchasakhas zinga Mahapurush dang, Shishu Ananta Das pawhin he thil chungchang hi a Malika lehkhabu ‘Patta Madana’-ah a ziak a ni. Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଆଉ ଶାସନ ନ ଥିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dibyasiṃha nṛupa rājattwa karibe āu śāsana na thiba.

Awmzia:
A awmzia chu, Lal Divyasingha Deva chuan ro a rel ang a, a hnuah chuan lal dang tumahin ro an rel tawh lo ang. Divyasingha Deva chu lal hnuhnung ber a ni ang. A nih loh leh, a hunah chuan Kaliyuga a tawp ang a, Lalpa Kalki tihchhiatna thiltih ropuite a thleng ang.

6.2 Shri Divyasingha Deva

Hetah hian chhinchhiah tlak chu Mahapurush chuan Kaliyuga tawpa Puri Lal hming chu Divyasingha Deva a ni ang tih sawiin a tawp mai lo; a chanchin tam tak a sawi zawk a, chungte chu lal 19-na a ni ang, Divyasingha Deva palina a ni ang, fapa a nei lo ang, fanu chauh a nei ang, Puri-ah awm lovin Bhubaneswar-ah a awm ang, Lalpa Jagannath rawngbawlna ni tin a ti thei lo ang, naupan laiin lal a ni ang a, tar thlengin lal a ni ang, tih te a ni.

Chung thil zawng zawngte chu tunlai Lal Shri Divyasingha Deva nen an inmil chiah a ni.

7 Jagannath Temple-a Kaliyuga tawpna chhinchhiahte

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das leh Panchasakhas dangte chuan an Malika lehkhabuteah hmun tam takah Pathian ngainatute chuan Jagannath Temple atangin entirna tam tak an dawng ang a, chu mi hmang chuan Kali Yuga a tawp tawh a, Lalpa Kalki a lo piang tawh tih an hria ang tih an ziak a ni. Chung entirnate chu Jagannath Temple atang chauh ni lovin, India rama hmun thianghlim leh temple dangte atang pawhin hmuh theih a ni ang, mahse a bik takin Puri-a awm Jagannath Temple atangin hmuh theih a ni ang.

Odia Sloka:

ବାରମ୍ବାର ଶ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ଦିରରେ ପୁଣି,
ନାନା ଅଘଟଣ ସତ୍ୟ ମୋ ବାଣୀ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bārambāra shrīmandirare puṇi,
nānā aghaṭana satya mo bāṇī.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabuah chuan Kali Yuga tawpah, Puri-a awm Shrimandir (Jagannath Temple)-ah chuan thil thalo chi hrang hrang (chhiatna entirna) hmuh nawn fo a ni ang tih a ziak a. Chubakah, ka thu hi a dik ngei ang a ti.

7.1 Jagannath Temple-a Chhiatna Entirna Chanchin

Vawiin hian kum 15-20 kalta chhung khan Jagannath Temple-ah chhiatna entirna tam tak hmuh a ni tih kan hria a, chungte chu kum 1990-ah temple 'Amla Bedha' atangin lung lian tak a tla; hmun thianghlim ber telin temple chung lam atangin lungte chu vawi tam tak a tla; Singhadwara (Lion Gate) hmaah temple ban leh Aruna Stambha chungah savawm (sa thi ei) an thu; temple chungah chingpirinu tam tak an thlawk kual; pathian lem chunga dah Ratnachandua (lung mawi tak takin chei) a kang; temple chunga zar 'Patitapabana Bana' (flag) a kang; 2.2-ton Neelachakra, Ashtadhatu (rangkachak chi riat) siam, thli na takin a tihkawi; Patitapabana Bana thliin a chhem thla a, thlipui na takah tuipuiah a tla; temple compound-a Kalpavata (thingkung lian tak) phel pakhat a tliak; Patitapabana Bana chu kawlthehin a rawn den avangin a kang; temple choka thianghlimah chuan bel chhiatna 40 vel chu mi sual tak takin an tichhe; temple Aruna Stambha, Natamandapa chhunga ban, hmun thianghlim ber hmaa hall (Jagamohana), leh Singhadwara bulah chêt a awm; Ratnasinghasana (lung mawi tak takin chei lalthutphah) atangin 'Padma Pakhuda' (lotus petal) lung a bo; temple chokaah kawltheh a tla; zanah temple-ah rukrukna a thleng; Corona hri leng laiin Pathian ngainatute an tlem avangin temple-ah sazu an pung a, buaina an siam a, temple thil tih dan an tibuai; temple Ratnasinghasana telin hmun tam takah thisen far a awm a, chu mi hnuah Chaturdha Murti (pathian lem pali) Mahasnana (tihthianghlimna lian) an nei; temple velah van a sen vek; temple-ah kelawng tam tak hmuh thin an bo thut; mobile phone hman phal loh chung pawha hman; mit hmaiah thup ruk spy camera hmanga temple compound-ah video lem lak; Hindu ni lote leh Sanatani ni lote temple-a luh phal loh chung pawha an luh; intihhlumna emaw thihna dang a thleng; Baisi Pahacha, a nih loh leh temple step 22-ah thihna a thleng; temple Hasti Dwara (North Gate) Gumuta (dome)-ah chêt a awm, tih te a ni.

Chung thil thleng thenkhat chungchang hrilh lawkna hrang hrangte chu Malika lehkhabu hrang hrangah hmuh theih a ni a, chutah chuan chung thil thlengte chu Kali Yuga tawpah chauh a thleng ang a, Pathian ngainatute hnenah Lalpa Kalki khawvelah a lo kal tur thu entirna a pe ang tih chiang takin a sawi a ni. Chuvangin, tunah Kali Yuga tawpna leh Lalpa Kalki lo pianna chungchangah rinhlelhna leh ringhlelhna lantirtute chuan an ngaihdan chu an ngaihtuah nawn leh tur a ni.




Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a 'Jaiphula Malika'-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁଁ ପଥର,
ଖସୁଥିବ ପୁଣି ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ତୁହି ଦେଖିବୁ ବେନି ନେତ୍ରର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Baḍa deuluṃ pathara,
khasuthiba puṇi tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
tuhi dekhibu beni netrara.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan sawi tawh hun chhung hian, Puri-a awm Jagannath Temple-ah chuan lung tla nawn fo thil thlengte chu a lo lang ang a, chu chu Pathian ngainatute chuan an mit ngeiin chiang takin an hmu ang a ti.

He hrilh lawkna dikna hi kan ngaihtuah chuan, June 14, 1990-a temple 'Amla Bedha' atanga lung 5-6 ton rit a tlak hnuah, chutiang thil thleng chu vawi tam tak a thleng tawh tih kan hria ang. August 13, 1992-ah chuan lung lian tak pahnih chu temple hmun thianghlim berah a tla thut a. Chutiang bawkin, October 26, 1998-ah chuan lung 15 kg rit chu temple pui atangin a lum thla a, temple pui compound chhunga Bimala temple bulah a tla a. November 1, 2011-ah chuan lung 1 ton vel rit chu Jagannath Temple kawngka thlang lam Dadhinauti bul atangin a tla thla a ni.

Kum 2015-ah pawh, February 2-ah, Jagannath Temple-a Bimala Temple bul, thlang lama sak Jalakrida Mandapa atangin lung rit tak, 30-40 kg vela rit chu leiah a tla a. Tun hnai thil thleng sawi chuan, August 3, 2022-ah, kum zana pahnihnaa sak biak in thianghlim ber chunga plaster 1.5 kg vela rit chu a tla a. October 23, 2022-ah, Majana Mandapa leh Charcha Ghara bul, biak in chhim-chhak lam atangin lung a tla a, hei hnu rei lo teah, October 28, 2022-ah, Natamandapa leh Jagamohana inzawmna bul, Sakhi lem chunga a chhawng pathumna atangin lung 2-3 kg vela rit chu a tla leh a. Kum 2024-ah pawh, March 9-ah, Jagannath Temple compound-a Bimala Temple hmaa awm Baraha Temple chunga lung 4 kg vela rit chu a tla a.

He tiang hian, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a'n kum za tam tak liamtaah Jagannath Temple atanga lung "tharaku thara" (zing takin) a tlak tur thu a lo hrilhlawk chu tun hnai kum tlemteah a dik tih a lang chiang a ni.

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ସେହିଦିନଠାରୁ ସଖୀ ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ପଥର ଖସିବ ଯୁଗ ଶେଷ ହେବାପାଇଁ,
ଜାଣି ମୁଁ ଗରୁଡ଼ ଏ ଶକୁନମାନ ଗୁପତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ଏକ ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ତେଇଶି ବରଷେ କଳି ଦେଶ ଛାଡ଼ି ଯିବ।
ଚୁଳରୁ ପଥର ଯେବେ ଖସିବ ସୁତ,
ଖସିଲେ ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ହେବ ଏ କଳି ହତ।
ମୋର ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ତୋ ଦେଉଳ ଚୁଳେ,
ଖସିବ ପଥର ଖଣ୍ଡ ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ତଳେ।
ବତାସରେ ହେ। ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବକ୍ର ହେବ ବାରେ ହେ।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ଅରୁଣ ଖମ୍ବର।
ବତାସର ହେ। ବକ୍ର ହେବ ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ମୋର ହେ।
ତରକିବେ ତେରଜଣ ଜାଣିଥା ନିକର,
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁ ପଥର ଖସି ବାରମ୍ବାର।
ଗୃଧ୍ରପକ୍ଷୀ ମଥାପରେ ବସିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ,
ସେହିଦିନୁ ହତଶିରୀ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Baḍa deularu mora pathara khasiba,
sehidinaṭhāru sakhī bipada paḍiba.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru pathara khasiba yuga sheṣha hebāpāiṃ,
jāṇi mu Garuḍa e shakunamāna gupate deli chetei.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru eka pathara khasiba,
teishi baraṣhe kali desha chāḍi jiba.
Chularu pathara jebe khasiba suta,
khasile aaṃlā beḍhāru heba e kali hata.
Mora baḍa deularu khasiba pathara,
Shrīkṣhetra rājana mora na sebi payara.
Basiba je giddha pakṣhī to deula chule,
khasiba pathara khaṃḍa paḍiba je tale.
Batāsare he. Nīlachakra bakra heba bāre he.
Baḍa deularu mora khasiba pathara,
basiba je giddha pakṣhī Aruṇa khambara.
Batāsara he. Bakra heba Nīlachakra mora he.
Tarakibe terajaṇa jāṇithā nikara,
baḍa deulu pathara khasi bārambāra.
Giddhapakṣhī mathāpare basiṇa je jiba,
sehidinu hatashirī pṛithwī hoiba.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan 'Rukmini Gita', 'Garuda Samvada', 'Hari Arjuna Chautisha', 'Kaliyuga Gita', 'Jai Birachita Chautisha', leh 'Chakada Madana' tih Malika lehkhabu tam takah Puri-a Jagannath Temple atanga Kali Yuga tawpna chhinchhiahna thalo tam tak a sawi chiang hle a. He thu-ah hian, Jagannath Temple-a "Amla Bedha" atanga lung a tlak hunah chuan, ringtute chuan Kali Yuga tawpna a hnai tawh hle a, chhiatna leh vanduaina tam takin khawvel a tlakbuak thuai dawn tih an hriatthiam tur thu a ziak a. He lung tlak hi Kali Yuga tawpna chhinchhiahna pawimawh leh thuruk a ni ang a, a tlak atanga kum 23 hnuah Kali Yuga a tawp ang a ti a. Chu hun chhungah chuan, savawm thalo pakhat chu Jagannath Temple chungah leh biak in Singhadwara hmaa awm Aruna Stambha chungah a thu ang a ti bawk a. Chu mai bakah, biak in chunga Ashtadhatu-a siam 2.2-ton Neelachakra chu thli chak tak avangin a kun ang.

Chhiartute chuan kum za tam tak liamtaah lo ziah tawh thu hrilhlawkna zawng zawngte chu tunah hian Jagannath Temple-ah a thleng dik ta vek tih hriat chu mak an ti hle ang. Ruah nasa tak a sur laiin, a hmasa berin, June 14, 1990-ah, biak in "Amla Bedha" atangin lung 5-6 ton vela rit chu a tla a. Chumi hnuah chuan, Aruna Stambha chungah savawm thalo pakhat a thu bawk a.

Mahse, Kali Yuga tawpna hunah hian, Malika thu hrilhlawkna chu a dik tih lantirin, biak in chunga Patitapabana Bana (flagstaff) chungah chhingkhuang/mu ang chi savawm thalo tam tak an thu hmu tawh a ni. Tun hnai kumah hian, August 11, 2018, December 26, 2018, leh July 23, 2020-ah te pawh hetiang tho hi a thleng a, savawm thalo chu biak in hnam puanzar chungah an thu hmu a ni. December 7, 2021-ah, hetiang savawm tam tak chu Jagannath Temple chungah an thlawk kual hmu a. August 17, 2022-ah pawh hetiang thil thleng hi hmuh leh a ni. He thil thleng hi a tawp mai lo a; kum 2023 May, June, leh September thlaah te khan, Puri khuaa chengte chuan he thil thalo thleng hi an hmu leh a, biak in chunga hnam puanzar-ah savawm ang chi an thu hmu a ni.

Kum 2025-ah pawh, September 4-ah Aruna Stambha chungah savawm hmuh a ni a, September 2, September 16, leh October 14-ah te chuan biak in chungah chhingkhuang an thu hmu a. December 9-ah, Shrimandir chungah savawm tam tak an thlawk kual hmu a ni.

Heng zawng zawng bakah hian, May 3, 2019-a Puri ramri tichhe tu thlipui na tak, 'Fani' chuan biak in chunga Neelachakra chu a tihniam a, hetiang hian kum za tam tak liamtaah lo ziah tawh thu hrilhlawkna dang pakhat chu a thleng dik ta a ni.

Kum 16-naa ziah thu hrilhlawkna te hi Kali Yuga tawpna chhinchhiahna mai a ni lo a, Kalki Avatar lo thlen tur Panchasakhas-te'n an lo hrilhlawkna a ni bawk tih hriat a pawimawh a, Bhavishya Malika telin lehkhabu thianghlim zawng zawngin an sawi angin, Kalki Avatar chu Kali Yuga tawpnaah chauh a lo thleng dawn a ni.





Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।
ହର ଉଦ୍ୟାନରେ ମାୟା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛକୁ ଗ୍ରାସୁଥିବେ,
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବାନା ଝଡ଼ିବ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଲୋଡ଼ିଯିବେ।
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ପରୁ ପତାକା ଛିଡ଼ିଣ ପଡ଼ିବ ସମୁଦ୍ର କୂଳେ,
କଳକୀ ରୂପକୁ ଚିନ୍ତା ପୁଣ ହେବ ଭାବିବେ କ୍ଷୀରାଧି କୂଳେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Nīlachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṃgā adhara,
Jājanagre janma hoibe Biṣhṇu Sharmā gṛihara.
Hara udyānare māyā prakāshiba mlechchaku grāsuthibe,
Nīlachakra bānā jhaḍiba nishchaya bhakataṃku loḍijibe.
Nīlachakra paru patākā chiḍiṇa paḍiba samudra kūle,
Kalki rūpaku chiṃtā puṇa heba bhābibe kṣhīrādhi kūle.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' leh 'Kalikalpa Gita'-ah Kali Yuga tawpna leh Lalpa Kalki pianna chhinchhiahna dang a sawi a. Shrimandir-a Neelachakra chunga Patitapabana Bana chu a hmun atangin a thlawk bo hun a thleng ang a ti a. Mahapurush chuan flag chu a thlawk bo ang a, tuipuiah a tla ang a ti bawk.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a thu hrilhlawkna hi a dik tih a lang chiang bawk. Thlipui na tak 'Fani' chuan Puri ramri a thlen hma ni khat, May 2, 2019-ah, thli chak tak avangin Patitapabana Bana chu a thlawk bo a, tuipuiah a tla a. Hetiang thil thleng chiah hi May 18, 2020-ah, Cyclone 'Amphan' chuan Odisha ramri a thlen hma ni hnihah a thleng leh a ni.



Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡ଼ି ଆମ୍ଭେ ଯିବୁ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ଲାଗିବ ରତ୍ନଚାନ୍ଦୁଆ ଅଗ୍ନି ସେତେବେଳେ।
ନିଶାକାଳେ ହେ। ନୀଳାଚଳୁ ଚୋରିଯିବ ହେଲେ ହେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Nīlāchala chāḍi āmbhe jibu jetebele,
lāgiba ratnachāṃduā agni setebele.
Nishākāle he. Nīlāchalu chorijiba hele he.

Awmzia:
Lalpa thu sawi chu tarlangin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Jai Birachita Chautisha'-ah Lalpa Jagannath-a chu Nilachala, chu chu Shrimandir, atanga mihring taksa-a pian turin a chhuah hunah, chu hunah chuan Jagannath Temple-a pathian pathum chunga dah lungmantam chhawlhdum chu a kang ang a ti a.

Ratnachandua kang thleng hi kum 2004-05-a thleng a ni a, chuvangin Mahapurush-a thu hrilhlawkna hi a dik tih a lang chiang bawk.

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍ପବଟ ଡ଼ାଳ ଛେଦନ ଭୋଇବ ଦେଖିବୁ ତୁ ନୟନରେ,
ଶିବକଳ୍ପ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ତୁରେ।
ଏକ ଦକ୍ଷିଣରେ ନବ ଯେ ଥିବ,
କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଛେଦନ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kalpabaṭa ḍāla chedana hoiba dekhibu tu nayanare,
Shibakalpa bāṇī anyathā nohiba shuṇa Rāmadāsa ture.
Eka dakṣhiṇare naba je thiba,
kalpabaṭa mora chedana hoiba.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'-ah kum 600 liamtaah khan Jagannath Temple compound-a Kalpavata zara chu a tliak hun a thleng ang a ti a. A lehkhabu dangah pawh 'pakhat' bulah 'pakua' a awm hunah, chu chu number 19 a nih hunah chauh he thil hi a thleng ang a ti bawk.

Mahapurush-a thu sawi ang chiahin, he thu hrilhlawkna hi kum 2019-ah a thleng dik a, May 3-a Puri ramri tichhe tu thlipui na tak 'Fani' chuan biak in compound-a Kalpavata lian tak, upa tawh tak chu a tichhe a ni.

Odia Sloka:

ରକ୍ତଛିଟା ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ରତ୍ନସିଂହାସନେ,
ଏହି ଘଟଣା ଘଟିବ ମନେ ରଖ ଦିନେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Raktachīṃṭā paḍiba je ratnasiṃhāsane,
ehi ghaṭaṇā ghaṭiba mane rakha dine.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush-a'n Kali Yuga tawpnaah Puri-a Jagannath Temple atanga hmuh tur chhinchhiahna tam tak a sawi zingah, pakhat chu pathian lemte Ratnasinghasana-ah thisen far hmuh a ni.

Mahapurush-a'n a ziah thu hrilhlawkna hi a dik tih a lang chiang bawk. Kum tlemte liamtaah khan, biak in hmun tam takah, Ratnasinghasana telin, thisen far a tla a, chumi hnuah chuan, biak in dan ang zelin, Chaturdha Murti chu Mahasnana (tihthianghlimna ropui) hmangin an tihthianghlim a ni. Entirnan, March 6, 2021-ah, biak in thianghlim ber atanga hla lo te-a awm 'Bhitar Katha' bulah thisen hmuh a ni. April 18, 2021-ah, biak in venhimna tura dah police pahnih insual avangin biak in compound-ah thisen far a tla a. March 7, 2022-ah, 'Abakasha Niti' (serh) neih laiin, biak in Jagamohana (biak in thianghlim ber hmaa hall) chhunga 'Bhitar Katha' bulah thisen far hmuh a ni. Chumi hnuah April 11, 2022-ah, biak in Dhukudi Dwara bulah thisen far hmuh a ni. November 3, 2022-ah, 'Mangala Alati', 'Mailama', leh 'Tadapa Lagi' serh hnuah, 'Bhitar Katha' bulah thisen far hmuh a ni. Hetiang thil thleng hi kum 2023 January thlaa kar thumnaah hmuh leh a ni. Lalpa Jagannath-a 'Abakasha' leh 'Besa Lagi' serh zawh hnuah, Shrimandir-ah thisen hmuh a ni a, chuvangin Gopalaballabha hlan (Bhoga) chu tihfel theih a ni lo. Chumi hnuah June 4, 2023-ah, pathian tihthianghlimna (Devasnana) zawh hnuah, Snana Mandapa-ah thisen far hmuh a ni. Tun hnai, October 13, 2023-ah pawh, Shrimandir-a 'Madhyahna Dhupa' zawh veleh servitor pahnih Prasad sem chungchanga an insual avangin biak in thianghlim berah thisen far a tla a. Kum 2024-ah pawh, April 23 zing hma takah, Singhadwara hawn hnuah, servitor chhungkuaa hmeichhe pakhatin biak in kawngkharah a kut a nghat a, a kutzungtang chu a chep a. Chuvangin, a kutzungtang atangin thisen chu Dhukudi Dwara bul, Bedha chhung lamah a chhuak a. Hetiang dinhmunah chuan, Lalpa Ropui ber Mahasnana chu Mangala Alati hma pawhin tih a ni. Hetiang tho hian, kum 2025-ah pawh biak in compound-ah thisen chhuak thleng a chhunzawm zel a ni.

7.2 Kum 2024 Rath Yatra-a Lalpa Balabhadra lem a tlak.

Shri Jagannath Puri-a ni 11 Rath Yatra neih laiin, July 9, 2024 zan dar 7-ah 'Adapa Mandapa Bije' serh chu an tan a. He mi lai hian, Lalpa Balabhadra lem chu tawlailir atanga Gundicha Temple-a Adapa Mandapa-ah an hruai laiin, Charamala-ah a hmai chungin a tla a, chuvangin servitor 12 an hliam bawk. Hetiang thil pawi tak hi chanchin hluiah a vawi khat nan a thleng a ni. Lalpa Balabhadra-a hetiang taka a tlak hi chhiatna lo thleng tur chhinchhiahna a ni ngei a, chu chu Lalpa Brahma leh Lalpa Vishnu-a hrilh angin, Shrikhetra dan leh hrai chungchang 'Niladri' lehkhabuah hetiang hian hmuh theih a ni.

Odia Sloka:

ମୁରବୈରିଣ ଏତସ୍ୟ ଜଗଦୀଶସ୍ୟ ଭୂପତେହଃ।
ଭଦ୍ରାୟାଶ୍ଚ ବଳସ୍ୟାପି ପ୍ରମାଦାତ୍ ପତନଂ ଯଦି।।
ମହାଭୟଂ ନୃପସ୍ୟାପି ରାଜ୍ୟସ୍ୟାପି ଭବେଦ୍ଧ୍ରୁବମ୍।
ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷଂ ମରଣଂ ଚୈବ ଜାୟତେ ଚ ପୁନଃ ପୁନଃ।।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Murabairiṇa etasya jagadīshasya bhūpatehaḥ.
Bhadrāyāshcha balasyāpi pramādāt patanaṃ yadi.
Mahābhayaṃ nṛipasyāpi rājyasyāpi bhabeddhrubam.
Durbhikṣhaṃ maraṇaṃ chaiba jāyate cha punaḥ punaḥ.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, "Aw Lalpa, Jagannath, Balabhadra, emaw Subhadra-te an tla palh a nih chuan, lal leh ram chungah vanduaina nasa tak a thleng ang. Tam a tla ang a, hripui a leng nawn fo ang."

Kum 2024-a Ujjain-a Mahakal Temple thianghlim berah kangmei.

Monday, March 25, 2024 zingah, Holi kut neih laiin, Ujjain-a Mahakaleshwar Temple thianghlim berah kangmei a chhuak a, Bhasma Aarti neih laiin 'gulal' (rawng powder) an theh avangin a ni. He thil thlengah hian mi 14 vel, puithiam lian leh servitor dangte telin, kangna hliam an tuar a ni.

7.3 Kum 2025-a Jagannath Temple atanga chhinchhiahna thalo hmuh te.

Jagannath Temple atanga chhinchhiahna thalo hmuh te chu kum 2025-ah pawh a chhunzawm zel a. Chhinchhiahna tam tak hmangin, Lalpa Jagannath-a chuan A ringtute chu chhiatna lo thleng tur chungchang a hrilh lawk a, chung chhinchhiahna pawimawh zualte chu kan rawn tarlang a ni.

(a) February 14-a Akshaya Danda a tliak.

Ni tin ang zelin, February 14, 2025 (Zirtawpni) chawhnuah, Chunara servitor chu biak in chunga Neelachakra-ah Patitapabana Bana emaw flag pui thlak turin a thlen chuan, Akshaya Danda (Patitapabana Bana vawngtu mau ban) chu a tliak tih a hmu a. Chumi hnuah chuan, servitor-te chuan flag chu Neelachakra-ah an phuar bet hram a ni.

Akshaya Danda hetiang taka a tliak hian ringtu tam tak rilru a tina a, lungngaihna nasa tak an lantir a. Chu mai bakah, ringtu tam takin tun hnai hunah chhiatna lo thleng tur chhinchhiahnaah an ngai a ni.

(b) February 16-a biak inah thisen far hmuh a ni; pathian lemte Mahasnana tih a ni.

February 16 (Pathianni)-ah, zing lama sakhaw serh leh sangte, 'Dwara Phita Niti' leh 'Mangala Alati' ang chi te an zawh hnuah, Lalpa Jagannath-a Darshan a tan veleh, rei lo te hnuah, rawngbawltute chuan 'Bhitar Katha' hmunah thisen kai an hmu. He thil thlen hnu lawkah, pathian lemte 'Bada Mahasnana Niti' neih turin ruahmanna siam a ni a, Darshan pawh rei lo te chu tihchawlh a ni.

(c) April 13-ah Patitapabana Bana nen chingpirinu pakhatin a thlawh bosan

April 13, 2025-ah, Puri Shrimandir-a awm Lalpa Jagannath-a zuituten thilmak tak hmuh ngai loh an hmu. Zan dar 5 velah, chingpirinu pakhatin a hmuiin Patitapabana Bana a keng chungin biak in a hual vel hmuh a ni. Thuthangte sawi danin, chingpirinu chuan biak in chu vawi 4-5 vel a hual a, chumi hnuah thlang lam kawngka chungah tuipui lam panin a thlawk.

He thil thlen dan video hi social media-ah a darh nasa hle. Zuitu tam takin an lungngaihna leh an manganna an lantir. Zuitu tam takin thil chhia lo thleng tur nen an sawi zawm a, a thenin Lalpa Jagannath-a hnen atanga thil chhia lo thleng tur entirna mak tak a ni an ti.

(d) April 21-ah biak inah thisen kai hmuh a ni a, pathian lemte Mahasnana neih a ni

April 21 (Thawhtanni)-ah pawh, Shrimandir-a Bhitar Katha bulah thisen kai hmuh a ni a, chumi hnuah Lalpa pathian lemte Mahasnana chu hnam dan angin neih a ni. Chutih laiin, zuitute tan Lalpa Chungnungber Darshan chu darkar 1 vel tihchawlh a ni. Thuthangte sawi danin, thawktute keah darthlalang them a luh avangin biak in chhungah thisen kai hmuh a ni.

(e) September 2-ah Patitapabana Bana chungah chingpirinu pakhatin a thu

Kum hmasa lamah khan Shrimandir chunga Patitapabana Bana-ah chingpirinu a thu hmuh a ni fo tawh. He thil hi a thlen apiangin Odisha-a chanchinbu channel hrang hrangah tihchhuah a ni thin. September 2-ah khan thil thleng ngai bawk chu a thleng leh a, chingpirinu pakhat Neelachakra bulah a thlawk hmuh hmasak a ni a, rei lo te hnuah Neelachakra chunga Patitapabana Bana-ah a thu hmuh a ni.

(f) September 4-ah Aruna Stambha chungah chingpirinu pakhatin a thu leh

Jagannath Biak In chauh ni lovin, Bhavishya Malika chuan biak in Singhadwara hmaa Aruna Stambha-ah chingpirinu emaw thil chhia entirna thil dang emaw a thut chu thil chhia entirna a ni a ti bawk. September 4-ah, chu chu September 2-a Shrimandir chunga chingpirinu hmuh a nih atanga ni hnih hnuah, Aruna Stambha-ah pawh chingpirinu a thu hmuh a ni. Mite pawhin hei hi thil chhia entirnaah an ngai. He thil thleng hmang hian, mi tam takin kum 600 kal taa Malika hrilh lawkna chu a takin a thleng a ni an ti.

(g) September 16-ah biak in chungah chingpirinu pakhatin a thu leh

September 2 atanga ni 15 chhung lekah, September 16 zing hma takah, biak in Patitapabana Bana chungah chingpirinu a thu hmuh leh a ni.

(h) October 14-ah Shrimandir Patitapabana Bana chungah chingpirinu a thu hmuh leh

September thlaah biak in chungah chingpirinu vawi hnih a thu hmuh a ni. He thil thleng hi a tawp mai lo; Puri khuaa chengte chuan October 14-ah he thil thleng hi a thleng nawn leh a ni tih an hmu. Biak in chunga chingpirinu a thut nawn fo chu zuitu tam takin thil chhia lo thleng tur entirnaah an ngai.

(i) December 9-ah Shrimandir chungah chingpirinu tam tak an hual vel

December 7, 2021, leh August 17, 2022-a thleng ang chiahin, December 9, 2025-ah pawh thil chhia entirna nasa tak hmuh a ni a, Jagannath Biak In chungah chingpirinu tam tak an hual vel hmuh a ni. He thil thleng hian Puri leh Odisha-a zuitute chauh ni lovin, Odisha pawn lama chengte pawh a hip hle. Zuitu tam takin he thil thleng hi hun lo awm turah thil chhia lian tak lo thleng tur entirnaah an ngai.

7.4 Kum 2025-ah sakhaw hmun pawimawh dang leh biak in atangin thil chhia entirna hmuh

Ujjain-a Mahakaleshwar Biak In compound-ah kangmei chhuak

May 5, 2025, chhun dar 12-ah, Ujjain-a khawvel hriat lar Baba Mahakaleshwar Biak In compound-a Shankh Dwara bulah boruak bawlhhlawh venna board control room-ah kangmei a chhuak. He thil thlen hnuah, biak in compound-ah buaina a chhuak. Kangmei a chhuah hnuah, zuitute biak in luh chu rei lo te tihchawlh a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a Peetha compound-ah kangmei chhuak

Devasnana Purnima ni, June 11, 2025-ah, Cuttack, Nischintakoili-a awm Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a Nemal Padmabana Peetha compound-ah kangmei a chhuak chu Malika zuituten thil chhia entirna nasa takah an ngai bawk. Zuitu sang tam tak zingah hlauhna boruak a awm a, compound-a hlan khawnvar dah chu thut thutah a kang a, rei lo teah meialh nasa tak a chhuak.

Sawrkarin Nemal banyan thingkung a sah thlak

August bul lamah, Malika lehkhabute ziaktu bulpui, sakhaw mi ropui Shri Achyutananda Das-a Sadhana Peetha-a biak in hmaa banyan thingkung upa takte chu a tam zawk sah thlak a ni. Sawrkarin Achyutananda Das Peetha hmasawnna hna thawh nan he hma hi a la niin an sawi. He thil hi khawtlang mite leh puithiamte chuan nasa takin an dodal a, an dodalna avangin banyan thingkung zung tlemte chauh sah lohvin a awm. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan 'Nemal Banyan Thingkung' hnuaiah ngaihtuahna fim takin Malika lehkhabute hi a ziak a ni tih hriat tur a ni. Nemal banyan thingkung sah thlak chu Malika zuituten thil chhia entirna nasa takah an ngai.

Mahakaleshwar Biak In-a chungnungbera dah hnam puanzar rangkachak a tla

June 1, 2025-ah, Ujjain-a khawvel hriat lar Mahakaleshwar Biak In atangin thil chhia entirna hmuh leh a ni. Thawklehkhatah, thli na tak avangin Mahakaleshwar Biak In chunga hnam puanzar rangkachak chu a tla thla. Biak in puithiam chuan he thil thleng hi sakhaw lam atanga ngaihtuahna thlen tur a ni a ti.

Mahakaleshwar Biak In-a Shivalinga-a Lalpa Shiva hmai tuamna siam a tliak a, a tla

August 18-ah Ujjain-a Mahakal Biak In-ah thilmak tak a thleng. Hetah hian, ni tin zan dar 8-ah Shivalinga chei (Shringara) laiin, Bhang (cannabis paste) hmangin Lalpa Shiva hmai tuamna siam a ni thin. Bhang tam tak hman a ni. Chumi hnu lawkah, Aarti tan dawn chiahin Bhang Shringara chu a tliak a, a tla thla. Lalpa Shiva hmai tuamna atangin hnar, hmui leh mit pakhat a tliak a, a tla thla.

Maa Samaleswari Biak In-ah thilmak tak hmuh

Odisha, Jharsuguda, Lakhanpur-a awm Maa Samaleswari Biak In hriat lar tak atangin, August 21, 2025, Ningani zanah, zuituten Pathiannu chu a lem atanga chhuak a ni tih entirna an hmu. Puithiamin zing hma takah 'Mangala Alati' atan biak in kawngka a hawnin, Pathiannu ke pên chhuak lam hawi, Alta (rawng sen) hmanga rawng hnawih chu hmuh a ni a, chumi rualin, Pathiannu lemah bet a khandaih leh a fei (Trishul) chu a sirah tla hmuh a ni.

'Bhavishya Malika' sawi danin, Kali Yuga tawp lamah, Dharma din hma in, Pathiannu zawng zawngte chu an hmun theuh atangin an chhuak ang a, mi sualte leh mi thil tih chhiate tihboral hna an thawk ang. He thil thleng hi chu hun a thleng tawh a, hrilh lawkna chu a takin a thleng a ni tih finfiahna a ni.

Brahmagiri Jagannath Temple-a Pathian milim thianghlimte kangmei chhuak

August 28, 2025-ah, Odisha-a Puri district hnuaia Kandagoda khuaa awm Brahmagiri Jagannath Temple-ah Lalpa Jagannath, u Balabhadra, leh nu Subhadra milimte chu kangmei a chhuak. Zan inkhawm zawh hnuah puithiamte an in lamah an haw a. Zan dar 8:30-ah, temple atanga khu chhuak vangin temple kawngkhar an tichhiat chuan, chhung lama milimte chu an kang tih hmuh a ni. Muktimandapa an rawn hnuah, chu mi ni atang chuan Shaligrama chu biak a ni. Nabakalebara lo thleng turah Daru (thing thianghlim) an lak khawm hnuah, milim thar an dah a ni.

Lingaraj Temple atanga chhiatna lo thleng tur chhinchhiahna hmuh, temple-a hnam puanzar ban a tliak

Thawhtanni, September 15, 2025-ah, ruah nasa tak leh thli tleh vanga Bhubaneswar-a awm, kum zabi 11-naa sak Lingaraj Temple-a hnam puanzar ban chu a tliak. Thawhlehni zingah, rawngbawltute'n an hmuh hnuah, ban thar chungah hnam puanzar an zar a ni.

Bhubaneswar-a awm Lingaraj Temple chhipa hnam puanzar chu ruah nasa tak leh thli vangin a chhe ta.

Temple-a hnam puanzar chu thli vangin a chhe a, kawltheh vangin temple-a CCTV camera leh eng system chu a chhe bawk.

(j) Kum 500 chhung a chanchinah a vawi khat nan, Vrindavan-a Banke Bihari Temple-ah Bal Bhog hlan a ni lo

Kum zanga vel kaltaah lo lang Thakur Banke Bihari chu ni khatah vawi li Bhog (chaw hlan) hlan thin a ni. Zing Darshan atana thutna an siam hmain, Bal Bhog hlan a ni. Chhunah temple a khar hmain, Raj Bhog hlan a ni a, zanah kawngkhar an hawn chuan, Utthapan Bhog hlan a ni. Kawngkhar an khar chuan, Shayan Bhog hlan a ni. Bhog siam hna chu confectioner (Halwai) hnenah pek a ni. Confectioner chuan Bhog chu a siam a, rawngbawltute hnenah a pe a, anni chuan Bhog chu an hlan thin.

Mahse December 15 zing dar 8:45-ah, temple kawngkhar chu a inhawng a, mahse confectioner chu Bal Bhog nen a lo thleng lo. An biak chuan, thla tin pek tur an pek loh vangin confectioner chu a lo thleng lo tih hmuh a ni. Kum 500 chhung a chanchinah a vawi khat nan, chu mi ni chuan Banke Bihari Ji hnenah Bal Bhog hlan theih a ni lo.

7.5 Hun remchang loah Rath Yatra buatsaih

Kan hriat theuh angin, Rath Yatra chu kum tin Ashadha thla Shukla Paksha Dwitiya Tithi-ah buatsaih thin a ni. He thil tih dan hi India leh ram dang Jagannath temple dangah, Puri-a Jagannath Temple telin, vawng reng a ni. Mahse kum hmasa tlemte chhung khan, ISKCON-in he thil tih dan hi an bawhchhe fo tih hmuh a ni.

He thil chungchangah hian, Shree Jagannath Temple Administration-a Chief Administrator leh Shree Jagannath Temple Managing Committee Chairman, Gajapati Maharaj Divyasingha Deva-in an hnial hnuah leh Mayapur-a awm ISKCON headquarters-ah lehkha ziakin an hek hnuah, tihdanglamna thenkhat chu a awm ngei a, mahse ISKCON chu India-ah leh ram dangah, hun remchang loah Rath Yatra an buatsaih fo tih hmuh a ni.

Hei hi chhunzawmin, ISKCON chuan November 23, 2025-ah Taiwan-ah Rath Yatra an buatsaih a, chu chu khawvel hmun hrang hrang Odia mipuite leh Odisha-a Jagannath Pathian mi tam takte'n an sawisel. Pathian mite chuan thil tih dan hlui bawhchhiatna mai ni lovin, Lalpa Jagannath thupek ngaihthahnaah an ngai bawk.


7.6 Shrimandir Administration-in an chhut 2026 English calendar-a tihsual

Tun hnaiah, Jagannath Temple Administration-in an tichhuah 2026 English calendar-ah tihsual lian tak hmuhchhuah a ni. A takah chuan, calendar-ah Ratnabedi thlalak chhut a ni a, chutah chuan Pathian pathumte dinhmun chu a dik lo. En tute ngaihdanah chuan, calendar thlalakah chuan, Lalpa Jagannath chu dinglam tawp lama dah a ni a, chu mi hnuah Pathiannu Subhadra, chu mi hnuah u Balabhadra. A dik takah chuan, Lalpa Balabhadra chu dinglam tawp lama awm tur a ni a, chu mi hnuah Pathiannu Subhadra, chu mi hnuah Shri Jagannath. Chu chu, calendar-ah chuan, u Balabhadra thlalak chu Lalpa Jagannath hmunah chhut a ni a, Lalpa Jagannath thlalak chu Lalpa Balabhadra hmunah chhut a ni. Chu bakah, calendar-a Ratnasinghasana thlalakah Chakraraj Sudarshan hmun pek a ni lo. Chu mai bakah, calendar-ah Rath Yatra thlalak chhut a ni a, chutah chuan Nu Subhadra tawlailir hmasa berin, chu mi hnuah Lalpa Chungnungber Jagannath tawlailir, a tawpah Lalpa Balabhadra tawlailir a lang.

Heng tihsualte vang hian, Pathian mite rilruah lungawilohna leh thinrimna a awm. Jagannath Temple-a rawngbawltute pawh he thil avang hian an thinrim hle. Pathian mi thenkhatte chuan, hei hi Lalpa Jagannath ngeiin chhiatna lo thleng tur leh kum tharah khawvel dinhmun inthlak danglam tur chhinchhiahna a ni an ti.

8 Lalpa Kalki pianna – Engtikah leh khawi hmunah?

Bhavishya Malika-in a sawi danin, Lalpa Kalki chu a piang tawh. Lalpa Kalki chauh ni lovin, Mahabharata hun laia mi huaisen zawng zawngte, Yudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babruvahana, Ekalavya, Ghatotkacha, Duryodhana, Shakuni, leh midangte pawh hi khawvelah hian an piang nawn vek tawh. Malika-in a sawi danin, tun tumah hian heng mi huaisen zawng zawngte hi Dharma lamah Adharma dovin an tangrual ang.

Mahse, hetaah hian mi huaisen dangte kan sawi lo vang a, Parahbrahma Narayana Mahavishnu Lalpa Kalki Ram chauh kan sawi ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯୋଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Nīḷachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṅgā adhara,
yojanagre janama hoibe Biṣṇusharmā gṛuhara.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Puri Jagannath Temple-a Patitapabana Bana (hnam puanzar) chu a hmun atanga a thlawh bo a, hmun dangah a kal chuan, chu mi hunah chuan, Odisha-a Jajanagar emaw Jajpur-ah Lalpa Vishnu fak hla sa thin Brahmin chhungkuaah Mahaprabhu Kalki chu a piang ang.

Chhiartute hnenah hian he thil hi Jagannath Temple atanga vawi tam tak hmuh tawh a ni tih ka hrilhfiah tur a ni, entirnan, kum 1990-91 chhungin, kum 2005 chhungin, chu mi hnuah kum 2019 leh 2020-a Cyclone Fani leh Cyclone Amphan hun laiin, leh a dangte pawh.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabuah he thil thlen hnuah chauh Mahaprabhu Kalki a piang ang tih a ziak. He thil hi a thleng tawh avangin, Lalpa Kalki chu a piang tawh tih chiang takin sawi theih a ni. Mahse he thil hi kum hrang hrangah a thleng tawh avangin, he thil atang ringawt hian Mahaprabhu pian kum dik tak hriat theih a ni lo.

Mahaprabhu pian kum hriat nan, Malika-a chang dangte puihna kan mamawh ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Tera Janma Sharana’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।
କେଉଁ ଦିନ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ କହେ ରାମଦାସ ଯହିଁ,
ବିଶାଖାପାଟଣା ନୃପ ନାଶ ହେଲେ ମୁହିଁ ବିକାଶ ହୁଅଇ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Māgha saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājatwa karibe ehi Ekāmra nagara.
Keuñ dina tumbhe prakāsha hoiba kahe Rāmadāsa yahiñ,
Viśākhāpāṭaṇā nṛpa nāśa hele muhiñ bikāsha huai.

Awmzia:
Chung changte hmang chuan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a zirtir Ramadas hnenah, Puri-ah Shri Dibyasingha Deb hming pu lal a awm hunah, chu mi hunah chuan Ananta Keshari, chu chu Mahaprabhu Kalki, chuan Ekamra Nagar emaw Bhubaneswar-ah ro a rel ang. Magha thla ni sarih (Saptami Tithi) a ni ang. Mahapurush chuan chu mi hunah chuan, Lalpa hnenah ka awm ang a, ka thang lian ang tih a sawi bawk. Hei hi Visakhapatnam Lal (chu chu Andhra Pradesh Chief Minister) a thih hunah a thleng ang.

Kan ngun taka kan ngaihtuah chuan, chhinchhiahna hmang hian, Mahapurush chuan Andhra Pradesh Chief Minister, Y.S. Rajasekhara Reddy thihna, kum 2009-a helicopter chetsualna vanga thleng kha a sawi a ni tih kan hmu ang. Chung chang atang chuan, Mahapurush chuan Lalpa pian kum a sawi lo, mahse kum 2009 velah Kalki leela (Pathian thiltih) chu a chhunzawm zel ang tih a sawi chiang a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ‘Jaiphula Malika’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଚୈତନ୍ୟ,
ଜନ୍ମିଛନ୍ତି ଆସି ଏହି ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସତଚାଳିଶକୁ ତୁହି ଘେନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Sehi Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Chaitanya,
janmichhanti āsi ehi martyeṇa lo jāiphula,
satachāḷishaku tuhi ghena.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lalpa, Shri Krishna leh Shri Chaitanya anga lo kal tawh kha, Kalki anga, number 47-ah khawvelah a lo kal ang.

He number 47 hi Puri Lal, Shri Dibyasingha Deb ta a ni tih hriat a ni a, chu chu kum 2005 leh 2008 inkarah a awm.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kalpatika’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଦ୍ରି ଛାଡି ପ୍ରଭୁ ପୀତବାସ,
ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟରେ ଜନ୍ମ ଲଭିବେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।
ଗୁଣରେ ଭେଦି ଭେଦ ଅଙ୍କେ ଜାଣ,
ଦେଲେ ଯେତେ ସାଲ ହେଲା ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ଏହି ସାଲରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିବେ,
ଅଙ୍କ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଯେ କହିଲୁ ଏବେ।
ବିଂଶ ରେବ ଅଙ୍କ ଯେବଣ ଦିନ,
ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଉଦେ ଗଗନ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ଲକ୍ଷଣ ଦେଖିବୁ ଯେବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇଥିବେ ତେବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Nīḷādri chhāḍi prabhu pītabāsa,
martyare janma labhibe abashya.
guṇare bhedi bheda aṅke jāṇa,
dele jete sāla helā pramāṇa.
ehi sālare prabhu janamibe,
aṅka pramāṇa je kahilu ebe.
biṃsha reba aṅka jebaṇa dina,
indu pare bindu ude gagana.
emanta lakṣaṇa dekhibu jebe,
prabhu janma hoithibe tebe.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahaprabhu chuan a Jagannath chenna hmun a kalsan hnuah khawvelah a lo piang ngei ang. Mahaprabhu pian kum a sawi pahin, Mahapurush chuan Revati Nakshatra number (27) ah sawmhnih (20) i belh chuan, a lo chhuak number chu Lalpa pian kumah ngaih tur a ni a ti. Chu chu, 20+27 = 47.

He number hi Puri Lal, Dibyasingha Deb-a rorel kum (Anka) a ni tih hriat a ni a, chu chu kum 2005 leh 2008 inkar kum pakhat a kawk.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu pakhatah heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଥାମାନ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଘଟିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ବାନା ଧରି ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଧରିଣ କଳକୀ ବେଶ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ehi kathāmāna nishchaya ghaṭiba Dibyasiṃha pañchachāḷisha,
shūnya bānā dhari āsibe Shrī Hari dhariṇa Kaḷakī besha.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Puri-a Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb-a kum 45-na a nih hunah, Lalpa Shri Hari chuan Kalki anga lo piangin khawvelah a lo kal ang.

Hriat tur pawimawh tak chu Puri Maharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb-a kum 45-na hi kum 2005 leh 2008 inkar kum a kawk a ni.

Achyutananda Das Ji chuan a Malika lehkhabuah a ziak nawn leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତି ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ହୋଇବ ସଂସାର,
କାହାରି ନ ଥିବ କୁଳ ବିଚାର।
ବହୁତ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସଂସାରେ ହେବ,
ନିର୍ଭୟ ହୋଇ ଜଣେ କେ ନଥିବ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶେ,
ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କଳକୀ ବେଶେ।
ନାଶିବେ ଯାବତ୍ ଅବନୀଭାର,
ଏଇଠୁ ହୋଇବ ପୃଥବୀ ସଂହାର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ati anyāya hoiba saṃsāra,
kāhāri na thiba kuḷa bichāra.
bahuta utpāta saṃsāre heba,
nirbhaya hoi jaṇe ke nathiba.
Dibyasiṃha deba pañchachāliśe,
āsibe Śrī Hari Kaḷakī beśe.
nāśibe jābat abanībhāra,
eithu hoiba pṛuthabī saṃhāra.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Kaliyuga tawp lamah, khawvelah hian dik lohna tam tak a awm ang. Tuman chhungkua an ngaihtuah lovang a, Dharma pawh an zui hek lovang. Hmun tinah buaina a awm ang; tuman thlamuang leh hlim takin an hun an hmang lovang. Chumi hunah chuan, Dibyasingha Deb tia hming pu lal kum 45-naah (2005-2008 inkarah), Lal Shri Hari chuan Kalki anga he khawvelah hian a lo kal ang. Lei atanga sual phurrit a la bo vang a, mi sualte a tiboral ang a, 'Mlechhas' ho a tichimih ang.

Mahapurusha-te chuan Lal Kalki lo pian tur thu hi lehkhabu hrang hrangah hmun tam takah an sawi a ni.

A Malika lehkhabu dang, ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ tihah chuan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ବିରଜା ପୀଠ ବୈତରଣୀ ନଦୀ,
ଦଶାଶନୀ ଘାଟ ଉଦୟ ଭେଦି।
ସପତ ମାତୃକା ଟେକିଅଛି ଶିର,
ତାଙ୍କ ଅଧରୁ ବହିବ ରୁଧିର।
ସେହି ସଂକେତକୁ ଜାଣିଥାଅ ରାମ,
କଳଙ୍କି ଉଦୟ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Birajā pīṭha Baitaraṇī nadī,
Dashāshanī ghāṭa udaya bhedi.
Sapata mātṛkā ṭekiachhi shira,
