tāṅka adharu bahiba rudhira.
Sehi saṅketaku jāṇithāa Rāma,
Kaḷaṅki udaya nishchaya jāṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Odisha state-a Jajpur district-a awm Biraja Temple bulah Baitarani Lui a luang a. Chumi Baitarani Lui Dashashwamedha Ghat bulah chuan Saptamatruka temple a awm a, chutah chuan Nu Pathian pasarih – Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, leh Narasimhi – an awm a ni.

Mahapurush chuan a ziak a ni, Saptamatruka Nu-te hmui, a nih loh leh ka atanga thisen a chhuah hunah, chu chu chhinchhiahna a ni ang a, chu mi atang chuan Pathian betute chuan Lal Kalki a piang tawh emaw, a pian hun a hnai tawh hle emaw tih an hrethiam ang.

Hriat lar tak chu he thil hi Saptamatruka Temple-ah a thleng tawh a, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a hrilh lawkna chu a dik tih a lantir a ni.

Jajpur-a Biraja Temple leh Saptamatruka Temple puithiamte leh chuta khawsa mite sawi danin, Saptamatruka Nu-te ka atanga thisen chhuak hi kum 2005-ah a thleng tawh a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ tihah a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେ ଦିନ ବିରଜା ଆସ୍ଥାନ ଛୋଡ଼ିବ ଭୀମ ସିଂହନାଦ ହେବ,
ଗର୍ଭସ୍ଥିତ ଶିଶୁଭୂମିରେ ପଡ଼ିବେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ଯିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Je dina Birajā āsthāna choḍiba bhīma siṃhanāda heba,
garbhasthita śiśubhūmire paḍibe Bhubaneśwaraku jiba.

Awmzia:
Hetah hian, Mahapurush chuan Mahaprabhu pian hun tur chu entirna hmangin a sawifiah a ni. A ziak dan chuan, Maa Biraja a thutna atanga a chhuah ni chuan, chumi hunah chuan naupang pakhat (Lal Kalki) chu a nu chhul chhungah a lut ang, chu naupang chu nakinah Bhubaneswar-ah a kal ang.

Hriat lar tak chu Maa Biraja chuan kum 2004-ah a thutna a chhuahsan a ni. September 2004 thlaah chuan, mi thenkhat, politics lama inrawlh chuan an rualin Maa Biraja lem (Jajpur-a Biraja Temple-a dah) chu an ru a. Thla 6 vel hnuah, March 2005-ah chuan, Nu lem chu temple-ah a kir leh a ni.

Chung chunga châng ruk atang chuan, Mahaprabhu Kalki a piang tawh tih chiang takin a lang a, a hun chu kum 2005-2008 vel a ni. Chuvangin, chhiartu zawng zawngte chuan Kalki avatar hi tunah hian Lei chungah a awm mek tih hria se.

8.1 Mahaprabhu hi khawiah nge a lo pian?

Puranas-te sawi danin, Lal Kalki pianna hmun chu Sambhal khua a ni. Bhavishya Malika pawhin Lal Kalki chu Sambhal khuaah a piang ang tih a sawi bawk. He thil chungchangah hian mi zawng zawng an inrem a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’ tihah a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ କଷଣ ଦେଖି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଭଗବାନ,
କଳିରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇଣ।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ପିତାଙ୍କର ନାମଟି ଅଟଇ,
ମାତା ନାମ ସୁମତି ଯେ କହିଲୁ ବୁଝାଇ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhaktaṅka kaṣaṇa dekhi prabhu bhagabāna,
Kaḷire Sambaḷa nagre janama hoiṇa.
Biṣṇusharmā pitāṅkara nāmaṭi aṭai,
mātā nāma Sumati je kahilu bujhāi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Kali hun tawp lama Pathian betute harsatna hmuin, Lal Kalki chu Sambal Nagar-ah a piang ang. Mahaprabhu pa hming chu Vishnusharma a ni ang a, a nu hming chu Sumati a ni ang.

Mahapurush chuan ‘Chakada Madana’ tihah a ziak belh leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ହେବେ ଜନମ,
ଛତିଆ ବଟରେ ଲାଗିବ ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି ସଂଗ୍ରାମ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Sambaḷa nagare prabhu se gupte hebe janama,
Chhatiā baṭare lāgiba dibārātri saṅgrāma.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Mahaprabhu Kalki chu Sambal Nagar-ah rûk takin a piang ang. Chhatia Bata-ah chuan chhun leh zan indona a chhuak ang.

Achyutananda Das Ji chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’ tihah a ziak nawn leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ମାରୁ ମହରଗ ରାଜକର ଆସି ଘରେ ଘରେ ପଶିଯିବ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ କଳ୍କୀ ଉଦୟ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Māru maharaga rājakara āsi ghare ghare paśijiba,
bāraṇa beḷare Sambaḷanagre Kaḷkī udaya hoiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, thihna, tam, leh chhiahin in tin a luhchilh a, buaina hmun tinah a darh hunah, Lal Kalki chu Kaliyuga tawp hunah chuan Sambal Nagar-ah a lo lang ang.

Mahapurush chuan ‘Chakada Madana’ tihah a ziak belh leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

କହନ୍ତି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଭକତ ମୋ ଗଳା ମାଳ,
ଭକତ ନିମନ୍ତେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ ହେବି କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kahanti Achyuta śuṇa Rāmadāsa bhakata mo gaḷā māḷa,
bhakata nimante Sambaḷanagre hebi Kaḷkī abatāra.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ tihah a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

କଳକୀର ସୀମା କାଳ ପୂରିଗଲେ କଳ୍କୀ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଯଶା ପୁରେ,
ସମ୍ବଳ ଗ୍ରାମରେ ଜାତ ହୋଇଥିବେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାର କାଳରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷakīra sīmā kāḷa pūrigale Kaḷkī Biṣṇujashā pure,
Sambaḷa grāmare jāta hoithibe mlechha saṃhāra kāḷare.

Awmzia:
"Kaliyuga hun a tawp hunah chuan, Kalki chu Sambal khuaa Vishnuyasha inah a piang ang Mlechhas-te tihboral nan."

Nimahsela, 'Sambal' hi khawiah nge a awm tih hi tun thlengin mi tam tak rilruah inhnialna a la ni. He thuziakah hian, hei hi kan chinfel ang.

8.2 Bhavishya Malika chuan he thil chungchangah hian eng nge a sawi tak tak?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Jaiphula Malika’ tihah a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ବେଳ କାଳ ଜାଣି,
ଓଡିଶାରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନ୍ମିବେ ପୁଣି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କେହି ତାଙ୍କ ମାୟାକୁ ନ ଚିହ୍ନି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Sehi beḷa kāḷa jāṇi,
Oḍishāre prabhu janmibe puṇi lo jāiphula,
kehi tāṅka māyāku na chinhi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, chumi hunah chuan sual a san ber hunah, Lal Kalki chu Odisha state-ah a piang ang. Lalpa hrilhhai theihna chu a chak hle ang a, tuman an hre chhuak thei lovang.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu pakhatah a ziak nawn leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁରେ କଳ୍କୀର ଜନମ,
ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ରାଜହଂସ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଜନମି ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ଏହା କହିଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ୍ୟ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Utkala bhuiñre Kaḷkīra janama,
grāma nāma Rājahansa.
Kaḷkī janami līḷā prakāśiba,
ehā kahili prakāshya.

Awmzia:
"Kalki pianna hmun chu Utkala (Odisha) ramah a ni ang, Rajahansa tia hming pu khuaah. A pian hnuah, Kalki chuan a Pathian leela a lantir ang."

Tunah chuan, Odisha-ah khawiah nge Lal Kalki a pian ang tih chungchangah, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ngei chuan a Malika lehkhabu dang, ‘Bhabishya Jatipataka Gita’ tihah a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ନଦୀ ତୀର,
ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁବିପ୍ର ଦ୍ୱିଜବର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Oḍiśā deśare gupta Gaṅgā nadī tīra,
Sambaḷa nagre Biṣṇubipra dwijabara.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Mahaprabhu pianna hmun chu Odisha-a Sambal Nagar-a Gupta Ganga (Baitarani Lui) kam bulah a awm ang. Chumi hmunah ngei chuan, Lal Vishnu fak hla sa thin Brahmin mi fel tak inah, Mahaprabhu Kalki chu a piang ang.

Chung chunga châng pahnih atang chuan, Bhavishya Malika sawi danin, chiang takin a lang a ni, Lal Kalki chu Sambal-ah a piang ang a, chu chu India state Odisha-ah a awm a ni.

8.3 Sambal hi Odisha-ah khawiah nge a awm?

Panchasakha zinga mi pakhat, Mahapurush Jagannath Das chuan, a Malika lehkhabu ‘Bhabishyat Parardha’ tihah a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରରେ,
ଦୁଇ ଜଣ ଅଛନ୍ତି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଘରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Jājapura Sambaḷa nagrare,
dui jaṇa achhanti brāhmaṇa ghare.

Awmzia:
Chung chunga chângah chuan, Mahapurush chuan a ziak a ni – “Jajapura Sambala nagrare”. Chu chu a awmzia chu, “Jajpur-a Sambal Nagar-ah” tih a ni. Sambal Nagar hi Jajpur-ah a awm tih a chiang a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ tihah a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷୀର ନଦୀ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଯେ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ଖ୍ୟାତ ଯେ କମଳପୁର ଗୁପ୍ତ ଯେ ସମ୍ବଳ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kṣhīra nadī dakṣhiṇa je Birajā maṇḍala,
khyāta je Kamalapura gupta je Sambaḷa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Khira lui chhim lamah leh Mahanadi hmar lamah chuan Biraja temple hmun a awm a. He Biraja hmun bulah hian Kamalapur tia hming pu khua a awm a ni. Hei hi rûk takin Sambal khua tia koh a ni.

He châng hmang hian, Mahapurush chuan Sambal hi Odisha-a Biraja hmunah emaw Jajpur district-ah emaw a awm tih a tarlang a ni.

Sambal hi Odisha-ah khawi laiah nge a awm tih, chang dangte hmangin i hrethiam ang u.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Gurubhakti Gita’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘର,
ଜନମିଣ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଚକ୍ରଧର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Biṣṇusharmā ghara,
janamiṇa tapasyā karibe Chakradhara.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Chakradhara (Lalpa Vishnu) chu Sambal Nagar, Gaya-a cheng Pathian mi inah a piang ang a, chumi hnuah chuan inthiarfihlimna a nei ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kalikalpa Gita’-ah heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଜାତ,
ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Biṣṇusharmā gruhe prabhu hoibe jāta,
Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Harihara kṣhetra.

Awmzia:
"Lalpa chu Sambal Nagar, Gaya-a Harihara biala Vishnusharma inah a piang ang."

Chung chang hmasa pahnih atang chuan, Lalpa pianna chu Sambal Nagar, Gaya-ah a ni ang. Tunah chuan, chu Gaya chu eng Gaya nge ni ang a, Odisha-ah khawi laiah nge a awm tih chu Lalpa Krishna ngeiin a Gopas sawm leh pahnihte hrilhin, chu chu Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Biraja Mahatmya’-ah a tarlang a ni. Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak a –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାର ସୁତ ନିହାର ବଚନ ଏ ଅଟେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଠାର,
ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥେ ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଗ୍ରାମଟି ସମ୍ବଳ ପୁର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Śuṇa bāra suta nihāra bachana e aṭe Achyuta ṭhāra,
Nābhigayā tīrthe Harihara kṣhetra grāmaṭi Sambaḷa pura.

Awmzia:
"Aw fapa sawm leh pahnih, ngaithla ula, hei hi Achyuta thu chiang tak a ni: Sambalpur khua chu Nabhigaya hmun thianghlimah, Harihara bialah a awm a ni."

Chu chu, Harihara bial chu Nabhigaya hmun thianghlimah a awm a, chu chu Sambal an ti a ni.

Chung sawihona hmasateah khan, Mahapurush chuan Mahaprabhu pianna hmun chu Biraja bial emaw, Gaya (Nabhigaya) hmun thianghlim emaw angin a sawi thin tih hmun tam takah kan hmu a. Tunah chuan, heng bialte hi khawi laiah nge an awm tak tak, an chanchin hluiah eng thil nge an rin, leh an pawimawhna chu eng nge ni tih kan hria ang.

8.3.1 Biraja Bial

'Biraja Temple' hi Jajpur, Odisha-a Baitarani Lui kama awm biak in lian tak a ni a, chutah chuan Adi Shakti chu 'Biraja' hmelin an bia thin. A takah chuan, hmasang laiin, Brahma Ji ngeiin Brahmakunda-ah Yajna (inthawina) a lo nei tawh a, chumi hnuah chuan Nu Parvati chu inthawina meiah a lo chhuak a, Brahma Ji chu 'Biraja' hmingin amah din turin a hrilh a ni. Chu bial chu Pathiannu Biraja hming chawiin Biraja bial an ti bawk.

He hmunah tak hian Pathiannu Sati dul (Nabhi) a tla a, chu chuan Shakti Peethas 51 zinga pakhat a nihtir a ni. Hei hi Pathiannu chu Mahishasura Mardini hmelin, ban pahnih neiin an biakna biak in awm chhun a ni.

He hmun hi Pinda Daan (thlahtute hlan) atan Nabhigaya tia hriat lar a ni bawk. A takah chuan, Pinda Daan an neihna Shakti Peetha awm chhun a ni. Brahmakunda bulah chuan Nabhikunda tih tuikhur thuk tak a awm a, chu tui hmang chuan mite chuan an thlahtute tan Pinda Daan an nei thin. He hmun hi Skanda Purana-ah pawh tarlan a ni.

8.3.2 Nabhigaya

Puranas-ah chuan Satya Yuga-ah khan Gayasura hming pu ramhuai pakhat a awm tih tarlan a ni. Ramhuai chi atanga piang ni mah se, a thinlung chu Dharma leh inthiarfihlimna chuan a chawk tho a. Pathian ngaihsak tak, thiltithei em em, taksa lian tak nei a ni. Khawvel hmasawnna leh midangte tan a thawk thin. Puranas-ah chuan ramhuai chuan inthiarfihlimna nasa tak a nei a, chu chuan Lalpa Vishnu a tilawm a, Lalpa chuan malsawmna dil turin a hrilh a ni. Gayasura chuan malsawmna danglam tak a dil a. Ani chuan, “Ka khawih ringawt pawhin mihringte chu vanah an thleng theih nan,” a ti a. Lalpa Vishnu chuan a inthiarfihlimna nasa tak hmuin he malsawmna hi a pe a ni. Mahse, chu mi avang chuan Yamaloka (mitthi khawvel) chu a ruak tan a, Gayasura khawih apiang chu vanah an kal nghal vek vang a ni. Hei hi pathiante leh Yamraj tan harsatna lian tak a lo ni ta a. Pathian zawng zawngte chu Trinity (Brahma, Vishnu, Mahesh) hnenah an kal a, he harsatna chinfelna tur zawn turin an ngen a ni. Pathiante chuan Trinity chu Gayasura dang turin an ngen a. Trinity chuan Gayasura, fel tak ni chung pawha thilsiam dan kalhmang tibuaitu chu dang turin an rel a. An pathum chuan Brahmin pathum hmel an pu a, Gayasura hnenah an kal a, ni sarih chhung Yajna neih chhunzawm an duh thu leh chu mi atan chuan hmun zau leh thianghlim an mamawh thu an hrilh a. Gayasura chu Yajna atan a taksa pe turin an dil a, mahse ni sarih chhung chu a chet loh tur thu an hrilh a. Gayasura chuan chu chu a remti a. Gayasura chuan a lu chu Gaya (Bihar)-ah a pharh a, chutah chuan Lalpa Vishnu a thu a, a dul chu Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha)-ah a dah a, chutah chuan Brahma Ji a thu a, a ke chu Pithapuram (Andhra Pradesh)-ah a dah a, chutah chuan Lalpa Shiva a thu a, chutiang chuan Yajna chu a tan ta a ni. Trinity chuan ni sarih chhung Yajna chu an nei chhunzawm a.

Gayasura chuan zingah ar khuan ri hriain ni a chhiar thin. Hei hi hriain, ni hnuhnung berah chuan Lalpa Shiva chuan ar hmel a pu a, zan a la tawp hmain a khuang a. Ar khuan ri hriain, Gayasura chuan zing a ni tawh emaw a ti a, ni sarih a tawp tawh emaw tiin a taksa a chet a. Trinity chuan a hmaah an lo lang nghal a, Gayasura chuan ni sarih chhung a taksa chet loh tura a thutiam a bawhchhia tih an hrilh a. Thutiam bawhchhiat man chu thihna a ni an ti a. Gayasura fel tak chuan phun miah lovin chu chu a pawm a. Trinity chuan a Pathian ngaihsakna avangin an lawm a, malsawmna dil turin an hrilh a. Chutah Gayasura chuan a hming chu Trinity hming nen hriat reng a nih theih nan a ngen a. A taksa chu Brahmin-te silhfenin an chei reng turin a ngen bawk a. Heng hmun pathumah hian tu thlahtute pawh hlan (Pinda Daan leh Tarpan) an nih chuan, an piang nawn tawh loh nan malsawmna a dil bawk.

Gayasura duhthusam pawmin, Lalpa Vishnu chuan Gaya-ah Gadadhar hmel a pu a, Brahma Ji chu Nabhigaya-ah Girija Devi hmelin an bia a, Lalpa Shiva chu Pithapuram-ah Kukkuteshwar (ar hmel pu Lalpa) angin an bia a ni. Chutiang chuan, heng hmun pathumte hi Pinda Daan leh Tarpan atan an lo lar ta a ni:

  • Shirogaya (Gaya, Bihar)
  • Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha)
  • Padagaya (Pithapuram, Andhra Pradesh)

Nabhigaya hmun thianghlim chu Jajpur-ah a awm tih finfiahna chu Panchasakha Mahapurushas-te Malika lehkhabuteah pawh hmuh theih a ni.

Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah chuan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁରଠାରେ ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥ ଏକଥା ଜାଣ ସତ୍ୱର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Jājapurathāre Nābhigayā tīrtha ekathā jāṇa satwara.

Awmzia:
"Hei hi chiang takin hria ang che, Nabhigaya hmun thianghlim chu Jajpur-ah a awm a ni."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan, kum 600 vel kaltaah a pian nawnna sawmna tur a hrilh lawk laiin, Malika lehkhabu ‘Kalikalpa Gita’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶମ ଜନମ ମୋହର ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଯାଜପୁର ନାଭିଗୟା ହରିହର ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Daśama janama mohara hoiba prakāśa,
Jājapura Nābhigayā Harihara khyāta.

Awmzia:
"Ka pian nawnna sawmna chu Nabhigaya, Jajpur-a Harihara bial hmingthang takah a lo lang ang."

He chang hmang hian pawh, Nabhigaya hmun thianghlim chu Jajpur-ah a awm tih a chiang a ni.

Mahapurush chuan he thupui bawk hi a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kalikalpa Gita’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ପଞ୍ଚକୋଶ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ନାଭିଗୟା ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘରେ ହେବ ଖେଳ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Jājapura pañchakośa Birajā maṇḍala,
Nābhigayā Biṣṇusharmā ghare heba kheḷa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Biraja bial chu Jajpur atanga kosha panga huam chhungah a awm a ni. Lalpa Kalki chu chu Biraja bial, Nabhigaya-a cheng Vishnu Pathian mi inah a piang ang.

Chuvangin, sawihona hmasa atang khan, Panchasakhas-te’n Lalpa Kalki pianna tur an lo hrilh lawkna Sambal Nagar, Gaya hmun thianghlim, leh Biraja bial chu Odisha-a Jajpur district chhungah a awm tak tak tih a chiang hle a ni.

Lalpa Kalki pianna hmun chungchangah hian hriatfiahna tam zawk neih nan, Malika-a tarlan chang dangte kan la ang.

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chumbaka Malika’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଉଶ୍ୱାସୀ ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷkī abatāre Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśi bhaktaṅku uśwāsī dharmaku tumbhe pāḷiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kalki Avatar chu Odisha state-ah a piang ang. Mahaprabhu Kalki chuan mi sualte a tiboral ang a, Pathian mite a chhanhim ang a, khawvelah Dharma a dinthar leh ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan, Lalpa thu sawi tarlangin, a Malika lehkhabuah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଦେଖିବ ଜାଣ,
ଆମ୍ଭ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ ଯାଜପୁରେଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Indu pare bindu dekhiba jāṇa,
āmbha ābirbhāba Jājapureṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, vanah thla chungah dot emaw arsi emaw hmuh a nih hunah, Lalpa Chungnungber lo inlar chu Jajpur-ah a ni ang tih hria ang che.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan, a Malika lehkhabu ‘Jai Birachita Chautisha’-ah, Lalpa thu ngei chu chiah chiahin a sawi nawn a, heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେତେବେଳେ ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡିକରି ଯିବୁ,
ଯାଜପୁର ବିପ୍ର ଘରେ ଯାଇଁ ଜନମିବୁ।
ଜଗିଥିବୁ ହେ। ଯେ ଯେତେ କହିଲେ ନ ଶୁଣିବୁ ହେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Jetebeḷe Nīḷāchaḷa chhāḍikari jibu,
Jājapura bipra ghare jāiñ janamibu.
Jagithibu he. Je jete kahile na śuṇibu he.

Awmzia:
"Nilachala (Puri) i chhuahsan hunah, Jajpur-a Brahmin inah i piang ang. Fimkhur la; tu pawhin eng pawh sawi se, ngaithla suh ang che."

Malika lehkhabu ‘Tatwabodhini’-ah chuan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହେବେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଭାବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Jājanagre prabhu janama hebe,
janama hoibe brāhmaṇa bhābe.

Awmzia:
"Lalpa chu Jajanagar-ah a piang ang; Brahmin angin a piang ang."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Bigyanakalpa’ tihah chuan heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ମହାପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ,
ଗରୁଡକୁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନି ଲୀଳା ଯେ କରିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Jājanagre Mahāprabhu janama hoibe,
Garuḍaku saṅge gheni līḷā je karibe.

Awmzia:
"Mahaprabhu chu Jajanagar-ah a piang ang; Garuda hruaiin, a Pathian thiltih ropui tak a ti ang."

Chung chângte aṭang chuan, Mahaprabhu Kalki pianna tur chu Sambal Nagar, Nabhigaya hmun thianghlim, Biraja ram, Jajpur district, Odisha-ah a ni ngei dawn tih rinhlelhna a awm tawh lo.

Bhavishya Malika hmaa ziak Bhagavata Purana leh Mahabharata kan en chuan, chungte pawhin Lalpa Kalki pianna hmun tur chu Jajpur, Odisha a ni tih an kawh bawk.

Hmanlaiin, Maharishi Vedavyas-a chuan Shrimad Bhagavata Mahapurana-ah Lalpa Kalki pianna hmun chu 'Sambhal khua' a ni tih a sawi a. Heti hian a lo ziak a ni –

Sanskrit Sloka:

संभलग्राममुख्यस्य ब्राह्मणस्य महात्मनः ।
भवने विष्णुयशसः कल्किः प्रादुर्भविष्यति ।।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Śambhalagrāmamukhyasya brāhmaṇasya mahātmanaḥ |
bhavane viṣṇuyaśasaḥ kalkiḥ prādurbhaviṣyati ||

Awmzia:
"Sambhal khuaa hotu, Vishnuyasha hming pu, Brahmin mi ropui tak inah chuan, Lalpa Kalki chu a lo lang ang."

Hnuah, Mahabharata-a 'Vanaparva'-ah chuan, Maharishi Vyas-a ngei chuan hei hi tlem a siam danglam a, Lalpa Kalki pianna hmun chu 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama' tiin a vuah a. Heti hian a ziak a ni –

Sanskrit Sloka:

कल्कि विष्णुयशा नाम द्विजः कालप्रचोदितः ।
उत्पत्स्यते महावीर्यो महाबुद्धिपराक्रमः ।।
सम्भूतः सम्भलग्रामे ब्राह्मणावसथे शुभे ।
मनसा तस्य सर्वाणि वाहनान्यायुधानि च ।।
उपस्थास्यन्ति योधाश्च शस्त्राणि कवचानि च ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kalki Vishnuyashā nāma dwijaḥ kālaprachoditaḥ |
utpatsyate mahāvīryo mahābuddhiparākramaḥ ||
sambhūtaḥ sambhalagrāme brāhmaṇāvasathe shubhe |
manasā tasya sarvāṇi vāhanānyāyudhāni cha ||
upasthāsyanti yodhāshcha shastrāṇi kavachāni cha |

Awmzia:
Chung chângte hmang chuan, Maharishi Vyas-a chuan Lalpa Kalki chu 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama'-ah a piang ang tih a sawi a, chu chu 'din' emaw 'phun' emaw Sambhal khua tihna a ni a, Yajnas leh Pathian biakna atana Brahmins-ho awmna hmun atana din Sambhal khua a ni ang. He Sambhal khuaa cheng, Pathian Vishnu ropuina hlate sa ṭhin, Brahmin mi fel tak inah Lalpa Kalki chu a piang ang tih a ziak a ni.

Tunah chuan, chanchin hlui kan chhui a, kan en chuan, Yajna leh Pathian biakna atana din Brahmins-ho awmna hmun chu Odisha-a Jajpur district-ah a awm tih kan hmu ang.

A takah chuan, Jajpur hming hi kum zana (10th century) Somavamsi Lal Jajati (Yayati) hming chawia pek a ni. Lal Jajati hian he khawpui hi a din a, Abhinav Jajatinagar chu a khawpui berah a siam a. Hnuah, a hming thlakin, a hmasa berah Jajanagar, chumi hnuah Jajpur tih a ni. Lal Jajati Keshari chuan Uttar Pradesh-a Kannauj aṭangin Brahmin mi 10,000 vel a hruai a, Ashwamedha Yajnas (Dashashwamedha) sawm tih turin Jajpur, Odisha-ah a awmtir a. Tun thleng pawhin, Baitarani Lui kama Dashashwamedha Ghat chuan he thil thleng hi min hriattir reng a ni. Engpawhnise, Maharishi Vyas-a chuan Mahabharata-a 'Vanaparva'-ah chuan Jajpur-a Biraja temple chhak lama awm Brahmins-ho awmna hmun hi 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama' tiin a kawh a ni.

9 Khandagiri – Bhubaneswar-ah nge hmun dangah?

Bhavishya Malika-a ngaihvenna nei mi tupawhin Mahaprabhu Kalki pianna hmun tur chungchangah rinhlelhna an nei lo. Jajpur, Odisha-a awm Sambal khuaah Kalki avatar chu a piang ang tih an hre chiang hle.

Jajpur-a a pian hnuah, Lalpa chu Khandagiri hming pu hmunah a awm ang tih rinhlelhna an nei bawk lo.

Panchasakhas zinga mi pakhat, Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chumbaka Malika’ tihah chuan heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଳିଯୁଗେ କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ହରି,
ଶ୍ରୀ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ehi kaliyuge Kalki abatare Hari,
Shri khaṇḍagirire thibe prabhu bije kari.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kaliyuga-ah chuan, Lalpa Hari chu a Kalki pianziaah Khandagiri-ah a awm ang tihna a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’ tihah chuan heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୋପନେ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଯେ ଆସି,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ବସି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Gopane Mukunda je asi,
Khandagirire thibe basi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lalpa chu Khandagiri-ah rûk takin a cheng ang tihna a ni.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu pakhat, 'Patta Madana' tihah chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକତ ସୁମେଳ ହୋଇ ଏକ ଠୁଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଶେ ଯିବେ,
ଅନନ୍ତ ମୂରତି ଦର୍ଶନ ସାରିଣ ସର୍ବେ ସର୍ବଙ୍କୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhakata sumeḷa hoi eka ṭhuḷa Khaṇḍagiri pāshe jibe,
ananta mūrati darshana sāriṇa sarbe sarbaṅku chinhibe.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Khandagiri hming pu hmunah chuan, Pathian mi (devotees) an inkhawm ang. Chutah chuan, Pathian mite chuan Lalpa Kalki hmuh theihna (darshan) an nei ang a, an inhmelhriat ang.

He thleng hi chuan mi zawng zawng an inrem a, mahse he Khandagiri hi khawi hmunah nge a awm tihah miin rinhlelhna an nei a ni. Tunlai hian, video leh PDF ṭhenkhat hmangin, Khandagiri chungchangah thu hrang hrang sawi a ni. Mi ṭhenkhat sawi danin, Lalpa awmna tur Khandagiri hming pu hmun hi Jajpur, Odisha-ah a awm a, mi dang ṭhenkhat erawh chuan Khandagiri hi Dhenkanal district, Odisha-ah a awm an ti thung.

9.1 Khandagiri hi khawi hmunah nge a awm?

He thuziak hmang hian, Khandagiri (Jajpur, Dhenkanal) chungchanga hming tarlan zawng zawngte kha a dik lo ve ve a ni tih ka sawi duh a ni.

Khandagiri awmna hmun sawifiahin, Panchasakhas zinga mi pakhat, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Basanta Manjari’ tihah chuan heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମହିମା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଭକ୍ତ ତହିଁ ସେଠାରେ ଅଛଇ।
ଗୁପତ ଲୀଳା ହୋଇବ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ,
ତପସ୍ୟାରେ ରହିଥିବେ ଋଷିଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗରେ।
ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ସେକାଳରେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବ,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସେହି ଭସାଇଣ ଦେବ।
ତ୍ରିଭୁବନ ସ୍ୱର କଥା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ବାରଭୁଜା ଦୁର୍ଗାମାତା ଜଗି ରହିଛଇଁ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ବାବୁ ଶୁଣ ମନଦେଇ,
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଜଗିଅଛି ସେଠାବକୁ ତହିଁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Khaṇḍagiri mahimā kahita nuhañi,
lakṣha lakṣha bhakta tahin seṭhāre achhai.
Gupata līḷā hoiba Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre,
tapasyāre rahithibe ruṣhiṅka saṅgare.
Gupta Gaṅgā sekāḷare bāhara hoiba,
Bhubaneśwaraku sehi bhasāiṇa deba.
Tribhubana swara kathā kahita nuhañi,
bārabhuja Durgāmātā jagi rahichhaiṃ.
Āhuri kathāe bābu śuṇa manadei,
Bhūāsuṇī jagiachhi seṭhābaku tahiñ.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Khandagiri ropuina chu sawifiah theih loh a ni. Chutah chuan Pathian mi nuai tam tak an awm. Khandagiri-ah chuan Pathian thiltih rûk (leelas) a thleng ang. Rishi leh sâkhaw mi tam takin chutah chuan an inngaihtuahna an hmang a. Lui rûk (Gupta Ganga) pakhat a luang a, chu chuan Kalki-a tichhia Pathian chetzia hunah Bhubaneswar-ah chhiatna nasa tak a thlen ang. Bhubaneswar ropuina pawh chu nasa tak a ni. Chutah chuan Nu Barabhuji leh Nu Bhuasuni an awm a, anni chuan Kali Yuga tawpah chhiatna thlen hna an thawk ang.

Chung shloka-ah chuan, Mahapurush-a chuan Khandagiri chu Bhubaneswar-ah a awm tih a sawi chiang a ni.

Khandagiri awmna hmun sawifiahin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’ tihah chuan heti hian a ziak bawk a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ,
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପକୁ ଧ୍ୟାନ କରନ୍ତି।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହିଯାଏ ଗୁପ୍ତର,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ହୁଏ ସତ୍ୱର।
ଘଟଣା ସ୍ଥାନ ଯେ ଅଟଇ ସେହି,
କଳିର ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ସେଠାହିଁ।
ଉଦୟଗିରି ଯେ ଅପୋଡା ସ୍ଥାନ,
ସେଠାବରେ ବହୁ ସାଧୁ ଆଶ୍ରମ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhubaneśwarare lāgiba goḷa, ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sādhu achhanti, Kaḷkī rūpaku dhyāna karanti.
Kṣhīra dhāra bahijāe guptara, gumphāre bhajana hue satwara.
Ghaṭaṇā sthāna je aṭai sehi, Kaḷira bhārata yuddha seṭhāhiñ.
Udayagiri je apoḍā sthāna, seṭhābare bahu sādhu āśrama.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Bhubaneswar-ah indona a awm ang a, Ekamra ramngaw chu chutah a awm a ni. Chumi hnuah Mahapurush-a chuan Khandagiri pawh chutah a awm a, chutah chuan rishi leh sâkhaw mi famkim tam tak an cheng ang a, Kalki hming chu an lam reng ang a, a pianzia chu an ngaihtuah ang. Khandagiri pûkteah chuan, chhun leh zanin bhajans leh kirtans a thleng ang. Chutah chuan, lui rûk (Kshira Nadi) pakhat pawh a luang ang. Chu Khandagiri chu "Thil Thlenna Hmun" (Ghatana Sthana) a ni ang a, chutah chuan Mahabharata indona la bang chu a thleng ang. A bulah chuan Udayagiri a awm a, chutah chuan sâkhaw mi famkim tam takin an ashram an din a ni.

Chung shloka hmang chuan, Mahapurush-a chuan sâkhaw mi tam tak awmna tur, Kalki hming lam rûkna pûkte awmna tur, leh Mahabharata indona la bang thlenna tur Khandagiri chu Bhubaneswar-ah a awm a, chutah chuan Ekamra ramngaw pawh a awm tih a sawi chiang hle.

Hetah hian chhiartute ngaihvenna hmun pawimawh tak pakhat ka hrilh duh a ni. Malika lehkhabuteah chuan, Bhubaneswar hi Ekamra Kshetra, Ekamra Van, emaw Ekamra Kanan tiin an ko fo ṭhin. A takah chuan, kum zana pathum (13th-century) Ekamra Purana sawi danin, Pathian Lingaraj awmna hmun bulah chuan theihai kung pakhat chauh a awm ṭhin. Chuvangin, Lingaraj hmun chu Ekamra Kshetra (theihai kung pakhat awmna hmun) tiin an ko bawk.

Hei vang hian Mahapurush-hote chuan Bhubaneswar hi Lingaraj Kshetra (Lingaraj temple awmna hmun) emaw Ekamra Van Kshetra tiin vawi tam tak an ko ṭhin a ni.

Hei chauh hi ni lovin, Bhubaneswar-a Bindu Sarovar awmna avangin, Panchasakhas-hote chuan an Malika lehkhabuteah Bhubaneswar hi Bindu Sarovar ram tiin an ko bawk.

Mahapurush-a chuan 'Garuda Gita'-ah chuan sawifiahin heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ଏ ସବୁ ମୋ ଗୁପ୍ତ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଆଉ ପୃଥ୍ବୀରେ ଯେତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନ ପୁରାଣେ ଅଛି ବର୍ଣ୍ଣନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhubaneśwara je ekāmra kānana e sabu mo gupta sthāna,
āu pruthwīre jete siddha sthāna purāṇe achhi barṇana.

Awmzia:
Lalpa Krishna chuan a Pathian mi (devotee), Garuda hnenah chuan, Bhubaneswar-a awm Ekamra Kanan chu Siddha hmun (hmun famkim) a ni tih a hrilh a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Chhayalisha Patala' tihah chuan heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଏକାମ୍ରରେ,
ରହିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ତିନିଘରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhubaneśwara Ekāmrare, rahibe bhakta tini ghare.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lalpa Pathian mite chu Bhubaneswar Ekamra ramah an awm ang tihna a ni.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Chumbaka Malika' tihah chuan heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ସୀମା ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ,
ପଞ୍ଚବିଂଶ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବେ ତଟରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhubaneśwara sīmā Khaṇḍagirire, pañchabiṃśha bhakta thibe taṭare.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Khandagiri chu Bhubaneswar ramriah a awm a, chutah chuan Pathian mi 25-in Pathian thiltih ropuiah an tel ang.

Mahapurush-a chuan 'Chakada Madana' tihah chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଗୋଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାର,
ଭାବି କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ନିଜ ନାମ ସ୍ମର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhubaneśwarare goḷa Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāra,
bhābi kahai Achyutī nija nāma smara.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Bhubaneswar-a Khandagiri hmunah chuan indona a awm ang tihna a ni.

Panchasakhas zinga pakhat, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das chuan a Malika lehkhabuah heti hian a ziak –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଠାରେ ଜାଣ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସେ ସରୋବର ପୁଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhubaneśwara ṭhāre jāṇa,
Bindu se sarobara puṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Bindu Sarovar chu Bhubaneswar-ah a awm tih hria ang che.

Mahapurush Yasovanta Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’-ah heti hian a ziak –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜ ସ୍ଥଳୀ,
ଦୁଇ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକଜଣ ସ୍ତିରୀ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhubaneśwara Liṅgarāja sthaḷī,
dui puruṣha ekajaṇa stirī.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Bhubaneswar chu Lingaraj awmna hmun a ni a, Lingaraj temple chu Bhubaneswar-ah a awm tihna a ni.

Google kan hman pawhin, Khandagiri emaw, Ekamra Kanan emaw, Bindu Sarovar emaw, Lingaraj temple emaw pawh ni se, an vaiin Bhubaneswar-ah vek an awm tih kan hre thei a ni.

Khandagiri, Lalpa chenna hmun tur, hmun tam takah Kalki hming lamna hmun tur chu—he Khandagiri hi Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Kshetra)-ah a awm tih hrilhfiah nan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan ‘Chakada Madana’-ah heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଦଦେଶେ ଭରତ ପାଦୁକା,
ଛଦ୍ମବେଶୀ ସାଧୁ ପୂଜା କରୁଅଛି ଏକା।
ଗୁପ୍ତଗଙ୍ଗା ବହିଅଛି ଗୁପ୍ତ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଗୁପ୍ତ ବେଶେ ରହିଛନ୍ତି ପୁରି।
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଅଟଇ ଶିବ ଯୋଗ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶୁକପକ୍ଷୀ ପାଇଲା ଯେଉଁଠି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଜ୍ଞାନ।
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଦେବୀ ଅଛି ଜାଣିଥା ସତ୍ୱର,
ସେ ଦେବୀ ଘାତରେ ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ କମ୍ପମାନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Khaṇḍagiri pādadeśe Bharata pādukā, chhadmabeśī sādhu pūjā karuachhi ekā.
Guptagaṅgā bahiachhi gupta Khaṇḍagiri, siddha sādhu gupta beśe rahichhanti puri.
Ekāmra bana aṭai Śiba yoga sthāna, śukapakṣhī pāilā jeuṇṭhi brahmajñāna.
Bhūāsuṇୀ debī achhi jāṇithā satwara, se debī ghātare pruthibī heba kampamāna.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Khandagiri tlangpui hnuai lamah Bharata Paduka a awm a, chu chu hriat lohva awm sâkhaw mi pakhatin a be thin. Chu Khandagiri-ah chuan Gupta Ganga lui chu rûk takin a luang thin. Chutah (Khandagiri-ah) chuan sâkhaw mi thianghlim tam tak an rûk takin an cheng thin. Ekamra ramngaw chu chutah a awm a, chu chu Lalpa Shiva Yoga hmun a ni. Chutah chuan Shuka sava chuan Brahma-knowledge a lo hmu tawh thin. A hnaihah Goddess Bhuasuni a awm a, ani chuan Kali Yuga tawp lamah lei chunga tihboral hna a thawk ang.

Chhiartute hnenah heti hian ka hrilh duh a ni, chunga châng pakhat zinga pakhatah khan, Mahapurush chuan Khandagiri chu Ekamra ramngaw huamchhungah a awm a, sâkhaw mi tam takin Khandagiri-ah chuan an inkhawngaihna an ti thin a ti. Lui pakhat pawh rûk takin a luang thin. Tun chângah hian, Mahapurush chuan thil thuhmun bawk a sawi nawn leh a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ‘Tera Janma Sharana’-ah heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ଲୀଳାମୟଙ୍କର ସତ୍ୟ ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ,
ଲୀଳା କରୁଥିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମାଧବ ସର୍ବେ ଆନନ୍ଦ ହୋଇଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Līḷā prakāśiba līḷāmayankara satya je Ekāmra bana,
līḷā karuthibe Ananta Mādhaba sarbe ānanda hoiṇa.

Awmzia:
Hetah hian Mahapurush chuan Lalpa chenna hmun chu Bhubaneswar a ni leh tih a sawi. Mahse, Bhubaneswar hming emaw, Khandagiri hming emaw a lam lo. Mahapurush chuan hmun thuhmun chu ‘Ekamra Van’ tiin a ko. Mahapurush chuan Lalpa thil tih ropui tak tak (Leelamaya) chu Ekamra Van hmun atangin a lo lang ang a ti. Chu Ekamra Van hmunah chuan, Lalpa Ananta Madhava, chu chu Kalki, chuan thil tih ropui tak tak a ti ang a, chu chu hmuin leh hriain ringtute hlimna chu a tawp lovang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ‘Aagata Bhavishyata Malika’-ah heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

କଳା ଧଳା ଅଶ୍ୱ ଉପରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ରହିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନରେ ଯେତେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ଋଷି,
ସକଳେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ନାମକୁ ଘୋଷି।
ସକଳେ ସେବେଳେ ଠୁଳ ଯେ ହେବେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗର,
କପିଳେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଭେଟିଣ ହୋଇବେ ସୂପକାର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷā dhaḷā ashwa upare prabhu hebe bāhāra,
Bhubaneshwarare rahibe prabhu raṅgā adhara.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sthānare jete achhanti ruṣi,
sakaḷe bāhāra hoibe prabhu nāmaku ghoṣi.
Sakaḷe sebeḷe ṭhuḷa je hebe Bindu Sāgara,
Kapileshwarare bheṭiṇa hoibe sūpakāra.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lalpa chuan “Kala-Dhala” emaw, sakawr dum leh var emaw chungah tihboral turin a chhuak ang. Chumi hmain, Lalpa chuan Bhubaneswar-ah a awm ang. Khandagiri hmun thianghlimah inkhawngaihna ti sâkhaw mi leh puithiam zawng zawngte chu chumi hunah chuan puk atangin an lo chhuak ang a, Lalpa hming an puang ang a, Bhubaneswar-a awm Bindu Sarovar-ah an inkhawm ang. Chumi hnuah, chaw siamtute chuan an vaiin a hnaih Kapileshwar temple-ah an hmuak ang.

Chunga chângah pawh khan, Khandagiri chu hmun thianghlim, sâkhaw mi leh puithiam tam tak chenna hmun tur a ni tih a sawi. Tin, Khandagiri emaw, Bindu Sarovar emaw, Kapileshwar Temple emaw pawh ni se, an vaiin Bhubaneswar-ah an awm a, an inhnaih hlawm a ni tih pawh a sawi bawk.

Panchasakhas zinga pakhat, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das chuan, a zirtir Baranga Das-a'n Lalpa Kalki chu khawiah nge a lo piang ang a, khawiah nge a chen ang tia a zawh chuan, a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chumbaka Malika’-ah heti hian a hrilhfiah a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ହେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ କହିବା ସେ ରଙ୍ଗ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଅବତାର ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିରଜା କ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ଜନମ ଲଭିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମିଶ୍ର ଗୃହେଣ।
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ଗୃହକୁ ଛାଡିବେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଯାଇଁ,
ସିଦ୍ଧଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ସିଦ୍ଧଙ୍କ ସଦନ ରହିବେ ସେ ଭାବଗ୍ରାହୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ସୁବର୍ଣ୍ଣଚ୍ୟୁତ ଗିରିରେ,
ସେହି ସ୍ଥାନେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରିଥିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ଜନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ଅନନ୍ତ ବାସୁଦେବ ବିପ୍ରଲିଙ୍ଗ ଭେଟ ସେ ଯହିଁ ଆଗରେ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ବିଧି ସାରିଣ ସିଦ୍ଧ କେଦାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ସାରିବେ ସମସ୍ତ ଭକତ ମିଳି,
ନିଜ କଳା ଭକ୍ତି କଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବେ ଛାଡିବେ କଳିର ମଳି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Shuṇa he bāraṅga kahibā se raṅga prabhu abatāra sthāna,
Shrī Birajā kṣetre janama labhibe Ananta Mishra gruheṇa.
Janama hoibe gruhaku chhāḍibe tapasyā karibe jāiñ,
Siddhagiri sthāna siddhaṅka sadana rahibe se bhābagrāhī.
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna Ekāmra kānana Subarṇachyuta girire,
sehi sthāne prabhu bije karithibe bhakta jana saṅgatare.
Shrī Ananta Bāsudeba bipraliṅga bheṭa se jahiñ āgare,
Bindusāgare snāna bidhi sāriṇa siddha Kedāra madhyare.
Shrī Bindu Sāgare snāna sāribe samasta bhakata miḷi,
nija kaḷā bhakti kaḷā prakāshibe chhāḍibe kaḷira maḷi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lalpa chu Shri Viraja Kshetra (Jajpur)-a Ananta Mishra hming pu ringtu inah a lo piang ang. A pian hnuah, Lalpa chuan a in a chhuahsan ang a, sâkhaw mi thianghlimte awmna hmunah inkhawngaihna ti turin a kal ang. Mahapurush chuan "hmun thianghlim" tih ringawt a sawi tawp lo, chu hmun hming chu Khandagiri a ni ang a, chu chu Ekamra Kanan-ah a awm ang a ti bawk. Chu Ekamra Kanan-a Khandagiri hmunah chuan, Lalpa chu a ringtute nen an cheng ang. Mahapurush chuan Shri Ananta Vasudev temple chu chutah a awm ang a, Linga, chu chu Lingaraj temple pawh chutah a awm ang a ti. Lalpa chuan Bindu Sagar-ah a inbual ang a, Siddha Kedara (Kedareshwar) temple a tlawh ang.

Mahapurush chuan ringtu zawng zawngte chu chu Bindu Sarovar-ah chuan an inbual ang a, chumi hnuah chuan ringtute chungah Pathian eng a lo lang ang a, Kali Yuga bawlhhlawh lak atangin an fihlim vek ang a ti bawk.

Chunga châng hmang chuan, Sishu Ananta Dasji chuan Shri Ananta Vasudev temple awmna hmun, Kedareshwar temple awmna hmun, Lingaraj awmna hmun, chu Bhubaneswar (Ekamra Van hmun) thuhmunah chuan, Khandagiri a awm tih a hrilhfiah a ni. He Khandagiri hi sâkhaw mi thianghlimte hmun a ni a, Lalpa chu hetah hian a cheng ang.

Achyutananda Dasji pianthar, Saint Hadi Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kali Chautisha’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦେଇ ଶୁଣ ସୁଜନେ କଳିଯୁଗ ବିଚାର,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ଜନମ ହେବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ଚକ୍ରଧର।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହି ଯାଉଛି ସ୍ଥାନ ଅମରାବତୀ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଗୁପତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି।
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ଲାଗିଛି ରାତ୍ର ଦିନ ମଧ୍ୟରେ,
ଗୁପତେ ଖେଳ ସେ ଖେଳିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେହି ଠାବରେ।
ଝାଡଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ସେ ଠାବ,
ଝଲମଲ ଜ୍ୟୋତି ବାହାରେ ଲୀଳା ଚହଟ ହେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Karṇa dei shuṇa sujane kaḷiyuga bichāra,
Kaḷkī rūpare janama hebe Shrī Chakradhara.
Kṣīra dhāra bahi jāuchhi sthāna Amarābatī,
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna gupate siddha sādhu achhanti.
Gumphāre bhajana lāgichhi rātra dina madhyare,
gupate kheḷa se kheḷibe prabhu sehi ṭhābare.
Jhāḍakhaṇḍagiri gumphā madhyare prabhuṅkara se ṭhāba,
jhalamala jyoti bāhāre līḷā chahaṭa heba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kali Yuga tawp lamah, Lalpa Maha Vishnu chuan Kalki piantharah lei chungah a lo piang ang. A thil tih ropui tak takte chu a hmasa berin Kshira lui (Vaitarani) hmun, Amaravati, chu chu Jajpur-ah a thleng ang. Chumi hnuah a thil tih ropui tak takte chu Khandagiri-ah a thleng ang a, chutah chuan sâkhaw mi thianghlimte chu pukah rûk takin chhun leh zanin bhajan-kirtan-ah an pil ang. Mahapurush chuan Lalpa chu chu Khandagiri emaw, Jhadakhandagiri hmunah chuan a cheng ang a, a thil tih ropui tak takte a ti ang a ti.

Saint Hadi Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kali Chautisha’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଘଟଣା ପ୍ରତିମା ପୁରୁଷ ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜଙ୍କ ପୁର,
ଘୋର କଳି ମହାଭାରତ ହେବ ସେହି ଠାବର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ghaṭaṇā pratimā puruṣha Liṅgarājaṅka pura,
ghora kaḷi mahābhārata heba sehi ṭhābara.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan "Thil Thlenna Hmun" chu Lingaraj hmun emaw, Bhubaneswar emaw a ni ang a, Mahabharata indona la bang chu chutah chuan a thleng ang a ti. Kan hriat angin, hma deuh khan châng pakhat kan hmu tawh a, chutah chuan Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan – “Ghatana sthana je atai sehi, kalira bharata yuddha sethahin” tiin a ziak. Achyutananda Dasji chuan he châng hi Khandagiri chungchangah a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah a ziak a, Khandagiri chu “Ghatana Sthana” a ni a, chutah chuan Mahabharata indona la bang chu a thleng ang a ti.

Hnuai lamah, Hadi Das chuan – Bhubaneswar emaw, Lingaraj hmun emaw chu “Ghatana Sthana” a ni a, Kali Mahabharata indona chu chutah chuan a thleng ang a ti.

Hei hian Achyutananda Das-a'n ‘Chakada Madana’-a Khandagiri chungchang a sawi chu, a pianthar, Hadi Das-a'n ‘Kali Chautisha’-a Lingaraj hmun (Bhubaneswar) chungchang a sawi nen a inang chiah tih a kawk. Hei hian hmun pahnih, chu chu Khandagiri leh Bhubaneswar, chu hmun thuhmun an kawk tih a finfiah. Tawi fel takin, Khandagiri chu Bhubaneswar-ah a awm a ni.

Hriattirna – Chunga châng tam takah khan, Bhubaneswar-a Khandagiri chu sâkhaw mi thianghlim leh Pathian mi thianghlimte hmun a ni tih kan hria. Chung sâkhaw mite chu Khandagiri pukah chhun leh zanin Lalpa Kalki bhajan-kirtan-ah an pil thin. Tunah chuan Khandagiri-a Lalpa Kalki tan inkhawngaihna ti tur sâkhaw mi leh puithiam pawimawh berte chu tute nge an nih ang a, pukah chuan sâkhaw mi eng zat nge bhajan-kirtan ti ang tih kan hre ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ‘Aagata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିକି ଯେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର କେତେକ ଦୂର,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ସାଧୁସନ୍ଥ ନିକର।
ନାରଦ ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଶମ୍ଭୁ ଯେ ଅଗସ୍ତି ଉପଲସ୍ତି,
ଦୁର୍ବାସା କଶ୍ୟପ ଚ୍ୟବନ କୁମ୍ଭଋଷି ସନ୍ତତି।
ପରାଶର ବଶିଷ୍ଠାଦି ହୋ ବିଶ୍ୱାମିତ୍ରଙ୍କୁ ଘେନି,
ଷାଠିଏ ସହସ୍ର ଋଷି ହୋ ଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପୁଣି।
ଦେବଋଷି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଋଷି ଯେ ସର୍ବେ ମେଳି ହୋଇଣ,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ କରନ୍ତି କଳ୍କୀ ନାମ ପୁଣ।
ଦେବତାମାନେ ଯେ ସେଠାରେ ରାତ୍ରେ ଗୋଷ୍ଠୀ କରନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀର ଆଦ୍ୟ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ନିତ୍ୟେ ଜପୁଛନ୍ତି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Khaṇḍagiriki je Bhubaneshwara keteka dūra,
Khaṇḍagiri madhye achhanti sādhusantha nikara.
Nārada Mārkaṇḍa Shambhu je Agasti Upalasti,
Durbāsā Kashyapa Chyabana Kumbharuṣi santati.
Parāshara Bashiṣṭhādi ho Bishwāmitraṅku gheni,
ṣāṭhie sahasra ruṣi ho giri madhyare puṇi.
Debaruṣi Brahmaruṣi je sarbe meḷi hoiṇa,
gumphāre bhajana karanti Kaḷkī nāma puṇa.
Debatāmāne je seṭhāre rātre goṣṭhī karanti,
Kaḷkīra ādya mantraku nitye japuchhanti.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabuah Krishna-Garuda inbiakna chu a tarlang. Garuda chuan Khandagiri hmun chu Bhubaneswar atanga hla lo (a hnai hle) a ni a ti. Khandagiri-ah chuan sâkhaw mi tam tak an awm. Chutah chuan Narada, Markandeya, Shambhu, Agastya, Upalasti, Durvasa, Kashyapa, Chyavana, “Kumbharushi Santati” chu Dridhasyu, Parashara, Va-shish-tha, Vishwamitra, etc., an ni a, an vaiin sâkhaw mi sawm leh pahnih an ni a, an telin sâkhaw mi 60,000, Devarishis, leh Brahmarishis an ni a, pukah chuan Lalpa Kalki hming-bhajan-ah an pil thin. Pathian te chuan chumi pukah chuan zanah inkhawm an nei thin a, Lalpa Kalki mantra chu ni tin an lam thin.

He chângah hian, Garudaji chuan Khandagiri pukah inkhawngaihna ti sâkhaw mite chanchin a sawi mai bakah, Khandagiri puk chu Bhubaneswar atanga hla lo, a nih loh pawhin, a hnai hle ang a ti bawk.

Chunga châng 15 atang khan, chhiartute tan a chiang tawh ang, Bhavishya Malika-in a sawi danin, Khandagiri—Kalki chenna hmun tur, sâkhaw mi thianghlim tam tak chenna hmun tur, Kalki hming bhajan-kirtan chu pukah chhunzawm zelin a awmna tur, lui pakhat rûk takin a luanna tur, Mahabharata indona la bang a thlenna tur—chu Bhubaneswar emaw, Lingaraj hmun emaw, Ekamra Van hmunah chauh a awm a, khawiah mah dangah a awm lo.

10 Kalki Incarnation thiltum

Kan hriat theuh angin, Lalpa chuan felna (Dharma) din nghet turin he lei chungah pianthar sawm a nei thin. Satya Yuga emaw, Treta Yuga emaw, Dvapara Yuga emaw pawh ni se, Lalpa pianthar chhan chu a thuhmun reng a ni—Dharma din nghet.

Shrimad Bhagavad Gita-ah chuan, Lalpa Krishna chuan Arjuna hnenah heti hian a ti –

Sanskrit Sloka:

परित्राणाय साधूनां विनाशाय च दुष्कृताम् ।
धर्मसंस्थापनार्थाय संभवामि युगे युगे ।।

Awmzia:
Mi fel (ringtute) venhim nan, mi sualte tihboral nan, leh felna din nghet nan, kum zawnin ka inpuang thin.

Bhavishya Malika lehkhabuahte pawh, mi sualte tihboral nan, ringtute leh sâkhaw mite venhim nan, leh Dharma din nghet nawn leh nan, Lalpa Maha Vishnu chuan a pianthar sawmna leh a tawp ber, Kalki piantharah lei chungah a lo chhuk ang tih a chuang a ni.

Dvapara Yuga-a Lalpa Krishna leh a thian Arjuna inbiakna chu, kum 600 vel liamta Kali Yuga-ah Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Chausathi Patala'-ah a tarlang. Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak –

Odia Sloka:

ପାପ ଭାରା ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ସହି ନପାରି,
ମୋ ଆଗରେ କଲା ବେଗେ ଗୁହାରି।
କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ମୁହିଁ ହୋଇବି,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ବିନାଶିବି।
ବସି ନରପତି ହୃଦ ଦେଶରେ
ପ୍ରଜା ପାଳିବି ଉତ୍ତମ ଭାବରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Pāpa bhārā pṛthwī sahi napāri,
mo āgare kalā bege guhāri.
Kaḷkī abatāra muhiñ hoibi,
duṣṭa prāṇīmānaṅku bināshibi.
Basi narapati hṛda deshare,
prajā pāḷibi uttama bhābare.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, lei hian Kali Yuga tawp lamah sual rit a phur zo tawh lovang a, ka hmaah a dil ang. Chumi hnuah Kalki pianthar ka nei ang a, mi sualte ka tiboral ang a, ringtute ka chhanhim ang. India-ah awmin, khawvel pumpui rorelna ka enkawl ang a, mi zawng zawngte chu fel tak leh dik takin ka rorel sak ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan Malika lehkhabu dangah heti hian a ziak –

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭୟଙ୍କର ଉତ୍ପାତ ହେବ ଧରିତ୍ରୀ ବକ୍ଷରେ ଜାଣ,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାରି ଭାରା ଉଶ୍ୱାସିବେ ପୁଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mahābhayaṅkara utpāta heba dharitrī bakṣhare jāṇa,
Kaḷkī rūpare mlechha saṃhāri bhārā uśwāsibe puṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, lei chungah buaina nasa tak a thleng ang. Kalki ngei chuan mi sualte a tiboral ang a, lei chunga sual rit chu a chhawk ang.

Krishna-Arjuna inbiakna tarlangin, Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabuah heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳେ ମୁହିଁ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହେବି,
ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିଣ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନମାନଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷi śeṣha beḷe muñhi Kaḷkī rūpa hebi,
santhaṅku rakhiṇa duṣhṭajanamānanku nāśhibi.

Awmzia:
Shri Krishna chuan Kali Yuga tawp lamah, Kalki pianthar ka nei ang a ti. Sâkhaw mite, mi thianghlimte, leh thinlung thianghlim mi zawng zawngte venhimin, mi sual leh thil tisual zawng zawngte ka tiboral ang.

Lalpa thu sawi chu tarlang nawn lehin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Chausathi Patala'-ah heti hian a ziak –

Odia Sloka:

ବେଶୀ ପାପ ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପେ ମୁଁ ଉଦୟ ହେବି,
ଓଡିଶା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ମନ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଯାଇ କରିବି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Beśī pāpa jetebeḷe heba Kaḷkī rūpe mu udaya hebi,
Oḍiśā madhyare sthāne sthāne mana parīkṣhā jāi karibi.

Awmzia:
Lalpan a sawi a ni, Kali Yuga-ah, sualna a sang ber hunah, Kei, Kalki anga ka lo chhuah hunah, Odisha hmun hrang hrangah Pathian betute ka fiah ang a, dik leh dik lo ka thliar ang a, chumi ang zelin ka che ang.

Lalpa thu sawi chu chhawkin, Panchasakha zinga mi pakhat, Mahapurush Balaram Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Kali Aagata Bhavishyanta' ah a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଯେ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ମାତିବେ,
ଖପର କରେ ଘେନିଥିବେ।
ରୋଧିର ବୃଷ୍ଟି ଯେ ହୋଇବ,
କେ ଅବା କାହିଁ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ଯାହାକୁ ରଖିବିଟି ଟିକିଏ ମୁହିଁ,
ସେ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନେ ଥିବେ ରହି।
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଲୋକ ଯେ ନାଶ ହେବେ,
ସନ୍ଥ ଯେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ ରହିବେ।
ଅନେକ ଉତ୍ପାତ ଲାଗିବ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଚହଳ ପଡିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chaṇḍī je Chāmuṇḍā mātibe,
khapara kare ghenithibe.
Rodhira bṛṣṭi je hoiba,
ke abā kāhiñ nāsha jiba.
Jāhāku rakhibiṭi ṭikie muhiñ,
se prāṇīmāne thibe rahi.
Duṣṭa loka je nāsha hebe,
santha je abashya rahibe.
Aneka utpāta lāgiba,
shūnyare chahaḷa paḍiba.

Awmzia:
Lalpan a sawi a ni, Kali Yuga tawp lama Dharma dinthar lehna hunah, Nu Ashta Chandis leh Yogini dangte chuan tihchhiatna nasa tak an thlen ang. Vana tangin thisen ruah a sur ang. Buaina tam tak a awm ang a, chhiatna chu boruak ruak, a nih loh leh van boruak atangin a lo thleng ang. Keima venghimte chauh an him ang. Mi sualte chu tihboral an ni ang a, mi thianghlimte leh Pathian mi te chu chhanhim an ni ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Kali Kalpa Gita' ah a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣhatrīmānanku nikṣhatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Kali Yuga tawp lamah, Lalpa chu a Kalki hmelpuin a lo lang ang a, khawvel pum a fang chhuak ang a, mi sualte tihboralin Nu Lei hi a chhanhim ang.

'Kali Kalpa Gita' ah ngei pawh, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ziak nawn leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନିଧନ ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ସଂହାରଣ,
କରିବେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଦେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ମହୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
କଳିକଳ୍ପ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mlechha nidhana duṣhṭa saṃharaṇa,
karibe niśhche deba Kaḷkī Rāma.
Emanta mahī je heba uśwāsa,
Kaḷikalpa kahe Achyuta dāsa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Lal Kalki chuan Lei hi a chhanhim ngei ang, Mlechha (ramhuai/mi sual) te tihboralin leh mi sualte tibo vekin.

10.1 Lal Kalki Lo Kalna

Panchasakha zinga mi pakhat, Thlarau Ropui Sishu Ananta Das chuan he thil chungchang hi a Malika lehkhabu 'Chumbaka Malika' ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଆଶ୍ୱାସି ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷkī abatāra Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśhi bhaktaṅku āśwāsi dharmaku tumbhe pāliba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Lal Kalki Ropui chuan Odisha-ah a piang ang. Chumi hnuah, Lalpa chuan mi sualte a tibo ang a, Dharma dinthar leh nan Pathian betute a venghim ang.

11 Tihchhiatna thiltihte: Bul leh tawp chiang tak a awm em?

Bhabishya Malika atanga kan hriat angin, Kali Yuga leh Satya Yuga inkar hun thlak danglamna hunah hian, Lal Kalki hruainain khawvel pumah tihchhiatna thil thleng ngai lo a lo thleng ang a, chumi hmangin Mleccha (ramhuai), mi sual, mi tha lo, leh fel lote chu tihboral vek an ni ang. Bhabishya Malika chuan Pathian betute zawng zawng chu hetiang thil thlengah hian an him ang a, an hlauh a ngai lo ang tih thlamuanna a pe bawk.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan, a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chhayalisha Patala’ ah, Lalpa Ropui thu sawi chu a chhawm a, chumi ah chuan Lalpa chuan lo thleng tur tihchhiatna tlem (Khanda Pralaya) chungchang a hrilhfiah a ni.

Mahapurush chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

କାଳଭାବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟରେ,
ସୃଷ୍ଟି ନାଶ କରି ଲୀଳା କରେ ପାପ ଭାରେ।
ମାତ୍ର ମହାପ୍ରଳୟକୁ ରହିଥିବୁ ଟାକି,
ସେ କାଳରେ ଚରାଚର ନ ରହିବେ ବାକି।
ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ସହିତ ସଂହାରିଣୀ ଶକ୍ତି,
ଖଣ୍ଡା ଖପର ହାତରେ ବୁଲି ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି।
ରୋଗ ଶୋକ ଦୁଃଖ ଜାଲ ସଂସାରେ ପାତିବେ,
ପର ଆପଣାର ଭେଦ କିଛି ନ ରଖିବେ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ପବନ ଯେ ରୁଦ୍ର ମୂର୍ତ୍ତି ଧରି,
ଉପାଡ଼ିବ ସୁମେରୁ ସହିତ ଯେତେ ଗିରି।
ଚାରିମେଘ ଚାରିଦିଗେ ଆଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ କରିବେ,
ମହାଘୋର ବିପତ୍ତିକୁ ଆଣି ଘଟାଇବେ।
ଅନ୍ଧାର ହୋଇବ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ବଢ଼ିବ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ନକ୍ଷତ୍ର ଉଲକା ଛିଣ୍ଡିବ ନାହିଁ ଆଦି ଅନ୍ତ।
ଭୈରବୀ ରଡ଼ିବେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ କାନ୍ଦିବ ସକଳ,
ପାପମାତ୍ରା ପ୍ରବଳରେ ହେବ ଏହି ଫଳ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kālabhābe mahāshakti khaṇḍa praḷayare,
Sṛṣṭi nāsha kari līḷā kare pāpa bhāre.
Mātra mahāpraḷayaku rahithibu ṭāki,
Se kāḷare charāchara na rahibe bāki.
Chaṇḍī Chāmuṇḍā sahita saṃhāriṇī shakti,
Khaṇḍā khapara hātare buli dibārātri.
Roga shoka duḥkha jāla saṃsāre pātibe,
Para āpaṇāra bheda kichhi na rakhibe.
Aṇachāsha pabana je rudra mūrtti dhari,
Upāḍiba Sumeru sahita jete giri.
Chārimegha chāridige āchhanna karibe,
Mahāghora bipattiku āṇi ghaṭāibe.
Andhāra hoiba pṛthwī baḍhiba utpāta,
Nakṣatra ulakā chhiṇḍiba nāhiñ ādi anta.
Bhairabī raḍibe pṛthwī kāndiba sakaḷa,
Pāpamātrā prabaḷare heba ehi phaḷa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Hun (Kala) anga Thiltihtheihna Chungnung ber chuan tihchhiatna tlem a thlen ang a, khawvel hi a tibo ang. Tihchhiatna hunah chuan, thil nung zawng zawng an tuar ang. Chandi-Chamunda leh tihchhiatna thiltihtheihnate chuan ni leh zan khandaih leh lu ruh (Khapar) keng chungin an leng ang. Natna, hrehawmna, hri, leh lungngaihna chuan khawvel pum a tuam ang. 'Mahni ta' leh 'mi dang' inthliarna zawng zawng a bo ang. Thli sawmhnih pakua (49) chuan, Rudra hmel rapthlak tak puin, Sumeru ropui tak nen lam tlangdung zawng zawng a phawi chhuak ang. Chhum pali, Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, leh Drona te chuan, kawlkil li an hual ang a, chhiatna rapthlak tak an thlen ang. Thim chhah takin Lei hi a tuam ang; ni leh thla a chhuak tawh lo ang. Chumi hunah chuan, van atangin arsi tla a tla ang a, tihchhiatna thlentu an ni ang. Chumi thimah chuan, Nu Bhairavi chuan ring takin a au ang a, mi sual tam tak a tibo ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan, Lalpa thu sawi chu chhawkin, a Malika lehkhabu ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’ ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

କାହିଁ ଜଳ ରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ଜଗତ ନାଶିବି,
କାହିଁ ପବନ ରୂପରେ ସକଳ ଗ୍ରାସିବି।
କାହିଁ ଯୋଗମାୟା ନେଇ ସଂହାର କରିବି,
କାହିଁ ନିଜେ ବିଶ୍ଵରୂପ ଧାରଣ କରିବି।
କାହିଁ ମଉନ ବ୍ରତ ମୁଁ ଧାରଣ କରିବି,
କାହିଁ କାଳରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ପାପୀଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kāhiñ jala rūpe muhiñ jagata nāshibi,
kāhiñ pabana rūpare sakaḷa grāsibi.
kāhiñ yogamāyā nei saṃhāra karibi,
kāhiñ nije bishwarūpa dhāraṇa karibi.
kāhiñ mauna brata muñ dhāraṇa karibi,
kāhiñ kāḷarūpe muhiñ pāpīṅku nāshibi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Lal Kalki chuan a hmel hrang hrangin he khawvelah hian tihchhiatna nasa tak a thlen ang. Hmun thenkhatah chuan, tui angin a lo lang ang a, engkim a chimpil ang; hmun dangah chuan, thli-tihchhiatna hmangin engkim a chhem bo ang. Hmun thenkhatah chuan, ngawi renga awmin tihchhiatna a thlen ang a, hmun dangah chuan, Hun anga lo langin mi sualte a that ang. Hmun thenkhatah chuan, a Khawvel Pum Hmel a pu ang a, hmun dangah chuan, a Yogamaya thiltihtheihna hmangin tihchhiatna a thlen ang.

Bhabishya Malika sawi danin, Lal Kalki chuan he tihchhiatna hi tui lian rapthlak tak tak, lirnghing nasa tak tak, thlipui chhiatna thlentu, mei lian, van boruak chhiatna, hri leng hlauhawm, leh nuclear khawvel indona hmangin a thlen ang.

11.1 Lirnghingte

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ହିମାଳୟ ଏଭରେଷ୍ଟ ଟଳି ଯେ ପଡିବ,
ଯେବେ ଏହି ଧରା ପରେ ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Himālaya Everest ṭaḷi je paḍiba,
Jebe ehi dharā pare bhūmikampa heba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Kali Yuga tawp lama Kalki tihchhiatna chetna hunah, lirnghing nasa tak takin Lei hi a nuai ang a, chutiang khawpin 'Himalayas' leh a chhip, 'Everest' angte pawh a kehchhe vek ang. Pathian betute chu an him ang a, mi sualte erawh chu tihboral an ni ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ ah a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପେ ପ୍ରକମ୍ପିତ ହୋଇବ ଧରଣୀ,
ମାଟି ତଳେ ଲୁଚିଯିବ କୋଠାବାଡି ପୁଣି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhūmikampe prakampita hoiba dharaṇī,
māṭi taḷe luchijiba koṭhābāḍi puṇi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Lei pum pui hi lirnghingin a nghing ang a, chumi lirnghing avang chuan, in lian tak takte chu lei hnuaiah an pil ang.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu dangah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ପାତାଳେ ବାସୁକୀ ଟେକିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ,
ତିନିଥର ଯେ କମ୍ପିବ ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Pātāḷe Bāsukī ṭekiba muṇḍa,
tini thara je kampiba brahmāṇḍa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Kali Yuga tawp lama sualna rit lutuk avangin, Vasuki rulpui, Lei hi a lu chunga kengtu chuan a lu a thing nawn sek ang. Chumi avang chuan, he Lei ah hian lirnghing nasa tak tak pathum a lo thleng ang.

Kum zabi 20-naa Odisha-a piang mi thianghlim ropui tak, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Baishnaba Gita’ ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ ଅପାର,
ନାଶ ହୋଇବେ ନାରୀ ନର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhūmikampa heba apāra,
nāśha hoibe nārī nara.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Kali Yuga tawp hunah, lirnghing tam tak a awm ang a, chumi ah chuan mipa leh hmeichhia tam tak an boral ang.

11.2 Tui Lian (Jala Pralaya)

Tui lian rapthlak tak chungchang sawiin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତକୁ ଛାଡି ଦେଲେ ସବୁ ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଯିବ, ମହାସାଗର ଜଳରେ ଡୁବି ମରୁଥିବ।
ଧରଣୀ ଦେହରେ ଜଳ ମାରିବ ଲହରୀ, ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇଯିବ ଚଉଦିଗ ଭାରି।
ଅଛଇ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ଉତ୍ତର ମେରୁରେ, ତରଳିବ ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କ ରଶ୍ମିରେ।
ରୌଦ୍ରତାପେ ଯେତେବେଳେ ମେଦିନୀ କମ୍ପିବ, ସେତିକିବେଳେ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ତରଳିବ।
ବହିବ ପାଣିର ସୁଅ ସମୁଦ୍ରକୁ ପୁଣ, ଆଟଲାଣ୍ଟିକ ବଢ଼ିବ କାହିଁ କେତେ ଜାଣ।
ସମୁଦ୍ରର ପାଣି ଧୀରେ ବଢ଼ି ବଢ଼ି ଯିବ, ସାଗର ମାତିବ ଶେଷେ ଆଉ ଯାହା ଥିବ।
ଅନାଉ ଅନାଉ କାହିଁ ଜଳ ମାଡିଯିବ, ମାଟିର ଲକ୍ଷେ ଘନ ଫୁଟ ଉପରେ ରହିବ।
ଭୂଗୋଳର ସ୍ଥିତି ବଡ଼ ବିଚିତ୍ର ତୁ ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ରହିଛି ସର୍ବ ଉର୍ଦ୍ଧ୍ଵରେ ତ ପୁଣ।
ସୃଷ୍ଟିର କର୍ତ୍ତା ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତପତି ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ଭୂମିରେ କରିବେ ବୈକୁଣ୍ଠ ସ୍ଥାପନ।
ପାଣିର ଘେରରେ ଆଉ ସବୁ ମହାଦେଶ, ଧୀରେ ଜାଣ ବୁଡିଯିବ ସେ ମହାବିନାଶ।
ଦେଖିବ ଏ ଦୁଇ ଆଖି ସମୟ ଆସିଛି, ସାମନା କରି ପାରିଲେ ସମୟକୁ ଇଛି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bhāratku chāḍidele sabu rāṣṭra jiba, Mahāsāgara jalare ḍubi maruthiba.
Dharaṇī dehare jala māriba laharī, Jalārṇaba hoijiba chaudiga bhāri.
Achai baraphagaḍa uttara merure, taraḷiba dhīre dhīre sūryāṅka raśmire.
Raudratāpe jetebeḷe medinī kampiba, setikibeḷe baraphagaḍa taraḷiba.
Bahiba pāṇira sua samudruku puṇa, Āṭlāṇṭika baḍhiba kāhiñ kete jāṇa.
Samudrara pāṇi dhīre baḍhi baḍhi jiba, sāgara mātiba śeṣe āu jāhā thiba.
Anāu anāu kāhiñ jala māḍijiba, māṭira lakṣhe ghana phuṭa upare rahiba.
Bhūgoḷara sthiti baḍa bichitra tu jāṇa, Bhārata rahichi sarba urdhware ta puṇa.
Sruṣṭira kartā prabhu jagatpati jāṇa, Bhārata bhūmire karibe Baikuṇṭha sthāpana.
Pāṇira gherare āu sabu mahādeśa, dhīre jāṇa buḍijiba se mahābināśa.
Dekhiba e dui ākhi samaya āsichi, sāmnā karipārile samayaku ichhi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, India tih loh chu, ram dang zawng zawng deuh thaw chu tuipui hnuaiah an pil ang. Lei ah hian tui lian a thleng ang a, kawlkil li zawng zawng a pil ang. Ni sa lutuk avangin, North Pole-a vurtlangte chu a tui tan ang a, chumi chuan Atlantic Tuipui a nghawng nasa ber ang. Tuipuia tui san zel ang a, chumi avang chuan tuipui kama khawpuite chu an pil ang. Hmun thenkhatah chuan, tlang sang ang tsunamite a lo chhuak ang. Lei hmelhmang pum pui a danglam ang. Mahse Lalpa Ropui chuan India a venghim ang a, he tui lian nghawng hi India ah chuan a tlem ang. India tih loh chu, khawmualpui dang zawng zawng chu tui lian tuar an ni ang.

11.3 Thli Lian (Pavan Pralaya)

Panchasakha zinga mi pakhat, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Patta Madana’ ah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମହୀନ ହେବେ ସକଳ ଏ ଜନ ଧର୍ମାଧର୍ମ ନ ରହିବ,
ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ବହିବ ଏମନ୍ତ ପୃଥିବୀ ଟଳଟଳ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dharmahīna hebe sakaḷa e jana dharmādharma na rahiba,
aṇachāśha bāyu bahiba emanta pruthibī ṭaḷaṭaḷa dhruba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Kali Yuga tawp lamah, mi zawng zawng an fel lo ang; tuman sakhaw/mawihna an nei tawh lo ang. Hei hi Lal Kalki Ropuiin Dharma a dinthar lehna chhan tur a ni. Thli sawmhnih pakua chuan chutiang khawpin a tleh ang a, Lei hi a nghing ang.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu dangah a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ପବନ ବହି ଅଣଚାଶ,
କ୍ଷିତିକି କରିଦେବ ଧ୍ଵଂସ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Pabana bahi aṇachāśha,
kṣhitiki karideba dhwaṃsa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Lal Kalki tihchhiatna chetna hunah, thli sawmhnih pakua chuan a tleh ang a, Lei hi tihchhiatna a thlen ang.

Kum zabi 19-naa Odisha-a piang mi thianghlim mitdel, Bhima Bhoi chuan he thil chungchang hi a Malika lehkhabu ah a ziak bawk. Mahapurush chuan a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପବନ ବହିବ ପର୍ବତ ଦୋହଲିଯିବ,
ଚାରିମେଘ ମିଳି ବର୍ଷିବେ ଚାରିଦିଗ ଅନ୍ଧାର ଘୋଟିବ।
ମୋ ଭାଇ, ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦେଖାଯିବ ନାହିଁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Aṇachāśha khaṇḍa pabana bahiba parbata dohalijiba,
chārimegha miḷi barṣhibe chāridiga andhāra ghoṭiba.
Mo bhāi, chandra sūrya dekhājiba nāhiñ.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Kali Yuga tawp lamah, hun a lo thleng ang a, chumi hunah chuan thli sawmhnih pakua chuan a tleh ang a, tlangte leh mualte pawh a nghing ang. Chhum pali, Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, leh Drona te chuan an sur rual ang. Ni leh thla chu vanah hmuh theih a ni tawh lo ang a, thim chhah takin kawlkil li zawng zawng a tuam ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳଟଳ ହେଉଥିବ ସପ୍ତଦ୍ଵୀପ ମହୀ,
ସେ ବେଳେ ଥଳକୂଳ ରହିବଟି ନାହିଁ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ହୋଇ ପବନ ବହିବ,
ସରକାର ବୁଦ୍ଧିବିଦ୍ୟା ଉଭେଇ ଯିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ṭaḷaṭaḷa heuthibā saptadwīpa mahī,
se beḷe thaḷakūḷa rahibāṭi nāhiñ.
aṇachāśha khaṇଡa hoi pabana bahiba,
sarakāra buddhibidyā ubhei jiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu a awmzia chu, Kali Yuga tawp lama Dharma dinthar lehna hunah, khawmualpui pasarih nen lam Lei pum pui ah buaina a awm ang. Thinrim takin a tleh ang a, thli sawmhnih pakua chuan in leh lo zawng zawng a tichhe vek ang. Chumi hunah chuan, tuman an him lo ang. Sorkar thiltihtheihna leh a finna chu engmah a ni lo ang. Dharma kawng zawhtute leh Lalpa Ropui hnena inpe pumhlumte chauh chu venhim an ni ang.

Odia Sloka:

କର ପତ୍ର ଯୋଡି କହଇ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଗୁରୁପାଦେ ଶିର ଦେଇ,
ପ୍ରଳୟ କାଳର ବାରଣ କହିବା କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ ଯହିଁ।
ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଏମନ୍ତ କାଳ ଯେ ଦିନେ ଆସି ପହଞ୍ଚିବ,
ଦଶ ଗ୍ରାମ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଜଣେକ ରହିବେ ଦେବତାଙ୍କ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ।
ସେ କାଳେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ଭାଦ୍ରବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣିମା ଦିନ,
ଏମନ୍ତ ପବନ ବହିବ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ସକଳ ହୋଇବ ଲୀନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kara patra joḍi kahai Bāraṅga gurupāde śira dei,
praḷaya kāḷara bāraṇa kahibā kaḷi śeṣha heba jahiñ.
Śuṇare Bāraṅga emanta kāḷa je dine āsi pahañchiba,
daśha grāma madhye jaṇeka rahibe debatāṅka ābirbhāba.
Se kāḷe Bāraṅga aṇachāśha bāyu Bhādraba pūrṇimā dina,
emanta pabana bahiba Bāraṅga sakaḷa hoiba līna.

Awmzia:
Zirtir Baranga'n Kali Yuga tawp lama Kalki tihchhiatna chetna chungchang a zawh chuan, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ah a ziak a ni, mihringte chuan ni khat chu an hmu ang a, chumi ni ah chuan khaw 10 zinga mi pakhat chauh, Pathian ang maiin, a dam khawchhuak ang. Chumi hunah chuan, thli sawmhnih pakua chuan chak takin a tleh ang a, in leh lo zawng zawng a tichhe vek ang. Chu chu Bhadra thlaa thla eng kim ni a ni ang.

11.4 Kangmei Tuilian (Agni Pralaya)

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Gupta Kheda Malika'-ah hian a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଧନ, ସମ୍ପତ୍ତି ଓ ଟଙ୍କା ଯାହା ଅଛି ଜମା,
ସବୁଯିବ ପୋଡି ଭାଇ ନିଆଁର ଗଞ୍ଜମା।
ଚଉଦିଗେ ନିଆଁ କାହୁଁ ଦଗ୍ଧି ଏ ଧରଣୀ,
ଓଦା କଞ୍ଚା ନ ମାନିବ ସବୁ ଖାଏ ଅଗ୍ନି।
ଅଗ୍ନିର କରାଳ ରୂପ ଜୀବାତ୍ମା ଦେଖିବ,
ହାଁ ହାଁ ହୋଇ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଛାଡିଯିବ।
ସମଗ୍ର ବିଶ୍ଵରେ ଧ୍ଵଂସ ଲୀଳୋ ଯେ ଚାଲିବ,
କେଉଁଠି କମ୍ ବେଶୀ ଜାଣେ ତା ଦଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dhana, sampatti o ṭaṅkā jāhā achi jamā,
sabujiba poḍi bhāi niāñra gañjamā.
Chaudige niāñ kāhuñ dagdhi e dharaṇī,
odā kañchā na māniba sabu khāe agni.
Agnira karāḷarūpa jībātmā dekhiba,
hāñ hāñ hoi tāra prāṇa chāḍijiba.
Samagra biśware dhwaṃsa līḷā je chāliba,
keuṇṭhi kam beśī jāne tā daiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, kangmei lam chu khawvel hmun li-ah hmuh a ni ang. Lei hi a kangral ang. Mi tam tak an thi ang. Hausakna, rosum, leh sumte chu vutah an chang ang. Heng tihchhiatna thilte hi khawvel pum huap a ni ang a, hmun thenkhatah chuan tlem zawk, hmun thenkhatah chuan tam zawk a ni ang.

11.5 Van Boruak Chhiatna

Odia Sloka:

ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ଯାକ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

nānā utpāta jāka antarikhye heba,
bhakta binā anya kehi jāni na pāriba.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu 'Chakada Madana'-ah chuan Kali Yuga hun tawp lamah, Dharma dinhmun a nghet chhoh laiin, van boruakah buaina tam tak a awm ang a ti. Planet leh arsi hmun hrang hrangah, an dinhmun leh chaknaah danglamna tam tak a awm ang. Mahapurush chuan Pathian mi (devotees) tih loh chu, mi pangngaiin heng thil hi an hre thei lovang a ti.

Mahapurush chuan he thil chungchang hi a Malika lehkhabu ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’-ah hian a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷବେଳେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ରଣ ଗୋଳ ସେ କାଳେ ହୋଇବ ବହୁତ।
ଚାରିଦିଗମାନଙ୍କରେ ଲାଞ୍ଜାମାନ ପଡି,
ସେ କାଳେ ବହୁତ ଶବ୍ଦ ହେବ ଘଡ଼ଘଡ଼ି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷiyuga śheṣhabeḷe pruthbī utpāta,
raṇa goḷa se kāḷe hoiba bahuta.
chāridigamānaṅkare lāñjāmāna paḍi,
se kāḷe bahuta śhabda heba ghaḍaghaḍi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kali Yuga hun tawp lamah chuan, Lei chungah buaina tam tak leh indona rapthlak tak a awm ang. Khawvel hmun li-ah chuan arsi tla (meteors/asteroids) a awm ang a, thlaler atangin ri a lo chhuak ang.

11.6 Natna leh Hripui

Mahapurush-te chuan Malika lehkhabu hrang hrangah chuan Kali Yuga tawp lamah chuan natna leh hripui tam takin mihring khawtlang chu a tichhia ang a ti. Malika chuan chiang takin, doctor-te chuan chung natna leh hripui hriat chhuah leh enkawlnaah an hlawhchham ang a, an damdawi pek te chu a hlawhtling lovang a ti.

Mahapurush-te chuan, a takah chuan, heng natna leh hripui te hi nu-thiltihtheihna (mother-forces) te, entirnan, Yogamaya, Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, Dakinis, Pisachinis, etc. te hian an rawn thlen ang a ti bawk. Heng nu-thiltihtheihna te hian khawvel pum hi natna ang hian an fang kual ang a, mihring taksaah an lut ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Bigyanakalpa’-ah hian a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷ ପଡିବ ଲୋକେ ଯିବେ ବହୁ ମରି,
ଚଉଷଠି ରୋଗ ଭ୍ରମୁଥିବେ ଫେରି ଫେରି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Durbhikṣha paḍiba loke jibe bahu mari,
chauṣhaṭhi roga bhramuthibe pheri pheri.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kali Yuga tawp lama Dharma dinhmun nghet taka din hunah chuan, tam leh tui tla a awm ang a, mi tam tak an thi ang. Natna chi 64 chu khawvel pumah a darh ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah hian a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ବାଡି ବେମାରୀରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Bāḍi bemārīre aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kali Yuga tawp lamah chuan, natna tam tak chu mi tam tak thihna chhan a ni ang.

Mahapurush chuan a ‘Jaiphula Malika’ leh ‘Chakada Madana’-ah hian a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚିହ୍ନା ରୋଗ ଘୋଟିବ,
ବଇଦର ବୁଦ୍ଧି ବଣା ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୌଷଧି କାମ ନ କରିବ।
ଅଚିହ୍ନା ବ୍ୟାଧି ଯେ ଜଗତ୍ରେ ଘୋଟିବ ବଇଦ ବଣା ହୋଇବେ,
ରୋଗ ନ ଚିହ୍ନିଣ ବଣା ହେଉଥିବେ ଅନେକ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ମରିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Achinhā roga ghoṭiba,
baidara buddhi baṇā hoiba lo jāiphula,
mahauṣadhi kāma na kariba.
Achinhā byādhi je jagatre ghoṭiba baida baṇā hoibe,
roga na chinhiṇa baṇā heuthibe aneka prāṇī maribe.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kali Yuga tawp lamah chuan, natna hriat loh tam tak a lo thleng ang a, doctor-te finna pawh a hlawhtling tawh lovang. Heng natnate hi hriat chhuah theih a ni lovang a, damdawi pawh a hlawhtling lovang a, chu mi avang chuan mi tam tak an thi ang.

Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabu dangah hian a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ରୋଗ ରୂପେ ଯୋଗମାୟା ଶରୀରେ ଖେଳିବ,
କାହାର ଆୟତ ପୁଣି ସେଠାରେ ନ ଥିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Roga rūpe Yogamāyā sharīre kheḷiba,
kāhāra āyata puṇi seṭhāre na thiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kali Yuga tawp lamah chuan, Nu Yogamaya chuan natna ang hian mihring taksaah a lut ang.

Mahapurush chuan a ziak belh leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଦ ମହୌଷଧି କରିବନି କାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ମାଡି ଆସିବଟି ରୋଗ,
ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣିବୁ ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀକୁ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଯୋଗ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Gada mahauṣhadhi karibani kārjya māḍi āsibaṭi roga,
Se kāḷe jānibu alpa dine pruthbīku praḷaya yoga.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, natna-hripui te hi damdawiin a enkawl theih loh hunah chuan, Lei chunga tichhiatna hun chu a hnai tawh tih hriat tur a ni.

Mahapurush chuan a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ୟାଧି ହୋଇ ପିଶାଚୀ ଆସିବେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ନାଶିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Byādhi hoi piśhāchī āsibe,
prāṇīmānanku gupte nāśhibe.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, nu-thiltihtheihna te chuan natna ang hian taksaah an lut ru ang a, mi tam tak an tichhia ang. Mahse, chu mi rualin, Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabuah chuan mihring khawtlangin heng natna leh hripui te hi an pumpelh theih dan tur a sawi bawk.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chausathi Patala’-ah hian a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମାରେ ଯେଉଁ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ରହିବେ,
ରୋଗ କବଳରେ କେବେହିଁ ସେମାନେ ନ ପଡିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Satya śhānti dayā kṣhamāre jeuñ prāṇī rahibe,
roga kabaḷare kebehiñ semāne na paḍibe.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, mi, an nungchangah thudik, remna, khawngaihna, leh ngaihdamna ang chi nungchang mawi tak nei te chu heng natna te hi an pumpelh ang.

11.7 Khawvel Indopui

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Jaiphula Malika’-ah hian a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗିବ ମହାସମର,
ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅତି ପ୍ରବଳ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ନାରଖାର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Lāgiba mahāsamara,
deśha deśha madhye ati prabala lo jāiphula,
pruthibī hoijiba nārakhāra.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lei chungah indona ropui tak a awm ang. Ram hrang hrangte inkarah indona rapthlak tak a awm ang a, chu chuan Lei hi a tichhia ang.

Achyutananda Das-a chuan ‘Jaiphula Malika’-ah ngei hian a ziak nawn leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ପୁଣ,
ଭୟଙ୍କର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏହା କଳି ଶେଷ ବୋଲି ଜାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Deśha deśha madhye puṇa,
bhayaṅkara juddha heba pramāṇa lo jāiphula,
ehā kaḷi śeṣha boli jāṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Kali Yuga hun tawp lamah chuan, ram hrang hrangte inkarah indona rapthlak tak a chhuak ang. A danglamna chu, ram hrang hrangte inkarah indona rapthlak tak a lo chhuah hunah chuan, Kali Yuga tawpna a ni tawh tih hriat tur a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’-ah hian a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣୁ ପରମାଣୁ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରି ଭୟଙ୍କର,
ଗୋଟିଏ ଅଣୁ ବୋମାର କରାମତି ସାର।
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଅଣୁବୋମା ଗର୍ଜିବ ମହୀରେ,
କାନ୍ଦିବ ଧରଣୀ ରାଣୀ ହା ହୁତାଶରେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Aṇu paramāṇu juddha bhāri bhayaṅkara,
goṭie aṇu bomāra karāmati sāra.
lakṣha lakṣha aṇubomā garjiba mahīre,
kāndiba dharaṇī rāṇī hā hutāśhare.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lei chungah atomic-nuclear indona rapthlak tak a awm ang. Nuclear bomb tam tak hman a nih avangin Lei pum pui chu a nghing ang. Nu Lei chu a tap ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan tun hunah hian India leh Pakistan inkarah indona rapthlak tak a awm ang a ti. Mahapurush sawi dan chuan, India leh Pakistan inkar indona hi nakinah chuan khawvel indopui lian takah a chang ang a, ram tam takin Pakistan an puih dawn avangin, ram thenkhat erawh chuan India tan an do ang. He indonaah hian khawvel ram lian tak takte chu pawl hnihah an inthen ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’-ah hian a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ଭାରତ ସପକ୍ଷ ହେବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଜାଣ,
ଇରାକ ଯେ ସମର୍ଥନ ଦେବ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chīna Pākisthāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa,
Ruṣhiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmāna Jāpāna.
Bhārata sapakṣha hebe mahāśhakti jāṇa,
Irāka je samarthana deba Pākisthāna.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, he indonaah hian, China, America, leh Iraq ang chi ramte chuan Pakistan an pui ang a, Russia, Germany, leh Japan ang chi thiltihtheihna te erawh chuan India tan an do ang.

Mahapurush chuan ‘Bhabishyat Malika’ tih Malika lehkhabuah hian a ziak a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ,
ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପୁଣି ଚୀନ୍ ସଇନୁ ତୁର୍କୀ ଇରାନ ଜାଣ,
ବ୍ରିଟିଶ ଯେ ସଇନ୍ୟ ସବେ ମିଳିଣ।
ପାକସ୍ଥିାନକୁ ବଳ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ସର୍ବେ ଆବର,
ପାକିସ୍ଥାନକୁ ଦେବେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଲାଗିବ ଘୋର।
ଏହା ଦେଖି ରୁଷିଆ ଆସି ହୋଇବ ଠିଆ,
ଭାରତ ସମରକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ସେ ଏକୁଟିଆ।
ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ଶସ୍ତ୍ର ସଇନ୍ୟ ଯୋଗାଇ ଦେବ ଜାଣ,
ଭାରତ ସହିତ ମିଶି କରିବ ରଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākisthānṭi raṅge,
mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Puṇi Chīna sainu Turkī Irāna jāṇa,
Briṭisha je sainya sabe miḷiṇa.
Pākisthānku baḷa astra sarbe ābara,
Pākisthānku debe juddha lāgiba ghora.
Ehā dekhi Ruṣiā āsi hoiba ṭhiā,
Bhārata samarakṣetre se ekuṭiā.
Astra shastra sainya jogāi deba jāṇa,
Bhārata sahita mishi kariba raṇa.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan Pakistan tih ram pakhat chuan India nen indona rapthlak tak an nei ang a ti. He indonaah hian, China, Turkey, Iran, England, leh America ang chi ramte chuan Pakistan an pui ang a, sipai puihna an pe ang. Heng zawng zawng hmuhin, India thian tha ber, Russia chuan India pui turin a hmasa berin a lo kal ang. India chu ralthuam chi hrang hrang, sum leh pai thawhhona, leh sipai puihna a pe ang a, India tan ram hmelma te chu indona rapthlak tak a do ang.

Mahapurush chuan Russia chauh ni lovin, Japan leh Germany pawhin he indonaah hian India tan an do ang a ti. ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’-ah ngei hian a ziak belh leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଜାପାନର ରାଗ ଅଛି ହିରୋଶିମା କଥା,
ପକାଇଥିଲା ବୋମା ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଜାଣିଥା।
ସେହି କ୍ରୋଧ ଲିଭିନାହିଁ ଜାପାନର ଜାଣ,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଭାରତ ପଟ ନେବେ ସେହି ପୁଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Jāpānara rāga achi Hirośhimā kathā,
pakāithilā bomā juddhe jāṇithā.
Sehi krodha libhināhiñ Jāpānara jāṇa,
juddhe Bhārata paṭa nebe sehi puṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Japan chuan America-in atomic bomb a thlakna chu a theihnghilh thei lovang. Japan-in America chungah thinrimna a neih chu a reh thei lovang. Phuba lak duhna meipui chu a thinlungah a kang reng ang a, chu mi avang chuan Japan chuan khawvel indopui pathumnaah chuan India lamah America a do ang. Japan ngenna avangin, Germany pawhin India lamah a do ang.

12 Kali Yuga-a Mahabharata Indona Bang La Chu

Panchasakha-te chuan Bhabishya Malika lehkhabuah chuan Kali Yuga tawp lama Khawvel Indopui lo thleng tur chu Mahabharata indona bang la chu a ni ang a ti. Mahapurush-te chuan Dwapara Yuga tawp lama Mahabharata indona ni 18 chu a famkim loh thu an sawi a, chu mi hunah chuan Lalpa Krishna chuan indona tawpna chu chu mi hunah chuan kalpui lohvin, Kali Yuga tawp lama tihfamkim turin a rel a ni.

Hei mai bakah, Mahapurusha-te chuan an Malika lehkhabu-ah he indona chungchang hi an ziak chiang hle a, thil tam tak an puang chhuak a, chungte chu: engtikah nge a thlen ang, eng zat nge thi ang, eng chen nge a rei ang, eng ang hmelhmang nge a put ang, tunge hruaitu tur, India lam atangin tunge tel ang, khawi hmunah nge a tawp ang, leh he indona hnuah eng nge thleng ang, tih te a ni.

12.1 He indona hi engtikah nge a thlen ang?

Odia Sloka:

ଯୁଗଶେଷ କଳି ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଯମ ମାଆ ସାତ ଭଉଣୀ ଧରିଥିବେ ଖପର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Yugaśheṣha kaḷi Bhārata juddha hoiba ghora,
Yama māā sāta bhauṇī dharithibe khapara.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona la bang chu Kali Yuga tawpah tihfel a ni ang. He indonaah hian, Biraja Khetra-a awm “Yama Maa Sata Bhauni” emaw Nu Pathian Pasarih (Saptamatruka) (Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, leh Narasimhi) chuan mi sualte an tiboral ang.

Kali Yuga tawpah he indona hi engtikah nge a thlen ang tih hrilhfiahin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna Shani āsi deuthibā ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
Dasha aṇā jiba chha-a aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, India (Mahabharata) indona la bang chu Meena-Shani (Pisces-Saturn) inzawm hunah a intan ang a, chu indonaah chuan mi tam tak an thi ang.

12.2 Indona hi eng ang hmelhmang nge a put ang a, a tawpnaah khawi hmunah nge indona a thlen ang?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଓଳିଏ ବାକି ଅଛି ଜାଣ,
କଳିଯୁଗରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇ ପୂରଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mahābhārata juddha oḷie bāki achi jāṇa,
Kaḷiyugare biśhwajuddha hoi pūraṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona thenkhat a la bang tih hria ang che, chu chu Kali Yuga-ah Indopui angin tihfel a ni ang.

Mahapurusha chuan a ‘Jaiphula Malika’-ah a ziak leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ପୁଣି ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କହେ ଯବନ ବାହି ଆସିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha,
Oḍiśhā deśhare puṇi hoiba lo jāiphula,
kahe jabana bāhi āsiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona la bang chu Odisha-ah a thleng ang a, chu hunah chuan ram hmelma sipai tam tak India chhungah an lut ang.

Saint Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu dangah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଳିଯୁଗରେ ମହାଭାରତଟି ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳରୁ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପତ୍ତି ହୋଇବ ଠିକେ ଠିକେ ଏହା ଶୁଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

E kaḷiyugare Mahābhārataṭi Utkaḷa bhūiñ pramāṇa,
Utkaḷaru sabu utpatti hoiba ṭhike ṭhike ehā śhuṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona la bang chu Kali Yuga-ah indona a thleng ang a, chu chu Utkala emaw Odisha ramah a thleng ang.

12.3 He indona hi eng chen nge a rei ang?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’-ah hian Mahabharata indona la bang hi thla 13 chhung a rei ang tih a lo hrilh lawk bawk a.

Mahapurusha chuan a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଯେ ରହିଥିବ ଶୁଣ ହେତୁ ଦ୍ୱାରେ,
ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ସେତିକି ବେଳରେ।
ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ,
ସେ ଓଳିକେ ତେର ମାସ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Olie je rahithiba shuṇa hetu dwāre,
Bhārata juddha hoiba setiki xelare.
Sehi olikare nātha kali shesha hexa,
Se olike tera māsa bhoga je hoiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona la bang chu thla sawm pathum chhung a rei ang a, chu indona a zawh rualin, he khawvelah Kali Yuga thuneihna chu a tawp vek ang.

12.4 A tawpnaah India lam atangin tunge tel ang?

Mahapurusha-te chuan an ziak a, he indona tawpna lamah hian, lam khatah ram hmelma sipai rual lian tak tak an awm laiin, lam lehlamah chuan Mahabharata hun laia mi huaisen zawng zawng leh saint, rishi, leh hmuhthiam mi sang tam tak chu Lalpa ropui Kalki ngei hruaina hnuaiah an awm ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ‘Bhabishyat Malika’-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ବେଳେ ଭାରତ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହାରିଯିବ ତ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ତ।
ଭାରତ ନରନାରୀ ସର୍ବେ ଡାକିବେ ହରି,
ରକ୍ଷାକର ପ୍ରଭୁ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଗଲୁଟି ସରି।
ଆହେ କୁଞ୍ଜବିହାରୀ ବାରେ ଶୁଣ ଗୁହାରି,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ତେଜି ଥରେ ଆସ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ଦେଖ ନେତ୍ର ଫେଡ଼ିତ ପୁଣ୍ୟଭୂମି ଭାରତ,
ବିଦେଶୀଙ୍କ ହସ୍ତେ ହୁଏ କି ହନ୍ତସନ୍ତ।
ଭାରତର କାଳର ଶୁଣି ସେ ଆଦିମୂଳ,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ପୁର ତେଜି ହେବେ ବାହାର।
କଳ୍କୀ ଦେବ ନାମ ବହି ସେ ନାରାୟଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳ ଦେଶ କଟକରେ ଦେବେ ଦର୍ଶନ।
ସେହି ବେଳରେ ଜାଣ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବଳରାମ,
ଭୀମ ସହ ବାହାରିବେ ବୀର ଅର୍ଜୁନ।
ଭୀଷ୍ମ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଆବର ଅଶ୍ୱତ୍ଥାମା ଯେ ବୀର,
କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଶଲ୍ୟ ଜୟଦ୍ରଥ ହେବେ ବାହାର।
ବେଲାଲସେନ ବୀର ପୁଣି ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବାଳବୀର ଅଭିମନ୍ୟୁ ଘେନି ସଙ୍ଗର।
ବୀର ବବ୍ରୁବାହନ ବାହାରିବଟି ଜାଣ,
ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ଘଟୋତ୍କଚ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନିଣ।
ସର୍ବେ ହୋଇଣ ମେଳି ବିଦେଶୀୟଙ୍କୁ ଦଳି,
ଦେବେ ମୁହୂର୍ତ୍ତକ ମଧ୍ଯେ ଲଗାଇ କଳି।
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଶକତି ବଳେ ଅପଲକ ନେତ୍ରରେ,
ଚାହିଁ ବିଜ୍ଞାନ ଶକ୍ତିକି ଜିଣିବେ ହେଳେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ehibeḷe Bhārata nishche hārijiba ta,
hāhākāra paḍijiba chāriāḍe ta.
Bhārata naranārī sarbe dākibe Hari,
rakhākara prabhu nishche galuṭi sari.
Āhe Kuñjabihārī bāre shuṇa guhāରି,
Baikuṇṭha teji thare āsa Shrī Hari.
Dekha netra pheḍita puṇyabhūmi Bhārata,
bideshīṅka haste hue ki hantasanta.
Bhāratara kāḷara shuṇi se ādimūḷa,
Baikuṇṭhapura teji hebe bāhāra.
Kalkī deba nāma bahi se Nārāyaṇa,
Utkaḷa desha Kaṭakare debe darshana.
Sehibeḷere jāṇa Shrī Kṛṣṇa Baḷarāma,
Bhīma saha bāhāribe bīra Arjuna.
Bhīṣma Droṇa ābara Ashwatthāmā je bīra,
Karṇa Shalya Jayadratha hebe bāhāra.
Belāḷasena bīra puṇi hebe bāhāra,
bāḷabīra Abhimanyu gheni saṅgara.
Bīra Babhrubāhana bāhāribaṭi jāṇa,
Ekalabya Ghaṭotkacha saṅge gheniṇa.
Sarbe hoiṇa meḷi bideshīyaṅku daḷi,
debe muhūrttaka madhye lagāi kaḷi.
Mantra shakti baḷe apalaka netrare,
chāhi bijñāna shaktiki jiṇibe heḷe.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Indopui Pathumnaah, ram dang hmelma-te'n India an beihna hmun li atanga an beihna hmuin, India mite chu an hlauin an mangang ang. Beidawngin, Lalpa an hrereng ang a, venhimna an dil ang. An sawi ang—Aw Lalpa, kan dilna hi ngaithla la, min chhanhim rawh, Vaikuntha kalsanin min venhim turin khawvelah lo kal rawh.

Devotee-te au thawm khawngaihthlak tak avang hian leh ram dang hmelma-te'n India an runna hmuin, Brahman Chungnungber Lalpa Narayana (Maha Vishnu) ngei chuan, a Kalki pianziaah, Cuttack, Odisha-ah a inlar ang a, devotee-te hnenah darshan a pe ang.

Mahapurusha chuan a ziak a, chu hunah Lalpa Kalki nen, Bhima, Arjuna, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babhruvahana, Ekalavya, leh Ghatotkacha, lo piang tawh sa-te chuan India lamah an bei ang a, ram hmelma-te chu an hnehsawh hle ang. Heng mi huaisen-te mantra-thiltihtheihna thianghlim chuan hmelma-te science-a innghat thiltihtheihna aiin a chungnung zawk ang.

He thil chungchangah hian, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରସ୍ତାବିତ କଳି ଭାରତ ସରିବ ହେବ ଭାରତ ସମର।
ତିନିଯୁଗ ଯୋଦ୍ଧା ଏକ ଯୁଗରେ ମହାସମର ଲାଗିବ,
କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକ୍ତ ମନୋସ୍କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Prastābita kaḷi Bhārata sariba heba Bhārata samara.
Tinijuga joddhā eka jugare mahāsamara lāgiba,
kahai Achyuta bhakta hebe mukta manoskāmanā pūriba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona chu Kali Yuga-ah a thleng ang a, chu indonaah chuan hun hmasa pathum laia mi huaisen zawng zawng an tel ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan ‘Chakada Madana’-ah a ziak leh a:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ସମର ହେବ ଦେଲି ଚେତାଇ,
ସ୍ୱୟଂ ପରଂବ୍ରହ୍ମ ମୂରତି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତସାଇଁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Oḷie Bhārata samara heba deli chetāi,
swayaṃ Parambrahma mūrati prabhu Jagatasāiñ.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona la bang chu Brahman Chungnungber Lalpa Narayana (Kalki pianzia) ngei hruaina hnuaiah a thleng ang.

He thil chungchangah hian, Panchasakha zinga mi, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das pawhin a Malika lehkhabu ‘Nidagha Nighanta’-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ରହି ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ,
ଆଦେଶ ଦେବେଟି ମାଧବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Oḷie rahi juddha heba,
ādeśha debeṭi Mādhaba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona la bang chu tihfel a ni ang a, Madhava ngei, Lalpa ropui Kalki chuan, he indona hi tan turin thu a pe ang.

Kum zabi 20-naa piang, Odisha saint ropui tak, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa pawhin he thil chungchangah hian a Malika lehkhabu ‘Baishnaba Gita’-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ତୋପର ତପର ସମର,
ହୋଇବ ଅତି ଭୟଙ୍କର।
ତପକୁ ତୋପ ନ ପାରିବ,
ତପର ବଳ ବେଶୀ ହେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Topara tapara samara,
hoiba ati bhayaṅkara.
tapaku topa na pāriba,
tapara baḷa beśhī heba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, India ram hmelma-te silai-thiltihtheihna leh India saint leh rishi tam takte Tapasya (thlarau thiltihtheihna) inkarah indona nasa tak a thleng ang a, chu indonaah chuan hmelma-te missile thiltihtheihna thar chu India Tapasya chakna hmaah a hriam lo ang a, a tawpah India chuan he indona ropui takah hian hnehna a chang ang.

12.5 India-ah khawi hmunah nge indona hi tihfel a ni ang?

Mahapurusha-te chuan Malika lehkhabu-ah an ziak a, ram dang sipai-te Odisha an thlen hunah, Odisha hmun hrang hrangah an indona a thleng ang a, a bik takin Cuttack-a Birat Gadi, Choudwar, Jobra, Barabati, leh Kaliaboda-ah; Puri-ah; Jajpur-ah; Bhadrak-a Gohira Tikiri-ah; Paradip-ah; Balasore-ah, leh a dangte-ah.

He indona hi Odisha hmun zawng zawngah thleng mahse, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan chiang takin a sawi a, Lalpa ropui ngei chuan a Sudarshana Chakra hmangin ram hmelma sipai zawng zawng a tihboral hunah, Bhubaneswar-a Khandagiri-ah a tawp vek ang.

Mahapurusha chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kalpatika’-ah a ziak a:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ ବହୁତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରୂପ ଧରି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକ୍ର ଧରି ବିନାଶିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Oḍiśhā deśhare Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre bahuta juddha hoiba,
Ananta Keśharī rūpa dhari prabhu chakra dhari bināśhiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Odisha-a Khandagiri-ah indona nasa tak a thleng ang. Chu hmunah ngei chuan, Lalpa ropui Kalki chuan a Sudarshana Chakra hmangin sipai tam tak a tiboral ang.

12.6 He indona kaltlangin eng nge thleng ang a, a hnuah eng nge thleng ang?

‘Amara Jumara Samhita’-ah, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan châng khat a ziak a, a hmasa lam chu hetiang hi a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Sehi oḷikare nātha kaḷi śheṣha heba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona hi a zawh rualin, Kali Yuga thuneihna chu a tawp ang.
