Chutah Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah châng khat a ziak leh a, a tlar hmasa ber chuan a sawi a:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଯେ ସରିବ ପୃଥିବୀ ଶାନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha je sariba pruthibī śhānta hoiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Mahabharata indona la bang a zawh rualin, khawvel hi a muang ang. Chutah khawvel buaina zawng zawng chu a tawp ang.

12.7 Khawvel Indopui-a America leh Europe ang ram chak tak takte dinhmun sawifiahna

Panchasakha Mahapurushate chuan an Malika lehkhabute-ah Khawvel Indopui Pathumna chu a nasa hle dawn a, ram tinin a nghawng vek dawn a ni, tiin an ziak. Tunlai khawvela ram chak ber bera ngaih ram zawng zawngte pawh hi Kali Yuga tawp indonain a tichhia vek dawn a ni.

He indonaa America dinhmun sawifiahin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଭାଇ ମୂଳୁ ଧ୍ୱଂସ ଯିବ,
ଗର୍ବରେ ଜଳୁଛି ରାଜା ବିଶ୍ୱ ମୁଖ୍ୟ ହେବ।
ଧରାକୁ ମଣୁଛି ସରା କହେ ମୁହିଁ ଶ୍ରେଷ୍ଠ,
ଉଡ଼ାଇବି ପୃଥିବୀକୁ କରେ ମୁଁ ଶପଥ।
କାହାରି ଗର୍ବ ସହି ନ ପାରିବେ ଧାତା,
ଜଳାଇ ଦେବେ ତାହାକୁ ଉଡ଼ାଇବେ ମଥା।
ନ୍ୟୁୟର୍କ ସହର ଧ୍ୱଂସ ପଦା ହୋଇଯିବ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଅଥର୍ବ ନିର୍ବେଦ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Āmerikā rāṣhṭra bhāi mūḷu dhwaṅsa jiba,
garbare jaḷuchi rājā biśhwa mukhya heba.
dharāku maṇuchi sarā kahe muiñ śhreṣhṭha,
uḍāibi pruthibīku kare muñ śhapatha.
kāhāri garba sahi na pāribe dhātā,
jaḷāi debe tāhāku uḍāibe mathā.
New York sahara dhwaṅsa padā hoijiba,
hāhākāra paḍijiba atharba nirbeda.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, America tia hming pu ram pakhat chuan khawvel hruaituah a inngai ang. Chapona leh uannain a khat ang a, amah chu tha berah a inngai ang a, indonaah ram dangte tihchhiat a vau ang. Mahapurush chuan Lalpa Ropui tak chuan tumah chapona a ngaithei lo a, chuvangin America chapona chu a tichhe vek ang, tiin a sawi bawk. America khawpui New York chu tihchhiat a ni ang a, chu ram chuan chhiatna rapthlak tak hmachhawnin a boral vek ang.

‘Gupta Kheda Malika’-ah ngei chuan, Saint Achyutananda Das chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ଇଉରୋପ ମହାଦେଶ ଜାଣ,
ସ୍ଥିତି ହରାଇବ ତାର ବିଷାକ୍ତ ପବନ।
ସବୁରି ଧ୍ୱଂସର କାରଣ ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ନିଶ୍ଚିହ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଗର୍ବି ଅଟେ ପୁଣ।
ନିଜର ଇଜ୍ଜତ ପୃଥିବୀ ପରେ ଦେଖାଇଲା,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧକୁ ତ ଜୀବନରେ ସିଦ୍ଧହସ୍ତ କଲା।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Āmerikā Iuropa mahādesha jāṇa,
Sthiti harāiba tāra biṣākta pabana.
Saburi dhwaṃsara kāraṇa Āmerikā jāṇa,
Nishchinha hoiba juddhe garbi aṭe puṇa.
Nijara ijjata pṛthwī pare dekhāilā,
Juddhaku ta jībanare siddhahasta kalā.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Khawvel Indopui Pathumna thlentu chu ‘America’ tia hming pu ram pakhat a ni ang. Chu ram chuan khawvel pumah zahna leh chapona a din ang a, indonaa thiamna sang tak a nei ang. Mahapurush chuan he chapona vang hian Khawvel Indopui Pathumnaah a hming leh hniak a bo vek ang, tiin a ziak bawk. Indona hnuah chuan chuta boruak chu a chhe ang a, chu vang chuan America leh Europe ramte-ah mi tam tak an thi bawk ang.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a ‘Bhabishyat Malika’-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଶେଷେ ଇଉରୋପ ଦେଶ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ହୋଇବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ,
ବଞ୍ଚି ରହିବେ କେବଳ ଭକ୍ତ ଅଂଶ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Śheṣhe Iuropa deśha juddhe hoiba dhwaṅsa,
bañchi rahibe kebaḷa bhakta aṅśha.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, he indonaah hian Europe ram chu tihchhiat vek a ni ang. Lalpa bhaktate tih loh chu, chu ramah chuan tumah an dam khawchhuak lovang.

‘Jaiphula Malika’-ah chuan, Saint Achyutananda Das chuan heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଦେଶ,
ହାଡ଼ ମାଳ ହୋଇଯିବ ବିଶେଷ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୀ ଦୁଲୁ ଦୁଲୁ ହେବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Paśhchima uttara deśha,
hāḍa māḷa hoijiba biśheṣha lo jāiphula,
mahī dulu dulu heba dhwaṅsa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Thlang leh Hmar lama awm ramte chu Khawvel Indopuiah tihchhiat an ni ang.

Achyutananda Das ji chuan ‘Jaiphula Malika’-ah ngei chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ଜର୍ମାନୀ ରୁଷିଆ ଆଦି,
ଇଟାଲୀ ଜାପାନ ତୁରସ୍କ ଗାଦି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସେହି ସରବେ ହୋଇବେ ରଦ୍ଦି।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Jarmānī Ruṣhiā ādi,
Itālī Jāpāna Turuska gādi lo jāiphula,
sehi sarabe hoibe raddi.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Germany, Russia, Italy, Japan, leh Turkey, etc., ramte chu Khawvel Indopuiah nasa taka tihchhiat an ni ang.

Kan lo sawi tawh angin, chung thil thleng zawng zawngte chu Meena-Shani yoga hun chhung, chu chu, kum 2025 leh 2028 inkarah a thleng ber ang.

March 29, 2025-ah, Saturn chu Pisces (Meena) zodiac-ah a lut a. Engtik lai emaw chuan retrograde (Vakri) emaw letlinga kalin, engtik lai emaw chuan direct (Margi) emaw ngil taka kalin, Saturn chu February 23, 2028 thleng Pisces zodiac-ah a awm ang. Chutih laiin, June 3, 2027 atanga October 20, 2027 thleng chuan Saturn chu Aries (Mesha) zodiac-ah hun reilote a awm ang.

Tunah zawhna pawimawh tak a lo chhuak a. Meena-Shani yoga a intan atanga thla 10 vel a ni tawh a, mahse chung chhiatna thleng turte chu a la intan lo. Engvang nge? Khawvel pumah chhiatna thleng a awm ngei a, ram hrang hrangte pawhin indona an nei bawk a, mahse Panchasakha-te'n Malika lehkhabute-a an hrilh lawk ang khawpa nasa chu a la thleng lo! Engtikah nge chung thil thlengte chu a nasa zual ang tih hi mi tam tak rilruah a lo lut fo thin!

Engtik atangin nge Chhiatna Ropui Tak chu a intan tak tak ang? Bhabishya Malika chuan he thil chungchangah hian engtin nge a sawi? Malika chuan chhiatna leela-te chu Meena-Shani hunah a thleng ngei ang a ti a, mahse Malika chuan chung chhiatna leela-te intanna chu kum 2025 ni lovin, kum 2026 tawp lamah a ni ang, tiin a sawi.

Panchasakha-te zinga mi pakhat, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das chuan Meena-Shani hun chhungah khawvel pumah buaina la thleng ngai lo a lo thleng tur chungchang chu a Malika lehkhabute-ah a ziak a, an hun dik tak chungchang pawh a hrilh a ni. A Malika lehkhabu pakhat-ah chuan heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଅକାଳେ ମଡ଼କ ପଡ଼ିବ ସେହି ସମୟରେ ଜାଣ।
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପରେ କମ୍ପିବ ସକଳ ମେଦିନୀ,
କାଳ ଭୈରବୀ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଯେ ରଡ଼ି।
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ତୃତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଜଳ ପ୍ରଳୟ ହୋଇବ ଭଲେ ତୁହି ଜାଣ।
ହୁତୁ ହୁତୁ ହୋଇ ଜଳୁଥିବେ ହିଙ୍ଗୁଳା ମାତିବେ,
ବଡ଼ ବଡ଼ କୋଠା ଘର ଜାଳି ଯେ ଦେବେ।
ଚୋର ଟାଉଟର ସଂଖ୍ୟା ପ୍ରବଳ ବଢ଼ିବ,
ସେଥିପାଇଁ ଦେବୀଙ୍କର କୋପେ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ନ ଦେଖିଲା ନେତ୍ର ପୁଣି ସେ ସମୟେ ଦେଖିବ,
ତ୍ରାହି ତ୍ରାହି ବୋଲି ପୁଣି ଡାକ ଛାଡୁଥିବ।
ହରି ଆଶ୍ରା କର ତୁହି ନିସ୍ତାର ପାଇବୁ,
ଦାସ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ କହେ ବିହନ ହୋଇବୁ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Mīna śhani yogare je dwitīya charaṇa,
akāḷe maḍaka paḍiba sehi samayare jāṇa.
bhūmikampare kampiba sakaḷa medinī,
kāḷa bhairabī chāḍibe śhūnyare je raḍi.
mīna śhani yogare je tṛutīya charaṇa,
jaḷa pralaya hoiba bhale tuhi jāṇa.
hutu hutu hoi jaḷuthibe hiṅguḷā mātibe,
baḍa baḍa koṭhā ghara jāḷi je debe.
chora ṭāuṭara saṅkhyā prabala baḍhiba,
sethipāiñ debīṅkara kope nāśha jiba.
na dekhilā netra puṇi se samaye dekhiba,
trāhi trāhi boli puṇi ḍāka chāḍuthiba.
Hari āśhrā kara tuhi nistāra pāibu,
dāsa Yaśhobanta kahe bihana hoibu.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Meena-Shani yoga kum hnih leh a chanve chhungin, phase hnihna (kum 2026-2027) leh phase thumna (kum 2027-2028) chhungin, thihna hun lo tam tak a thleng ang a, khawi hmunah pawh buaina a awm ang. Lirnghing nasa tak a thleng ang a, khawvel pum a nghing ang. Bhairavi au thawm a awm ang, chu chu, Nu Bhairavi chuan boruak atangin au thawm rapthlak tak a chhuah thut ang. Tui lian rapthlak tak chu khawvelah hmuh tur a awm ang. Nu Hingula thinurna vangin, in lian tak tak, banglo, etc., chu nasa takin a kang ang. Mahapurush chuan mi sual zawng zawngte chu Nu Pathian thinurna tuar an ni ang, tiin a ziak. Chutih hunah chuan, mitin chuan an mitin a la hmuh ngai loh zawng zawng chu an hmu ang, chu chu, engkim chu la thleng ngai loh danin a thleng ang. Mi zawng zawngin khawngaihna an dil ang. Lalpa Shri Hari ke bulah intulut a, a hnuai belte chu, Mahapurush chuan chung chauh chu heng zawng zawng atanga chhandamna hmuin, Satya Yuga chi an ni ang, tiin a sawi.

A chunga chângah khan, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das chuan chung chhiatna leela-te chu Meena-Shani yoga phase hnihna, chu chu, kum 2026-2027 atangin a intan tak tak ang, tiin a sawi.

He Kali Yuga hun chhung hian, Mahapurushate tam tak chu Odisha-ah hun hrang hrangah an piang a. Kum zabi 20-na chhungin, Odisha-a piang Mahapurush pakhat chuan Lalpa Ropui tak, Nu Yogamaya, leh pathian leh pathiannu-te'n bhaktate tan an hrilh lawkna chu a puang chhuak a ni. Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଆଦେଶ ଦେବୀଙ୍କୁ ହେବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Binśha chabisha sāla sariba,
Śhrī Hari ādeśha debīṅku heba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, kum 2026 tawp lam a lo thlen hunah emaw kum 2026 a tawp hunah emaw chuan, Lalpa Kalki chuan thiltihtheihna thianghlim zawng zawngte (Nu Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, crore kua Katyayanis) chu tihchhiatna intan turin thu a pe ang.

Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ସମୟ ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ ଯେବେ,
ସବୁ ଦେବା ଦେବୀ ବାହାରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Samaya binśha chabisha sāla sariba jebe,
sabu debā debī bāhāre biśhwajuddha karibe.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, kum 2026 hnuah chuan, pathian leh pathiannu-te zawng zawngin Deva-Asura indona tur au thawm an chhuah ang. Khawvelah indona a intan ang.

Hriat reng tlak chu, a chunga châng pahnihah khan, Mahapurush chuan “Kum 2026” tiin chiang takin a ziak a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଛଅ ସାତ ମିଶାଇଣ ତେର ଯୋଗ କର,
ଏହିଠାରୁ ଘୋର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରତ ବକ୍ଷର।
ସେ ବେଳେ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟଟି ହେବ ଜାଣ,
ଛିନ୍ନଛତ୍ର ଏ ପୃଥିବୀ କହିଲି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chha-a sāta mishaiṇa tera yoga kara,
ehi thāru ghora juddha bhārata bakṣhara.
se beḷe khaṇḍa pralayaṭi heba jāṇa,
chhinnachhatra e pruthibī kahili pramāṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, paruk (6) leh pasarih (7) belh la, chu chu sawm pathum (13) belh leh rawh, chu chu, 6+7+13=26, chu mi atang chuan India-ah indona nasa tak a intan ang. Chutih hun hnuah chuan, tichhiatna tlem (Khanda Pralaya) a thleng ang a, khawvel chu a chhe tan ang.

Hriat reng tlak chu, hetah hian Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan, a chunga châng pahnih ang lovin, “Kum 2026” ti lovin, 26 chauh a ziak a ni. Hetah hian, 26 tih hian Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan kum 2026 lam a kawk a ni mai thei.

Kum zabi 20-naa piang Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ପରେ ତ କରିବୁ ବିଚାର,
ମନେ ନ କରିବୁ ଯଦି ଯିବୁ ଯମପୁର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Binśha chabisha pare ta karibu bichāra,
mane na karibu jadi jibu Yamapura.

Awmzia:
A chunga chângah khan, Nu Yogamaya chuan kum 2026 hnuah chuan mi zawng zawng rorel an ni ang, a ti. Ringlo mite, mi sualte, thil tisualtute, etc., zawng zawng chu tihchhiat an ni ang.

Chuvangin, Malika-a sawi Kalki chhiatna leela-te chu Meena-Shani yoga hun chhungah ngei a thleng ang a, mahse kum 2025-ah ni lovin, kum 2026 tawp lam atangin a intan ang tih hi chiang taka hriat tur a ni.

Heng chhiatna leela-te hi kum eng zat nge a awh ang tih chungchangah chuan, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Kaliyuga Malika’-ah heti hian a ziak:

Odia Sloka:

ଚବିଶ ଠାବରୁ ଅଣତିରିଶ ଯାଏ ପ୍ରମାଦ ଅଛି ଅପାର,
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେଟି ହୋ ଗରୁଡ଼ ମହାକଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଭିତରେଟି ଗରୁଡ଼ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସରିବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Chabiśha ṭhābaru aṇatiriśha jāe pramāda achi apāra,
aṇatiriśha aṅkareṭi ho Garuḍa mahā Kaḷkī abatāra.
aṇatiriśha bhitareṭi Garuḍa sabu utpāta sariba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, chhiatna leela-te chu number 24 atangin a intan ang a, number 29 thleng a awh ang. Chhiatna leela zawng zawng chu number 29-ah a tawp ang.

Hriat tur chu, a chunga chângah khan, Mahapurush chuan number 24 chungchang a sawi a, kum 2024 chungchang a sawi lo. Mahse Mahapurush chuan chung chhiatna leela-te chu number 24 atanga intanin number 29 thleng, chu chu, kum 6 chhung a awh ang tih hi chiang takin a sawi a ni. Mahapurush chuan eng kum atangin nge a intan ang a, eng kumah nge a tawp ang tih chu a sawi chiang lo, mahse number 24 atanga number 29 thleng, chu chu, kum 6 chhung a awh ang tih chu a sawi a ni.

A chunga chângte atanga heng chhiatna leela-te hi kum 2026 tawp lam atanga intan tur a ni tih kan hriat tawh avangin, ‘Kaliyuga Malika’-a number 24 hian kum 2026-2027 a kawk a ni tih tlangkawmna hi awlsam takin kan hmu thei tawh a ni. Chutiang zelin, he Malika lehkhabu series-a number 29 hian kum 2031-2032 lam a kawk a ni.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chuan, a Malika Pothi hmangin, heti hian a sawifiah leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହା ତ ହେଉଛି କାଳର ଭୟ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ତହିଁ,
ଏହା ତ କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳଟି ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳ କରୁଅଛନ୍ତି ହେତୁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଆଉ ବା ଏକତିରିଶ ସେ ତ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ।
ମହାମାରୀ ସେ ତ ଆସିବ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଆସ,
ତେତିଶି ମସିହା ସୁଦ୍ଧାଟି କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଠା ଠିକଣା ଯେ ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହି,
ଏଭଳି ସମୟ ଆସିବ ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କାହାରି ପତା ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ,
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନୀମାନେ ବାଟଟି ପାଇବେ ନାହିଁରେ ଜାଣ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Ehā ta heuchhi kāḷara bhaya Achyutī tahiñ,
ehā ta Kaḷkī kheḷaṭi dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kaḷkī kheḷa karuachhanti hetu pramāṇa,
āu bā ekatiriśa se ta heture jāṇa.
Mahāmārī se ta āsiba kahe Achyutī āsa,
Tetiriśhi masihā suddhāṭi kahe dīna Achyuta.
Ṭhā ṭhikaṇā je rahiba nāhiñ Achyutī kahi,
ebhaḷi samaya āsiba dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kāhāri patā rahiba nāhiñ heture jāṇa,
Baijñānīmāne bāṭaṭi pāibe nāhiñ re jāṇa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lalpa Kalki chuan A leela (A chhiatna leela-te) chu a ti ang. Heng chhiatna leela-te hi kum 2033 thleng a awh ang. Chutih hun chhungin, hri lian tam tak a lo thleng ang a, chu chuan mihring khawtlangah buaina a thlen ang. Khawi hmunah pawh buaina a awm hun a thleng ang. Mi zawng zawng chu dinhmun chhe takah an awm ang. Scientist-te hriatna pawh a hlawhtling lovang.

Nimahsela, Achyutananda Das ji chuan a ‘Kaliyuga Malika’-ah chuan chhiatna thil thleng pawimawh berte chu number 27-ah a tawp ang, tiin a sawi bawk.

Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak –

Odia Sloka:

ସତାଇଶ ଅଙ୍କେ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଦିଗରୁ ସମୁଦ୍ର ଆସିବ ମାଡ଼ି,
ଉତ୍ତରରୁ ଗଙ୍ଗା ଉଛୁଳି ପଡ଼ିଣ ତହିଁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଦେବ ଧାଡ଼ି।
ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଦିଗରୁ ଜଳ ସ୍ରୋତ ଏକ ଆସି ତହିଁ ଯିବ ମିଶି,
ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟର ସୃଜନା କରିବ ପାପୀ ତହିଁ ଯିବେ ଭାସି।
ପୁଣ୍ୟାତ୍ମାମାନଙ୍କୁ ସ୍ୱୟଂ ବଳଦେବ ଉଦ୍ଧାର କରି ରଖିବେ,
ଭାରତର ଗିରି ପର୍ବତ ପାହାଡ଼େ ନେଇ ଯତନେ ପାଳିବେ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Satāisha aṅke dakhina digaru samudra āsiba māḍi,
uttararu Gaṅgā uchhuḷi paḍiṇa tahiñ saṅge deba dhāḍi.
Pashchima digaru jala srota eka āsi tahiñ jiba mishi,
khaṇḍa praḷayara sṛujanā kariba pāpī tahiñ jibe bhāsi.
Puṇyātmāmānaṅku swayaṃ Baḷadeba uddhāra kari rakhibe,
Bhāratara giri parbata pāhāḍe nei jatane pāḷibe.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, number 27-ah chuan, Chhim lam atangin tuipui fawn a lo thleng ang a, Hmar lam atanga Ganges lui liam nen an intawng ang. Chutih rualin, Thlang lam atangin tsunami fawn nasa tak a lo thleng bawk ang a, buaina a siam ang. He tichhiatna tlem hian mi sual tam tak a tihlum ang. Mi thianghlim leh mi felte chu, Baladev ngeiin a chhanhim ang a, India tlang leh mualte-ah him takin a venhim ang.

Mahapurush sawi danin, hei hi number 27-a chhiatna lian tak tak lo thleng turte zinga chhiatna tawp ber a ni ang, chu chu, a chunga sawi chhutna ang chuan kum 2029-2030-ah a ni ang.

Tunah hian châng dangte hmangin he hunbi hi hriatthiam kan tum ang. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan ‘Bhabishyata Malika’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ସତୁରି ଯାଏ ଠୁଳ,
ଲାଗି ରହିବ ତୃତୀୟ ମହାସମର।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଚମକ ପଡିବ ସ୍ଵର୍ଗ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ପାତାଳ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Pāñcha barṣha kāḷa saturi jāe ṭhuḷa,
lāgi rahiba tṛutīya mahāsamara.
pāñcha barṣha kāḷa juddha hoiba ghora,
chamaka paḍiba swarga martya pātāḷa.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Indopui Pathumna chu kum nga a awh ang. Kum nga chhung indona nasa tak a awm ang; Van, Lei leh Hnuai Khawvelte chu an khur ang.

Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu dangah heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କହନ୍ତି ବାସବ ଶୁଣ,
ଚବିଶ ଠାରୁ ସତେଇଶ ଜାଣ।
ଏଥି ଭିତରେ ଯେ ହୋଇବ ଗୋଳ,
କାହାରି ନ ରହିବ ଥଳକୁଳ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Śhrī Hari kahanti Bāsaba śhuṇa,
chabiśha ṭhāru sataiśha jāṇa.
ethi bhitare je hoiba goḷa,
kāhāri na rahiba thaḷakuḷa.

Awmzia:
Châng chungahte hian, Lalpa Shri Krishna chuan Indra nen an inbiak laiin, number 24 atanga number 27 thleng indona nasa tak a awm ang a ti.

Châng chungahte hian, indona pui ber emaw Indopui Pathumna emaw chu kum 4-5 vel a awh ang tih a chiang. Chu chu, 2026-2027-ah tanin 2029-2030-ah a tawp ang.

Chuvangin, Mahapurush-in ‘Kaliyuga Malika’-ah number 27-naah emaw kum 2029-2030-ah emaw tichhiatna pui ber a sawi chu châng chungahte hian a inmil chiah a ni.

Sawihona chungahte atanga tlangkawmna tawi te siam a nih chuan:

Kum 2026 – 2027: Tichhiatna hun a intan ang.
Kum 2029 – 2030: Thil thalo leh chhiatna nasa berte chu an tawp ang.
Kum 2031 – 2032: Tichhiatna thil zawng zawngte chu an tawp ang.

Hriattirna: Kum 2029-a tawp tur tichhiatna thilpui berte chu sawi fo thinte an tel, chungte chu – lirnghing nasa takte, tui tichhiatna nasa takte, thlipui chhiatna, kangmei chhiatna, arvun tla, natna-hripuite, India leh ram hmelahte indona, Indopui, Kali-Mahabharata indona, Hirakud leh tuihna dangte tichhiatna, ni sarih leh zan sarih thimna, Bhairavi Dak, leh a dangte.

13 Satyayuga Intanna

Satyayuga-a luh tur chuan, mihringin Dharma kawng zawh a ngai a ni. Thutak a sawi fo tur a ni a, thutak chauh a zui tur a ni; hlimhlawp kawng ni lovin, remna kawng a zawh tur a ni. Thil nung tinrengte chungah hmangaihna, ngilneihna leh khawngaihna a nei tur a ni. A rilru, a tawngkam leh a chetzia hmangin thil nung engmah a tina tur a ni lo. Engvang mahin thil nung zawng zawngte tan duhsakna a nei tur a ni a, a chetzia chu midangte ngaihdamna a ni tur a ni, mi pangngai leh ngilnghet a ni tur a ni, tumah a huat tur a ni lo a, a taksa hriatna zawng zawngte chu a thunun thiam tur a ni. Pathian mi rinawm a ni tur a ni a, a rilru chu Pathianah a nghat fo tur a ni, hla saknaah hun a hmang tur a ni, Pathian hming lamnaah, thlarau lam inkhawm (Satsang)-ah a tel tur a ni, ni tin vawi thum tawngtai (Trisandhya) a nei tur a ni, Pathian thu a zui tur a ni a, ni tin Shrimad Bhagavat Mahapurana (Purana 18 zinga Mahapurana tia hriat) a chhiar tur a ni. Pathian leela chhiar emaw ngaithla thin mi a ni tur a ni. Fai leh Sattvic (chawhmeh) ei a ei tur a ni. Khawvel thil a tih pahin, Yoga, hming lamna, leh a dangte pawh a ti tel tur a ni. A nu leh pate leh upate a zah fo tur a ni a, naupangte a hmangaih tur a ni a, a rualpuite nen inngaihzawnna a siam tur a ni.

Kan zaa hian lo thleng tur tichhiatna thil, chhiatna hluar leh Indopui lakah tlan chhuakin, Lalpa Kalki khawngaihna hmuin Satyayuga-a luh kan duh theuh a ni. Mahse, Satyayuga-a luh chu a awl em ni? Awl lo. Satyayuga-a luh chu no-a tui chawhpawlh siam a, in ang mai a ni lo, chutianga in apiang chu Satyayuga-ah an lut ngei ang tihna a ni lo!

Satyayuga-a luh tur chuan, mihring tinrengin an nunah inthlak danglamna bulpui an siam a ngai a ni. Khawvel thil leh mawhphurhna a tih pahin, thlarau lam nun a tan tel a ngai a ni.

Lo thleng tur hun chu mi rinawmte tan engtin nge a awm ang tih hrilhfiahin, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das-a chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Chakada Madana’-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ହଟି ଯାଇ ସତ୍ୟର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଭକତମାନେ ରହିବେ ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଉଲ୍ଲାସ।
ବହୁ କଷ୍ଟ ପାଇଛନ୍ତି ଭକତ ମୋହର,
ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନରେ ଦୁଃଖ କଷ୍ଟ ଯିବ ଦୂର।
ଏ କଳି ହଟିବ ଯୁଗ ଅନନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରଣ ହୋଇବ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Kaḷiyuga haṭi jāi satyara prakāśha,
bhakatamāne rahibe atyanta ullāsa.
bahu kaṣhṭa pāichhanti bhakata mohara,
alpa dinare duḥkha kaṣhṭa jiba dūra.
e kaḷi haṭiba juga ananta hoiba,
dīna Achyutī kāmanā pūraṇa hoiba.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, mi rinawmte chuan Kaliyuga-ah nasa takin an tuar a, mahse rei lo teah Kaliyuga a tawp hunah leh chatuan hun (Ananta Yuga) eng a lo lan hunah chuan, mi rinawmte chu an hlim em em ang. Chutah chuan an lungngaihna leh natna zawng zawngte chu an bo vek ang.

Saint Bhima Bhoi chuan a Malika lehkhabu ‘Stuti Chintamani’ pakhat-ah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ନିବାରିବେ ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିବେ ପାଳିବେ ଜନ ପରଜା,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ ଅନାଦି ହୋଇବେ ରାଜା।
ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ ଭକତ ସକଳ ହରି ହରିବେ ଯେ ଦୁଃଖ,
ହରିବୋଲ ହୁଳହୁଳି ଶବଦରେ ପୂରିବ ଏହି ତ୍ରୈଲୋକ୍ୟ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Duṣṭa nibāribe santhaṅku rakhibe pāḷibe jana parajā,
Kaḷiyuga sheṣe satya upujiba Anādi hoibe rājā.
Haraṣa hoibe bhakata sakaḷa hari haribe je duḥkha,
Haribola huḷuhuḷi shabadare pūriba ehi traiḷokya.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, Lalpa Chungnungber chuan mi sualte a tichhe ang a, mi thianghlimte leh mi rinawmte a humhim ang a, Lei chunga mite a enkawl ang. Kaliyuga a tawp ang a, Satyayuga a lo thleng ang a, Lalpa Kalki ngei chu khawvel zawng zawng roreltu a ni ang. Lalpa chuan mi rinawm zawng zawngte lungngaihna a la bo ang a, an hlimna a vawng ang. Mi rinawmte chu an hlim ang a, khawvel pathumte chu 'Hari' hming lam riin a khat ang.

13.1 Satyayuga engtikah nge a intan ang?

Bhabishya Malika chuan Ananta Yuga emaw Adi Satyayuga emaw chu kum 2033-ah a intan ang a ti. Mahapurush chuan a Malika lehkhabuah heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ଡାହାଣରେ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ରଖ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ଯବନ ଗଣ ବିନାଶଟି ଧ୍ରୁବ,
ଦୁଇ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କରୁ ଆରମ୍ଭ।
ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ଅସତ୍ୟ ମାନ ହଟି ଯାଉଥିବ,
ଅଧର୍ମର ନାଶେ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରଭାବୀ ହୋଇବ।
ତେତିଶିରେ ଚାରି ପାଦ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମା ତହୁଁ ବିକଶିବ।
ସରଳ ସଜ୍ଜନ ସାଧୁ ସନ୍ଥ ଜନ ମେଳ,
ହେବା ପାଇଁ ଏ ଧରା ରେ ଆସିଗଲା ବେଳ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Dui ḍāhāṇare shūna eka tini rakha,
sampūrṇṇa satya prakāsha hoiba pratyakṣa.
Mlechha jabana gaṇa bināshaṭi dhruba,
dui shūna eka tini aṅkaru ārambha.
Anyāya asatya māna haṭi jāuthiba,
adharmara nāshe dharma prabhābhī hoiba.
Tetishire chāri pāda dharma prakāshiba,
satya shānti dayā kṣamā tahuñ bikaśhiba.
Saraḷa sajjana sādhu santha jana meḷa,
hebā pāiñ e dharā re āsigalā beḷa.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan kum 2013 atangin, Kaliyuga tichhiatna leh Thutak eng chu tlem tlem a intan ang a ti. Chumi hun atang chuan, mi sualte leh mi sualna neihte tichhiatna a intan ang. Dawt, dik lohna leh fel lohna thiltihtheihna chu a tlahniam ang a, Dharma thiltihtheihna chu a pung chho ang. Mahapurush chuan hetianga kal zel chuan, kum 2033-ah chuan Dharma ban pali zawng zawngte eng chu a lo lang ang a, chu chu Dharma dinhmun famkim a lo thleng ang tihna a ni. Chumi hun atang chuan, Thutak, Remna, Ngilneihna leh Ngaihdamna mize tha takte chu famkim taka thang chho tan ang. Mi ropui, ngilnei leh mi thianghlim rilru pu, saint-te, sage-te chu an inpumkhat ang.

Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଗୁପତୁ ଗୁପତ, ତେତିଶି ଅଙ୍କେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ଭକତମାନେ ଯେ ଜାଣିବେ, ସମସ୍ତେ ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ।
ସଂସାର ଉଶ୍ଵାସ ହୋଇବ, କାଳର ଭୟ ଯେ ଛାଡିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ବସ୍ତ୍ରରେ ସୁଖୀ ହେବେ, ଧର୍ମ ପଥକୁ ଆଚୋରିବେ।
ଶସ୍ୟ ଯେ ବହୁତ ହୋଇବ, ଲକ୍ଷ୍ମୀ କୃପାରୁ ସବୁ ହେବ।
ସତ୍ୟରେ ସର୍ବେ ଆତଜାତ, ସତ୍ୟ କଳି ଯେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ପୁରାଣ ଭାଗବତ ମାନ, କରିବେ ଗାନ ସଂକୀର୍ତ୍ତନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶ ଯିବ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
କହନ୍ତି ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ କରତା, ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନଥିବେ ଏକ ଜାଣ, ଭକ୍ତ ଭଗବାନ ମିଳନ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Atyanta gupatu gupata, Tetishi aṅke heba khyāta.
Bhakatamāne je jāṇibe, samaste haraṣa hoibe.
Saṃsāra ushwāsa hoiba, kāḷara bhaya je chhāḍiba.
Anna bastrare sukhī hebe, dharma pathaku āchoribe.
Shasya je bahuta hoiba, Laxmī kṛpāru sabu heba.
Satyare sarbe ātajāta, satya kaḷi je heba khyāta.
Purāṇa Bhāgabata māna, karibe gāna saṅkīrttana.
Kaḷi kalmaṣa nāsha jiba, Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Kahanti Brahmāṇḍa karatā, ananta jugara byabasthā.
Mlechha nathibe eka jāṇa, bhakta Bhagavān milana.

Awmzia:
Mahapurush chuan hei hi thuruk nasa tak a ni a, number 33-naah (kum 2033) a thleng ang a ti. Mi rinawmte chauhvin hei hi an hria ang. Hei hi hriain an hlim em em ang. Khawvel chu sualna lakah a fihlim ang; tumah hlauhna emaw buaina emaw an nei tawh lo ang. Mi tin an hlim ang, mi tinin chaw leh silhfen an ngah ang, mi tinin Dharma kawng an zawh ang. Chutah chuan thar chhuah tha tak a awm ang a, hei hi Nu Lakshmi khawngaihna vang a ni ang. Mi tinin Thutak kawng an zawh ang; chatuan hun a lo thleng ang. Mi tinin Puranas leh Bhagavat an chhiar ang a, hla an sa ang a, hming an lam ang. Kaliyuga chu tihchhiat vek a ni ang; he thu hi a dik loh phah lo ang. Mahapurush chuan hei hi chatuan hun a ni ang a, hei hi chatuan hun (Adya Satyayuga) ruahmanna a ni ang a ti bawk. Lei chungah mi fel lo, mi sual, emaw mlechha pakhat mah an awm tawh lo ang. Mi rinawmte chauh an awm ang a, an zaa chuan Pathian nen an inpumkhat ang.

Kum zabi 20-naa piang Mahapurush pakhat (kan sawi tawh thin) chuan, Lalpa Chungnungber, Nu Yogamaya, leh pathian leh pathiannu-te thu a sawi chhuah pahin, heti hian a ziak a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତିନି ଚାରି ତିନି ଧାଡିରେ ତ ଜାଣ,
ଏହି ଦିନ ଠାରୁ ହେବ ସତ୍ୟର ନିୟମ।
ଗୁରୁବାର ଦିନ ଥିବ ଶୁଣୁଥାଅ ନର,
ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ସତ୍ୟ ଭୋଗ ତ ଆମର।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Tini tini chāri tini dhāḍire ta jāṇa,
ehi dina thāru heba satyara niyama.
Gurubāra dina thiba śhuṇuthāa nara,
sehi dinathāru satya bhoga ta amara.

Awmzia:
Hetah hian Mahapurush chuan “3” vawi li zawnin ziak turin a ti a. Chu chu 3333 a ni. Mahapurush chuan he ni atang hian, Satyayuga chu Lei chungah a intan ang a ti. Hetah hian 3333 tih hian, Mahapurush chuan – ni thumna, thla thumna, leh kum sawmthum pathum, chu chu March 3, 2033 a kawk a ni. Mahapurush chuan chu ni chu Ningani a ni ang a, Satyayuga chu chu ni atang ngeiin a intan ang a ti bawk.

Mahapurush chuan heti hian a ziak leh a ni –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତାରିଖ ତ ତିନି ମାସ ବିଂଶ ଶାଳ,
ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କ ତ ପରେ ସତ୍ୟ ସତ୍ୟ ବୋଲ।

Hawrawp Dangin Ziakna:

Tini tārikha ta tini māsa biṃśha śhāḷa,
tetishā aṅka ta pare satya satya bola.

Awmzia:
Chu chu, kum sawmhnihna number sawmthum pathumna thla thumna ni thumna hnuah, chu chu March 3, 2033 hnuah, Satyayuga a lo thleng ang.

Chuvangin, kum 2033-ah hun thar (Ananta Yuga emaw Adi Satyayuga) a intan ang tih rinhlelhna a awm tawh lo. Chu chu mi rinawm leh Pathian chauh hun a ni ang. A nih loh chuan, mi rinawmte chauhvin he hunah hian an lut thei ang. Lalpa chuan Lei hi Thutak, Dharma leh Dikna hmangin a humhim ang a, mi rinawm zawng zawngte chuan an nunah Thutak, Remna, Ngilneihna leh Ngaihdamna an hmang ang.

14 Tlangkawmna

Bhabishya Malika chu kum 600 vel kalta-a piang Panchasakhas-te leh kum zabi 17-na, 18-na, 19-na leh 20-naa piang Odia saint-te leh mi ropui dangte ziak lehkhabu tam tak hrang hrangte hlawmkhawm a ni. Malika lehkhabute chu Lalpa Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu Panchasakhas-te ziak a ni deuh ber. Heng Panchasakhas-te hi hun tinah hming hrang hrangin Lalpa thian hnai tak angin an lo piang thin a, Kaliyuga-ah pawh, kum 600 vel kaltaah khan, heng Panchasakhas-te hian Lalpa lo piang, Chaitanya Mahaprabhu hnaih an hmu a ni. Kaliyuga-a piang Panchasakhas-te hmingte chu – Achyutananda Das, Balaram Das, Jashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, leh Jagannath Das an ni.

Panchasakhas-te chu Lalpa lo pianna thenkhat (Anshavatar) angin an ngai a, kum 600 vel kaltaah khan, Lalpa thupek chauhvin Malika lehkhabute hi an ziak a ni. Malika lehkhabute ziah chhan ber chu mi rinawmte thatna tur a ni – Kaliyuga tawp hunah mi rinawmte rilru muhilte kaihthawh leh kawng kawhhmuha chhan chhuah an ni. Panchasakhas-te chuan an lehkhabuah hian mi rinawmte bak tumah hrilh lo turin chiang takin an ziak a ni. Panchasakhas-te chuan Malika thu hi Lalpa mi rinawmte lo ni tawh thin, pianna hmasa leh hun hmasa atanga lo awm tawhte chauhvin an pawm thei ang tih an hrilhfiah bawk.

Malika chu Mahaprabhu ngei aw a nih avangin leh Lalpa thupek chauhvin Panchasakhas-te ziak a nih avangin, Malika thu tinreng chu lung chunga ziak ang mai a ni a, a dik lo thei ngai lo ang a, tihdanglam theih a ni ngai lo ang. Tun hun atana Malika hrilh lawkna tam takte chu 100% dik a ni tih hi mak a ni lo. Entirnan – India zalenna hrilh lawkna, Gandhiji pianna hrilh lawkna, Pakistan hming pu ram dinna hrilh lawkna, Odisha-a Hirakud tuihna sakna hrilh lawkna, leh a dangte.

Bhabishya Malika chuan Kaliyuga tawpna, Kalki Avatar, mi sualte leh mi sualna neihte tichhiatna, mi rinawmte humhimna, Dharma dinna, Ananta Yuga lo thlenna, leh a dangte chu chipchiar takin a sawi. Malika chuan Kaliyuga hun, kum 4,32,000 awh tur chu, Kaliyuga-a sualna hrang hrang tih avangin kum 5,000 chauhvin a tlem ang a ti. Kum 5,000 a liam hnuah, Lalpa Jagannath chuan A Nilachala Dham, Puri a chhuahsan ang a, Odisha-a Biraja Kshetra, Jajpur-ah mihring taksaah a lo piang ang a, chumi hnuah Khandagiri, Bhubaneswar-ah a awm ang. Malika sawi danin, hei hi Pathian Kalki Avatar a ni ang.

Malika chuan Kaliyuga tawp hunah, mi rinawmte chuan Puri Jagannath temple leh India ram biak in leh hmun thianghlim dangte atangin chhinchhiahna tam tak an hmu ang a, chungte chu hmuin Kaliyuga a tawp tawh a, Lalpa Kalki a lo piang tawh tih an hrethiam ang. Entirnan, Jagannath temple atanga hnam puanzar pui ber thlawh bo, temple atanga lung tla nawn fo, Nilachakra a kun, temple chunga muvanlai thu fo, leh a dangte. Heng chhinchhiahna zawng zawngte hi mi rinawmte chuan kum hmasa lamah an hmu tawh tih hriat a ni.

Malika chuan Lalpa Kalki pianna chu kum 2005-2008 inkarah a kawk. Malika sawi danin, Kalki Avatar thiltum chu mi sualte tichhiat, mi rinawmte humhim, leh Lei chungah Dharma dinthar leh a ni. Malika chuan he Yuga-parivartana hi kum 2026-2027-ah Meena-Shani yoga hunah Lalpa Kalki-in a tan ang a, kum 2031-2032-ah a tawp vek ang a ti. Chumi hun chhungin, tichhiatna thilpui zawng zawngte chu kum 2029-ah a thleng ang.

Malika chuan Lalpa'n Lei chungah Dharma a din hunah, Puri Lal hming chu Shri Dibyasingha Deb a ni ang a ti bawk, chu chu Lalpa leela tichhiatna zawng zawngte chu Puri Lal Dibyasingha Deb rorel laiin a thleng ang tihna a ni. Malika sawi danin, heng leela tichhiatna hi Thil Pangngai (Panchabhuta) tichhiatna leh Indopui hmangin a thleng ber ang.

Malika chuan tichhiatna thil zawng zawngte kum 2031-2032-ah a tawp vek hnuah, Adi-Satyayuga emaw Anantayuga emaw chu kum 2033-ah a intan ang a ti.
